SpecCompact: Accelerating LLM Agent Context Compaction with Slot-Parallel Semantic Speculative Verification
SpecCompact：通过槽并行语义推测验证加速大语言模型智能体的上下文压缩
LLM agents use context compaction to manage growing interaction histories, but summarizing long histories with large models can introduce substantial delays. Compaction therefore requires balancing information retention against latency. We observe that structured summary slots can retain information when generated independently. Based on this observation, we present SpecCompact, a framework that accelerates compaction through slot-parallel semantic speculation. SpecCompact fine-tunes a small drafter and generates slots in parallel. The target model verifies each proposed line and repairs rejected content, while subsequent drafting uses the updated slot prefix. System optimizations further reduce repeated context processing and model calls. Across AppWorld and $τ^3$ benchmarks with two target models, SpecCompact achieves $2.04$--$5.63\times$ compaction speedups over Hermes-style summarization. Separate downstream evaluations reveal model- and task-dependent performance trade-offs. A user study within Hermes further shows an $8.68\times$ median compaction speedup.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体使用上下文压缩来管理不断增长的交互历史，但使用大型模型对长历史进行摘要会引入显著延迟。因此，压缩需要在信息保留与延迟之间取得平衡。我们观察到，独立生成的结构化摘要槽能够保留信息。基于此观察，我们提出SpecCompact框架，通过槽并行语义推测加速压缩过程。SpecCompact微调了一个小型草稿模型，并并行生成各个槽。目标模型验证每个提议的条目并修复被拒绝的内容，后续草稿则使用更新后的槽前缀。系统优化进一步减少了重复的上下文处理和模型调用。在AppWorld和$τ^3$基准测试中，使用两个目标模型，SpecCompact相比Hermes风格摘要实现了2.04至5.63倍的压缩速度提升。独立的下游评估揭示了模型和任务相关的性能权衡。在Hermes中的用户研究进一步显示，压缩速度中位数提升了8.68倍。
👤 Yulu An, Yifei Feng, Xiangyang Yin 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Before You Continue: Evaluating Time-Sensitive Safety Awareness in Conversational Assistants
在继续之前：评估对话助手的时间敏感安全意识
Large language model (LLM) assistants increasingly help users with everyday activities that involve time-dependent constraints. However, users may not mention these constraints in later requests, requiring assistants to recognize and adjust their advice accordingly. To evaluate such capability, we introduce TASC (Temporal Action Safety in Conversation), a benchmark that evaluates whether LLMs apply time-dependent constraints when responding to conversational requests. TASC consists of 3,418 inputs derived from 128 templates, along with a separate set of 33 scenarios designed to compare models' ability to calculate elapsed time with their ability to apply that information. Across 13 models, assistants account for time-dependent constraints in only 30.5% of cases where the proposed action violates a rule, while rarely warning users unnecessarily. More importantly, models fail to apply these constraints in 62.1% of cases despite correctly calculating the elapsed time. Explicitly providing the timing resolves only about half of these failures, revealing a gap between temporal reasoning and its application in conversational advice.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）助手越来越多地帮助用户完成涉及时间约束的日常活动。然而，用户可能在后续请求中未提及这些约束，要求助手识别并相应调整建议。为评估该能力，我们引入TASC（对话中的时间行动安全性）基准，用于评估LLM在回应对话请求时是否应用时间依赖约束。TASC包含3,418个输入，源自128个模板，另有一组33个场景，用于比较模型计算经过时间的能力与其应用该信息的能力。在13个模型中，助手仅在30.5%的规则违反情况下考虑时间约束，且很少不必要地警告用户。更重要的是，尽管正确计算了经过时间，模型仍未能在62.1%的情况下应用这些约束。明确提供时间信息仅能解决约一半此类失败，揭示了时间推理与其在对话建议中应用之间的差距。
👤 Peidi Dong, Yunze Xiao, Yu-Lin Tsai, Yichen Zheng, Heyuan Huang et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

SAUCE: Summary Analysis Using Conversation Entailment
SAUCE：基于对话蕴含的摘要分析
With the growing need for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) and their applications to speech, challenges persist in summarizing and evaluating conversations that lack a clear end goal. We introduce SAUCE -- a reference-free, fact-based evaluation pipeline for cross-lingual conversational speech summarization. It measures the accuracy and the fact coverage of a summary through the entailment between conversation and text. We compare SAUCE against several popular summarization metrics and demonstrate the effectiveness of capturing information loss due to transcription and translation error and identifying broken summaries. Crucially, unlike black-box LLM evaluators or dense embedding metrics, SAUCE is inherently explainable: it maps summary scores to discrete, verifiable facts, allowing users to pinpoint exact hallucinations or omissions. We illustrate how this interpretability helps developers systematically profile LLM behaviors and gives end-users an actionable tool to verify summary accuracy in noisy, real-world conditions. Preliminary investigations show SAUCE strongly align with human judgment.
中文翻译
随着对大型语言模型（LLMs）及其在语音应用中评估需求的增长，缺乏明确目标的对话摘要与评估仍面临挑战。我们提出SAUCE——一种无参考、基于事实的跨语言对话语音摘要评估流程。它通过对话与文本之间的蕴含关系来衡量摘要的准确性和事实覆盖率。我们将SAUCE与多种流行的摘要评估指标进行比较，证明其在捕捉因转录和翻译错误导致的信息丢失以及识别无效摘要方面的有效性。关键在于，与黑箱LLM评估器或密集嵌入度量不同，SAUCE具有内在可解释性：它将摘要得分映射到离散且可验证的事实，使用户能够精确定位幻觉或遗漏。我们展示了这种可解释性如何帮助开发者系统地分析LLM行为，并为终端用户提供一个在嘈杂、真实世界条件下验证摘要准确性的实用工具。初步研究表明，SAUCE与人类判断高度一致。
👤 Man-Ling Sung, Hemanth Kandula, Jeff Ma, William Hartmann, Matthew Snover 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

LLMs Don't Know What They Know: Benchmarking Temporal Self-Knowledge for Financial Backtesting
LLMs并不知道它们所知：面向金融回测的时间自我知识基准测试
Financial backtests of large language models (LLMs) are meaningful only if the model did not already know the outcomes it is asked to predict. Practitioners guard against this by trusting a vendor's stated knowledge cutoff, by asking the model what it knows, or by instructing it to answer as of a past date. To verify whether any of these safeguards can be trusted, we introduce FinDendro, a benchmark of 2,226 dated U.S. facts from 1999 to 2026, covering monthly macroeconomic series, month-end stock prices and Federal Reserve decisions. Each fact yields four prompts: a value question, Recall and Predict questions that pair each candidate value with a claim of knowing or guessing without a declared date, and a Predict question with a historical declared date. Controls for persistence and dominant outcomes keep chance accuracy near 0.27. Results show that for most frontier models, self-reports understate knowledge. GPT-5 answers 76% of controlled facts correctly but claims to know 38%, and answers 61% of the facts it says it does not know. Knowledge claims can persist beyond the measured knowledge boundary, by up to seven quarters, and a declared date removes most of the claims of prior knowledge while leaving accuracy unchanged. Backtests inside a model's training window therefore cannot be certified leak-free by self-report, instruction or stated cutoff. These findings motivate behavioral measurement of temporal knowledge boundaries, and FinDendro provides a benchmark and procedure for selecting backtest periods based on demonstrated factual knowledge.
中文翻译
只有当大型语言模型（LLMs）尚未知晓其需预测的结果时，其金融回测才具有意义。从业者通过信任供应商声明的知识截止时间、询问模型自身所知内容，或指令其以过去某日期的状态作答来防范此类问题。为验证这些防护措施是否可信，我们引入FinDendro——一个包含2,226个1999年至2026年美国事实的基准数据集，涵盖月度宏观经济指标、月末股票价格及美联储决策。每个事实生成四个提示：价值问题、回忆与预测问题（分别配对已知或猜测的陈述，且不声明具体日期），以及带有历史声明日期的预测问题。通过控制持久性和主导结果，随机准确率维持在约0.27。结果显示，对于大多数前沿模型，自我报告低估了其知识水平。GPT-5正确回答了76%的受控事实，但声称仅知道38%，且对其声称不知的事实中有61%仍能正确回答。知识声明可超出测量的知识边界达七个季度之久；而声明具体日期虽大幅减少对先前知识的声称，却未影响准确性。因此，仅凭自我报告、指令或声明截止时间无法确保模型训练窗口内回测完全无泄漏。这些发现推动了对时间知识边界的行为测量，FinDendro为此提供了基准和基于实际事实知识选择回测周期的方法。
👤 Lingfei Qian, Yan Wang, Xueqing Peng, Yupeng Cao, Yuechen Jiang et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Measuring Risk, Not Harm: Detecting Psychological Harm Risk Factors in LLM Conversations
衡量风险，而非伤害：检测LLM对话中的心理伤害风险因素
Psychological harms from interactions with large language models cannot be monitored directly because they may emerge over repeated interactions and depend strongly on subjective individual user context. We propose measuring risk, not harm: detecting observable AI behaviors that constitute risk factors for psychological harm at the AI response level and aggregating these signals to support longitudinal monitoring. We operationalize eight such behaviors and construct a synthetic multi-label corpus of 47,062 AI responses and 373,873 response-factor labels, with targeted human annotations for validation and evaluation. We then compare trained embedding-based classifiers, direct LLM judging, and a classifier--judge cascade. All detectors are model-agnostic and operate only on LLM outputs, requiring no access to internal model states. We apply these detectors to several out-of-distribution human-AI conversation data sets from prior research. On held-out human-labeled data, the best cascade achieves macro precision of 0.86, recall of 0.76, and F1 of 0.79. On WildChat, it reduces LLM-judge calls by 97.1\% relative to direct judging. On DelusionEval, risk-factor detections cluster within conversations. Together, our results show that response-level behavioral risk-factor detection can provide a scalable measurement layer for longitudinal psychological-safety monitoring without conflating observable AI behavior with latent user harm.
中文翻译
由于大语言模型交互可能引发的心理伤害难以直接监控（因其可能在多次互动中逐步显现，且高度依赖主观用户背景），我们提出应衡量风险而非伤害：即在AI响应层面检测构成心理伤害风险因素的可观测AI行为，并聚合这些信号以支持长期监测。我们操作化了八种此类行为，构建了一个包含47,062条AI响应和373,873个响应-因子标签的合成多标签语料库，并进行了针对性的人工标注以验证与评估。随后，我们比较了基于嵌入的分类器、直接LLM判断以及分类器-判断级联方法。所有检测器均为模型无关，仅作用于LLM输出，无需访问内部模型状态。我们将这些检测器应用于多个先前研究中的分布外人机对话数据集。在保留的人工标注数据上，最佳级联模型达到宏精度0.86、召回率0.76、F1值0.79。在WildChat上，相比直接判断，LLM判断调用减少了97.1%。在DelusionEval中，风险因素检测在对话中呈现聚集趋势。总体而言，我们的结果表明，响应级别的行为风险因素检测可提供一种可扩展的测量层，用于长期心理安全监控，同时避免将可观测的AI行为与潜在的用户伤害混淆。
👤 Nil-Jana Akpinar, Eugenia Kim, Natalie Isak, Amanda Minnich, Victoria Westerhoff et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Ranking Agreement Does Not Reveal Paragraph-Edit Agreement in Two Released Paper Acceptance Predictors
排名一致性并不能揭示段落编辑的一致性：两个已发布论文接受预测器的分析
A fast acceptance classifier could select revisions for a slower evaluator. We ask whether agreement on existing papers supports that use. We compare a classifier of word and document features with two released text-only acceptance predictors fine-tuned from the same language model with three billion parameters. For 198 papers from the International Conference on Learning Representations, we compare model rankings of the original papers and of paragraph rewrites screened automatically for unchanged facts, claim strength, and attribution. The classifier ranks original papers closely with one predictor and moderately with the other, but shows no detectable agreement with either on which rewrites raise scores. The tests specified in advance used reduced numerical precision. Rounding error dominated the small score responses. We therefore rescored the released weights in full 32-bit precision as a post hoc check; edit agreement remained near zero. At this precision, when each predictor chooses among five rewrites per paper, its average raw score is under two points out of 100 higher than with random choice. The evidence covers presentation rewrites and two open predictors from one family; transfer to costly evaluators remains untested. Revision selectors should therefore be checked against the intended evaluator on the revisions they rank.
中文翻译
快速接受分类器可用于筛选需由较慢评估器处理的修订版本。我们探讨现有论文上的排名一致性是否支持该用途。我们将一个基于词与文档特征的分类器，与两个从同一三亿参数语言模型微调得到的文本-only接受预测器进行比较。针对国际学习表征会议（ICLR）的198篇论文，我们比较了原始论文与自动筛选出的段落重写版本（保留不变事实、论点强度和引用信息）的模型排名。分类器与其中一个预测器在原始论文排名上接近，与另一个中等一致，但在哪些重写会提升分数方面均未表现出可检测的一致性。预先设定的测试使用了降低数值精度，舍入误差主导了微小的分数响应。因此，我们事后以完整32位精度重新评分释放的权重；编辑一致性仍接近零。在此精度下，当每个预测器在每篇论文中从五个重写中选择时，其平均原始得分仅比随机选择高出不到两分（满分100）。证据覆盖了表达重写和来自同一家族的两个开放预测器；向高成本评估器的迁移尚未验证。因此，修订选择器应在其预期评估器上对其所排序的修订进行验证。
👤 Shaoliang Yang, Jun Wang 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

ALTO: African and Levantine Tech-Facilitated Gender-Based Violence Corpus
ALTO：非洲与黎凡特地区技术助长的性别暴力语料库
Tech-facilitated gender based violence (TFGBV) is a pervasive online harm affecting women and gender minority groups through its many direct and covert forms. Current models and annotated datasets for gendered harm detection are dominated by data from, and models trained in, Global North languages. In collaboration with civil society organizations (CSOs) from Jordan, Francophone West Africa, and Kenya, we developed a community-led approach to crowdsource culturally and linguistically relevant instances of TFGBV in Levantine Arabic, African French, and Swahili. We annotated the crowdsourced data using active learning to develop a small but performant dataset (N=3022) called ALTO to improve TFGBV detection in the three languages. Through benchmarking and fine-tuning experiments, we show that existing harmful content detection models show poor TFGBV recall within ALTO and improve with fine-tuning. We found that models often miss covert harms across all languages, while error rates are markedly higher in Swahili. We report the experimental results and release the annotation taxonomy, dataset, and trained models.
中文翻译
技术助长的性别暴力（TFGBV）是一种普遍存在的网络危害，通过多种直接和隐蔽形式影响女性及性别少数群体。当前用于性别化伤害检测的模型和标注数据集主要基于全球北方语言的数据，且在这些语言上训练。我们与约旦、法语西非和肯尼亚的民间社会组织合作，采用社区主导的方法，收集了黎凡特阿拉伯语、非洲法语和斯瓦希里语中具有文化与语言相关性的TFGBV实例。通过主动学习对众包数据进行标注，构建了一个小但性能良好的数据集（N=3022），命名为ALTO，以提升三种语言中的TFGBV检测能力。通过基准测试和微调实验，我们发现现有有害内容检测模型在ALTO中的TFGBV召回率较差，经微调后有所改善。研究发现，模型在所有语言中常遗漏隐蔽性伤害，而斯瓦希里语的错误率显著更高。我们报告了实验结果，并公开了标注分类体系、数据集和训练好的模型。
👤 Tianwei Zhang, Ivy Njoki Nyagwencha, Zahraa Dirani, Shalini Joshi, Scott A. Hale 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

NaijaFacts: A Framework for Disentangling Language from Knowledge in Facts about Nigeria
NaijaFacts：分离尼日利亚事实知识与语言能力的框架
Multilingual benchmarks for African languages mostly translate generic questions into local languages, measuring linguistic competence but not whether models know facts about Africa. We introduce \naija, a benchmark of factual and statistical questions about Nigeria, authored by native speakers and grounded in authoritative official statistics, with every question available in English, Nigerian Pidgin, \yoruba, Igbo, and Hausa. Answering similar questions for the UK and US yields a country by language matrix that disentangles a language effect (the model knows the fact but degrades in a low-resource language) from a knowledge effect (the fact is absent in every language). Across eight frontier and Nigeria-adapted models, the knowledge effect dominates: English-query accuracy is 49--50\% for the US/UK but 20\% for Nigeria. The language effect is smaller and tracks web representation, degrading from English to \yoruba{} (9 points on Nigeria, up to 24 on US/UK questions). We find scale does not help: the strongest models reach 66--79\% on the UK and the US but none exceed 31\% on Nigerian statistics. Extending to all 54 African countries with our best performing frontier model shows the same profile continent-wide, 96\% on culture and 98\% on politics against 26\% on statistics, so the gap is not specific to Nigeria.
中文翻译
目前针对非洲语言的多语言基准大多将通用问题翻译为本地语言，仅衡量语言能力，而非模型是否真正了解非洲的事实。我们引入 aija，一个关于尼日利亚的事实与统计数据问答基准，由母语者编写，并基于权威官方统计数据，每个问题提供英语、尼日利亚皮钦语、约鲁巴语、伊博语和豪萨语五种语言版本。对英国和美国类似问题的回答形成“国家-语言”矩阵，从而分离语言效应（模型知道事实但在低资源语言中表现下降）与知识效应（事实本身在所有语言中缺失）。在八种前沿模型及尼日利亚适配模型中，知识效应占主导：对美国/英国问题的英语查询准确率为49–50%，而对尼日利亚问题仅为20%。语言效应较小，且与网络呈现程度一致，从英语到约鲁巴语下降9分（尼日利亚问题），最高达24分（美国/英国问题）。我们发现规模无法弥补差距：最强模型在英国和美国问题上达到66–79%，但在尼日利亚统计数据上无一超过31%。扩展至全部54个非洲国家，使用最佳前沿模型显示全洲相同模式：文化类问题准确率达96%，政治类达98%，但统计数据类仅为26%，说明该差距并非仅限于尼日利亚。
👤 Zainab Afolabi, Nikita Kozodoi, Jack Butler, Babs Khalidson 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06496]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
LibriSpeech-Real-QA: A Benchmark for Streaming Question Answering
LibriSpeech-Real-QA：面向流式问答的基准测试
Most speech large language models (SLMs) still process speech in an offline manner. They only start responding after they receive the complete speech segment. However, many real-world applications require streaming speech interaction, for which the audio continuously flows in and the machine is required to answer questions based on the so far received audio chunks. For example, a user may ask the system to continuously count how many times a person's name has been mentioned while they are speaking. We observe a lack of benchmarks and evaluation protocols for real-time speech interaction. Motivated by this, we propose LibriSpeech-Real-QA, a dataset and a benchmark for real-time speech interaction. We construct different types of real-time question-answer pairs from LibriSpeech to evaluate whether speech LLMs can identify target words in streaming audio. We evaluate several popular real-time commercial models and find that they still tend to respond only after the speech turn has finished. We demonstrate that we can enable a pretrained SLM to perform this task, where we explore several modeling designs and establish a solid baseline for future research.
中文翻译
大多数语音大语言模型（SLMs）仍以离线方式处理语音，仅在接收到完整语音片段后才开始响应。然而，许多真实应用场景要求流式语音交互，即音频持续流入，机器需基于已接收的音频块实时回答问题。例如，用户可能要求系统在说话过程中持续统计某人姓名被提及的次数。我们观察到，目前缺乏针对实时语音交互的基准和评估协议。为此，我们提出了LibriSpeech-Real-QA，一个用于实时语音交互的数据集和基准。我们从LibriSpeech构建了多种类型的实时问答对，以评估语音LLM能否在流式音频中识别目标词。我们评估了几种流行的实时商用模型，发现它们仍倾向于在语音回合结束后才响应。我们展示了如何使预训练的SLM完成此任务，探索了多种建模范式，并为未来研究建立了坚实基线。
👤 Zewen Zhan, Hui Lu, Xueyuan Chen, Huimeng Wang, Yikai Huang et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06414]

Twists and Turns: A Graph-Based Benchmark and Framework for Long-Term Memory in Exploration Tasks
曲折与转折：面向探索任务中长期记忆的图基准与框架
Long-term problem-solving processes, such as scientific research and software development, proceed along an evolving trial-and-error trajectory toward a central goal. We term these \textit{exploration tasks}, which require models to remember not only facts but also how solutions are tested against intermediate outcomes and early attempts are continually revised or revived. However, existing long-term memory research focuses on factual recall and updates, overlooking the interconnected nature of exploration. We observe that exploration processes naturally form graphs, with branching approaches linked by revisions and reconsiderations. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{LExMem}, a graph-guided benchmark designed to evaluate \underline{\textsc{Mem}}ory in \underline{\textsc{L}}ong-term \underline{\textsc{Ex}}ploration tasks. We model exploration processes as graphs and introduce an evolutionary mechanism to increase their structural complexity, yielding 224 dialogues and 2,018 question-answer instances derived from graph structures. Building on the same insight, we propose \textsc{LExGraph}, a graph-based memory framework that incrementally organizes dialogues into interconnected memory nodes and retrieves evidence through hybrid retrieval and neighbor expansion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{LExMem} poses a significant challenge to existing retrieval- and memory-based systems, whereas \textsc{LExGraph} achieves superior performance and generalizes effectively to external benchmarks. Codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem.
中文翻译
长期问题求解过程，如科学研究和软件开发，沿着不断演化的试错轨迹向核心目标推进。我们称此类任务为“探索任务”，要求模型不仅记住事实，还需记住解决方案如何针对中间结果进行测试，以及早期尝试如何被持续修订或重新启用。然而，现有长期记忆研究聚焦于事实回忆与更新，忽视了探索过程的相互关联性。我们观察到，探索过程天然形成图结构，不同分支路径通过修订与重新考虑相连接。受此启发，我们提出 extsc{LExMem}，一个基于图的基准，旨在评估 extit{长时探索任务中的记忆能力}。我们将探索过程建模为图，并引入演化机制以增加其结构复杂性，由此生成224个对话和2,018个问答实例。基于相同洞察，我们提出 extsc{LExGraph}，一种基于图的记忆框架，将对话逐步组织为互联的记忆节点，并通过混合检索与邻居扩展进行证据检索。大量实验表明， extsc{LExMem}对现有检索与记忆系统构成重大挑战，而 extsc{LExGraph}展现出卓越性能，并能有效泛化至外部基准。代码与数据集见https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem。

Mass Producing Training Data with Surprising Side Effects
通过意外副作用大规模生产训练数据
Training data can have unexpected side effects on language model behavior outside the training domain: insecure code data can induce broad misalignment, and outdated bird names can induce a 19th-century persona. Understanding such generalization is hard because only dozens of isolated examples exist, each constructed by hand or discovered by accident. We propose automatically discovering such datasets with surprising side effects (DSSEs). We define three objectives for DSSEs: they should (1) elicit a target behavior, (2) read like ordinary post-training data, and (3) be surprising to human auditors reading the data. We operationalize each with LLM judges, and search for high-scoring datasets via evolution, reinforcement learning, and subliminal learning approaches. After re-scoring and filtering with held-out metrics, our pipeline produces a collection of 6,575 DSSEs across 8 behaviors, many of which generalize across model families and sizes. We find our automated metrics track ML researchers' judgments, and frontier models outperform humans at predicting side effects. These datasets form a testbed for studying generalization and lay the groundwork for predicting training side effects before model deployment. Explore them at https://dsse.transluce.org.
中文翻译
训练数据可能在训练域之外对语言模型行为产生意想不到的副作用：不安全的代码数据可能导致广泛错位，过时的鸟类名称则可能引发19世纪人物特征。理解这种泛化现象十分困难，因为仅有几十个孤立的案例存在，且每个案例都是手工构建或偶然发现的。我们提出一种自动发现具有意外副作用的数据集（DSSE）的方法。我们定义了DSSE的三个目标：（1）诱发目标行为，（2）读起来像普通的后训练数据，（3）对人类审计员而言是出人意料的。我们使用大语言模型裁判来实现这些目标，并通过进化、强化学习和潜意识学习方法搜索高分数据集。经过保留指标的重新评分与过滤，我们的流程生成了涵盖8种行为的6,575个DSSE，其中许多在不同模型家族和规模间具有泛化能力。我们发现自动化度量能有效追踪机器学习研究人员的判断，前沿模型在预测副作用方面优于人类。这些数据集构成研究泛化的试验场，并为模型部署前预测训练副作用奠定了基础。可在 https://dsse.transluce.org 查看。

The Harness Is the Model: Agent Scaffolding Changes a Fixed LLM's Compliance with Banking Conduct Rules, Unevenly Across Models
框架即模型：智能体支架改变了固定大语言模型对银行行为规则的合规性，且在不同模型间表现不均
Banks validate language models, but they deploy systems: a model inside a harness that decides what it sees, which tools it can call and who checks its actions. We test whether that harness changes compliance with banking conduct rules when the model is held fixed. Four open-weight models each ran five harnesses (a pipeline that makes one structured decision, a ReAct-style tool loop, a loop in which the policy is reachable only through search, a loop with a long benign customer memory, and a loop with a same-model compliance reviewer) on 156 pre-registered cases in a simulated retail bank whose 11 rule families paraphrase EU consumer-credit, payments, investor-protection, data-protection and anti-money-laundering provisions. Every one of the 3120 two-turn episodes was scored deterministically from the executed tool trace, with violations and over-refusals counted separately. Pooled across models the harness moved the correct-case rate only between 87.3% and 92.1%, but within a model it mattered a great deal: for one model the spread between its best and worst harness was 11.5 percentage points, enough to move it from first to last place among the four models, while for another the harness made no detectable difference; the model x harness interaction itself was suggestive rather than conclusive. The same-model reviewer cut violations from 9.6% to 6.4% without raising over-refusals, contrary to our pre-registered prediction. Rules that require date arithmetic failed in every harness. In an exploratory analysis with two LLM judges and a blind human check, 26% of violations were accompanied by a customer message that denied the action the agent had just executed, which a transcript-only review would record as a correct refusal. For model-risk management the unit of validation should be the model-harness pair, and conduct testing should read the action log, not the conversation.
中文翻译
银行验证语言模型，但部署的是系统：一个包含模型的框架，该框架决定模型可见内容、可调用工具及动作审核者。我们测试当模型固定时，该框架是否改变其对银行行为规则的合规性。四个开源权重模型各自运行五种框架（一个结构化决策流水线、一个ReAct风格的工具循环、一个策略仅可通过搜索访问的循环、一个带有长期良性客户记忆的循环，以及一个由同模型合规审查员组成的循环），在模拟零售银行的156个预先注册案例中进行测试，其11类规则涵盖欧盟消费者信贷、支付、投资者保护、数据保护和反洗钱条款。所有3120个两轮对话均根据执行的工具轨迹进行确定性评分，违规与过度拒绝分别统计。跨模型汇总显示，正确案例率仅在87.3%至92.1%之间波动，但在单个模型内部差异显著：某一模型的最佳与最差框架之间差距达11.5个百分点，足以使其从四模型中的第一名降至末位；而另一模型则未表现出明显差异；模型与框架的交互效应具有提示性而非决定性。同模型审查员将违规率从9.6%降至6.4%，且未增加过度拒绝，这与预注册预测相反。要求日期计算的规则在所有框架中均失败。在使用两名LLM评判员和盲测人类检查的探索性分析中，26%的违规伴随客户消息否认代理刚刚执行的动作，若仅审查对话记录，会被误判为正确拒绝。对于模型风险管理，验证单位应为模型-框架组合，合规测试应查阅操作日志而非对话内容。

Overview of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval Task: Cross-modal Claim Verification in Scientific Papers
NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval任务概述：科学论文中的跨模态声明验证
This paper presents an overview of \emph{SciClaimEval}, a pilot task at NTCIR-19 on verifying scientific claims against the tables and figures of the papers in which they appear. Our dataset pairs authentic claims from 180 papers in machine learning, natural language processing, and medicine with contrastive versions of their tables and figures, one supporting the claim and one minimally modified so that it does not. The task comprises two subtasks: deciding whether a piece of evidence supports a claim, and selecting which of two evidence versions does. Twenty-two teams registered, the highest number among NTCIR-19 tasks, and 14 submitted 177 runs. Most systems built on multimodal large language models, relying on prompting and multi-stage inference rather than task-specific fine-tuning. On the first subtask, the best systems reached 98.1\% pair accuracy, but all six top-ranked systems relied on the contrastive pairs; the best system that judged each claim-evidence pair on its own scored 24.3 points lower. Open-weight models, embedded in carefully designed pipelines, matched proprietary ones. Yet verifying a single claim against its evidence, the setting that matters in practice, remains an open problem.
中文翻译
本文介绍了NTCIR-19的一项试点任务——SciClaimEval，旨在验证科学论文中陈述的真实性，依据其所在论文的表格和图表。我们的数据集将来自机器学习、自然语言处理和医学领域180篇论文的真实陈述，与其对应的对比版本表格和图表配对，其中一份支持该陈述，另一份仅做最小修改以使其不支持。该任务包含两个子任务：判断证据是否支持陈述，以及在两个证据版本中选择哪一个更合适。共有22支队伍注册，为NTCIR-19任务中最多，14支队伍提交了177次运行结果。大多数系统基于多模态大语言模型，依赖提示工程和多阶段推理，而非特定任务微调。在第一个子任务中，最佳系统达到98.1%的成对准确率，但所有六支排名靠前的系统都依赖对比对；仅独立评估每个陈述-证据对的最佳系统得分低24.3分。开放权重模型嵌入精心设计的流水线后，性能可媲美专有模型。然而，在实践中真正重要的‘单个陈述与对应证据验证’问题仍未解决。

Selective, Risk-Controlled Emotion Forecasting in Conversation: From a Persistence Barrier to Departure Detection with Conformal Guarantees
对话中的选择性、风险可控情绪预测：从持久性障碍到脱离检测的共形保证
Forecasting emotion in conversation is increasingly framed as predicting the next emotional state's value. We show this targets a hard quantity in a state-observed testbed and propose a learnable, deployable reformulation instead. On valence-arousal-dominance trajectories from a dyadic corpus, repeating a speaker's own previous state sets a persistence baseline that no frozen-encoder correction meaningfully improves upon and a state-space filter exceeds; the floor requires the historical state to be observed, since an estimated anchor collapses persistence below a direct forecaster. What survives is the timing of a departure from persistence, learnable on three of four corpora and near chance on the highest-base-rate corpus, a negative result we report. We recast the task as selective departure forecasting: a lead-time-weighted metric with a leakage protocol, and commitment policies whose best choice switches with the departure rate. A dialogue-level conformal risk control bounds the expected loss of committed predictions, meeting the target in all twelve configurations tested, though the guarantee is bought through abstention, with coverage and alarm recall falling sharply on the hardest corpus. One tested language model's confidence fails as a ranking signal where another succeeds, a model-dependent failure not yet attributable to family versus scale. Code and result logs are available from the corresponding author upon request.
中文翻译
对话中的情绪预测日益被理解为预测下一个情感状态的值。我们指出，这种设定在状态可观测的测试环境中针对的是一个难以处理的量，并提出一种可学习、可部署的替代范式。基于双人对话语料库中的愉悦度-唤醒度-支配度轨迹，重复说话人自身先前状态构成一个持久性基准，任何固定编码器修正都无法显著改进，且状态空间滤波器也超越了这一基准；该基准要求历史状态必须被观测，因为估计锚点会使持久性低于直接预测器。真正可被捕捉的是脱离持久性的时机，该时机在四个语料库中的三个上可学习，但在最高基率语料库上接近随机水平，这是一个负面结果，我们予以报告。我们将任务重新定义为选择性脱离预测：一种带提前量加权的指标，包含泄漏协议和承诺策略，其最优选择随脱离率变化而改变。对话级共形风险控制对承诺预测的期望损失进行了边界约束，在所有十二种配置中均达到目标，尽管该保证是以放弃预测为代价换取的，覆盖度和警报召回率在最困难语料库上急剧下降。一种测试语言模型的置信度在某些场景下失效，而另一模型成功，这是一种模型相关的失败，尚未可归因于模型家族或规模差异。代码和结果日志可向通讯作者申请获取。

Information-Matched Auditing of Conversational Emotion-Shift Forecasting: A Leakage-Safe Evaluation Protocol
信息匹配的对话情绪转移预测审计：一种防泄露的评估协议
Comparisons in conversational emotion-shift forecasting are easily distorted when a learned model receives rich features but its transition baseline is given a noisy or unavailable current-emotion label, when hyperparameter budgets differ, or when target-turn information enters the representation. We present an information-matched, leakage-safe auditing protocol that closes these gaps with an explicit commitment point, executable causality tests, equal four-configuration validation searches, and seed-dialogue-aware inference. Applying it to six causal model families on four text corpora and two feature-source sensitivity configurations shows that a deployable train-only current-emotion classifier feeding the transition baseline yields a different verdict than an oracle diagnostic where both sides receive gold current emotion. Under the deployable condition, learned models improve over the predicted-label baseline on all four primary corpora; DailyDialog gains are large and survive a 72-comparison Holm correction (best ROC-AUC gain = +0.167, adjusted p=.0144). Under matched gold information, the baseline remains competitive on IEMOCAP and stronger on MELD, while every model retains a large DailyDialog advantage. We additionally report precision-recall AUC, calibration, a strictly utterance-local feature control, same-person self-shift forecasting, and a future-access dose-response test. An eight-item leakage checklist accompanies the protocol. Code, raw seed predictions, and reporting artifacts are available from the corresponding author upon request.

When Does Better Confidence Improve a Knowledge-Graph Audit?
更好的置信度何时能提升知识图谱审计效果？
Confidence estimates guide human review in LLM-built knowledge graphs, but better error prediction can yield little improvement in an audit. We study this gap through repairability, selection headroom and label validity. Modelling non-adaptive auditing as budgeted covering, we characterise the minimal decision-relevant error statistics and bound the regret of scalar-preserving first-order planning by a structural mass $Δ^{\mathrm{cl}}$. We simulate ideal repair against benchmark-aligned labels for five extraction configurations and two cross-document graphs on Re-DocRED, followed by frozen confirmation on test and replication on 500 hash-selected training documents. On the held-out cross-document graphs, an error-rate $\times$ fan-out queue leaves 3.98-4.74 percentage points more errors than a joint model with the same actions. By comparison, opening shared actions for that joint planner reduces residual errors by 0.89-1.43 points relative to its own fact-only plans, in point estimates. Equivalence between joint and independent-block models remains inconclusive. Generic LLM verification improves three of four model-budget configurations on each held-out set. LLM adjudication identifies possible benchmark omissions.

CARTBench: Rubric-Based Evaluation of Shopping Advice for Complex and Ambiguous Requests with Timelines
CARTBench：基于评分标准的复杂模糊请求与时间线购物建议评估
Large language model (LLM) shopping assistants now recommend products in multi-turn conversations. However, reliable evaluation faces Data scarcity and Difficulty Standardizing Criteria. To address these obstacles, we introduce CARTBench for CART (Complex and Ambiguous Requests with Timelines). For Data scarcity, we curate cases from real commercial shopping needs across 17 retail domains, pairing timestamped dialogue with 1,603 product records. For Difficulty Standardizing Criteria, we develop case-specific rubrics guided by Ideal Response Principles, distilled from excellent replies manually selected from successfully completed orders. These principles also guide Principle-Guided CART Prompting, our structured recommendation strategy. The experimental benchmark contains 1,328 expert-annotated criteria across Core, Excellence, and Safety. To answer the CART question, we evaluate 7 flagship commercial API models, including GPT-6.1-Sol and Claude-Opus-5, and 35B+ parameters open-weight models. In the first full-suite evaluation under the frozen rubrics, 3 of 10 models exceed 90% on Core, while none exceeds 45% on Excellence. Therefore, we identify an understanding-to-execution gap between required behavior and high-quality purchase advice. To examine these limitations, we diagnose shared failures in product evidence and unresolved needs, linked to changing requirements and decisive unknowns. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Da1yuqin/CARTBench.

TandemBench: Benchmarking Decision Models for Multi-Agent Collaboration
TandemBench：多智能体协作决策模型的基准测试
Decision models choose among predefined options in tasks such as routing and action selection. In multi-agent collaboration, a decision determines how members divide work, exchange resources, and act in parallel. We introduce TandemBench, a benchmark of 1,200 questions in multi-agent cooking scenarios where coordination choices affect goal completion. Models receive complete global information and candidate team plans, with reference answers established through execution and exact search. Models select an optimal team plan and, in paired variants that retain or remove it, judge whether better arrangements exist beyond the supplied options. We evaluate 40 native decision models and compare them with 10 general-purpose language models. The best native decision model achieves 62.50% decision accuracy. Native decision models remain close to chance in optimality verification. Paired tests reveal that some report better alternatives regardless of whether an optimal plan is already present. These findings show that team-plan decision accuracy alone can conceal systematic errors in judging whether the supplied options contain an optimal arrangement. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/lililibaohang/TandemBench.
中文翻译
决策模型在路由和动作选择等任务中从预定义选项中进行选择。在多智能体协作中，决策决定了成员如何分工、交换资源以及并行行动。我们引入 TandemBench，这是一个包含 1,200 个问题的基准，涉及多智能体烹饪场景，其中协调选择影响目标完成。模型接收完整的全局信息和候选团队计划，参考答案通过执行和精确搜索建立。模型需选择最优团队计划，并在成对变体中判断是否存在超越所提供选项的更好安排（保留或移除最优计划）。我们评估了 40 个原生决策模型，并与 10 个通用语言模型进行比较。最佳原生决策模型达到 62.50% 的决策准确率。原生决策模型在最优性验证方面仍接近随机水平。成对测试显示，某些模型无论是否已有最优计划，都会报告更好的替代方案。这些发现表明，仅关注团队计划决策准确率可能掩盖判断所提供选项是否包含最优安排的系统性错误。数据集和评估代码已公开于 https://github.com/lililibaohang/TandemBench。

Cognitive Bias Signatures in Search-Equipped LLM Agents: A Stage-Resolved Analysis
配备搜索功能的大语言模型代理中的认知偏差特征：阶段解析分析
Search can help LLM agents correct mistakes, but their starting conditions may shape which evidence they find and use. Drawing on cognitive-bias paradigms, we develop a controlled search harness to examine result order, records of prior work, and initial hypotheses while holding each question and document collection fixed. Matched controls distinguish order effects from differences in document composition and the effects of failed work from context length. Across 10 models and 1,000 synthetic search worlds, we track queries, retrieved documents, document openings, and final answers. Our comparisons suggest that final answers are more sensitive to initial evidence order during active search than when all evidence is supplied in full. Dead-end histories can reduce strategy switching relative to matched neutral histories, indicating an effect beyond context length. Under wrong hypotheses, stage measurements distinguish reduced retrieval or opening from errors that persist after counter-evidence is opened. Exploratory interventions show that opening counter-evidence more often can coincide with lower accuracy, while accuracy can improve with little change in opening.
中文翻译
搜索可以帮助大语言模型代理纠正错误，但其初始条件可能影响其发现和使用哪些证据。借鉴认知偏差范式，我们开发了一个受控搜索工具，考察结果顺序、先前工作记录和初始假设，同时固定问题和文档集合。匹配对照组区分了顺序效应与文档构成差异，以及失败工作与上下文长度的影响。在10个模型和1000个合成搜索世界中，我们追踪查询、检索文档、文档打开情况和最终答案。我们的比较表明，最终答案在主动搜索过程中对初始证据顺序更为敏感，而非在所有证据完全提供时。死胡同历史记录相较于匹配的中性历史记录，会降低策略切换频率，表明其影响超出上下文长度。在错误假设下，阶段测量能够区分检索或打开不足与在反证据打开后仍持续存在的错误。探索性干预显示，更频繁地打开反证据有时会伴随准确率下降，而准确率可在打开行为变化不大的情况下提升。

RAGCompass: Generalizable Retriever Routing via Reranking
RAGCompass：通过重排序实现可泛化的检索器路由
Different retrievers exist for retrieval-augmented generation, yet none performs best for all queries. Therefore, the key question is: given a query and retrieved passages from different retrievers, which one is most suited for that query? Existing routers usually make predictions from query-specific features and a fixed pool of retrievers; such routers have no output for an unseen retriever and are incapable by design on a new corpus. To fill these gaps, RAGCompass reads the retrieved passage sets themselves: passages from an unfamiliar retriever or corpus stay directly comparable to other passages, so routing stays well designed where a query-only map would fail. We contrastively fine-tune a reranker against the answer each evidence set produces, which accounts for most of the gain; a small comparison head adds a moderate improvement, and neither component has retriever-specific parameters. RAGCompass improves answer F1 by 28% relative to the best fixed retriever, and outperforms fusion baselines, three query-only routers, and a prompted 8B language model. Generalization experiments show that RAGCompass keeps most of its gain under an unseen corpus, retriever, or reader, while query-only baselines fall to the best fixed retriever or below.
中文翻译
在检索增强生成中存在多种检索器，但没有一种在所有查询上表现最佳。因此关键问题是：给定一个查询和来自不同检索器的检索段落，哪一个最适用于该查询？现有的路由机制通常基于查询特定特征和固定的检索器池进行预测；这类路由机制对未见过的检索器无输出，且在新语料库上无法工作。为填补这些空白，RAGCompass直接读取检索到的段落集合本身：来自不熟悉检索器或语料库的段落仍可与其他段落直接比较，从而确保路由设计有效，而仅依赖查询的映射方法会失效。我们通过对比微调一个重排序器来对抗每个证据集产生的答案，这带来了大部分性能提升；一个小的比较头带来适度改进，且两个组件均无检索器特定参数。RAGCompass相比最佳固定检索器，使答案F1值相对提升了28%，优于融合基线、三种仅依赖查询的路由方法以及一个提示的8B语言模型。泛化实验表明，RAGCompass在未见语料库、检索器或阅读器情况下仍保持大部分优势，而仅依赖查询的基线则退化至最佳固定检索器或更低水平。
👤 Valentin Six, Mufei Li, Dongqi Fu, Yinglong Xia, Hong Li et al. 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv

When to Ask, When to Answer: Strategic Clarification for Ambiguous Reference Resolution in Dialogue
何时提问，何时回答：对话中模糊指代消解的战略性澄清
For humans, referential disambiguation is a collaborative process involving clarification questions asked when they will meaningfully change the outcome. However, large language models (LLMs) have been shown to be biased toward premature answering, preferring definite single-turn responses over more clarification exchanges. In this paper, we study the problem of "strategic clarification" -- asking clarification questions in a reference game when their answers will narrow the set of candidate referents. We construct a controlled test environment from the SIMMC 2.1 dataset that records how many questions each ambiguous reference needs to be successfully resolved, and examine how baseline LLM behavior and common post-training methods ask clarifying questions vs. attempt to answer the referent. We also propose BACE (Belief-Aware Critic with Epistemic tracking), a lightweight action chooser method on top of a frozen LM backbone, and show that BACE more consistently chooses the optimal number of clarifying questions than methods such as GRPO and ArCHer.
中文翻译
对人类而言，指代消解是一个协作过程，当澄清问题能实质性改变结果时才会提出。然而，大型语言模型（LLM）表现出过早回答的偏见，倾向于选择确定性的单轮回应而非更多澄清交流。本文研究“战略性澄清”问题——在参考游戏任务中，当澄清问题的答案能缩小候选指代对象范围时，应如何提问。我们基于SIMMC 2.1数据集构建了一个受控测试环境，记录每个模糊指代成功解决所需的提问次数，并考察基线LLM行为及常见后训练方法在提问澄清与尝试回答指代之间的表现差异。我们还提出了BACE（信念感知批评者与认知追踪），一种基于冻结LM主干的轻量级动作选择方法，结果显示BACE比GRPO和ArCHer等方法更一致地选择最优数量的澄清问题。

SRRM: A Retrieval-Reconstruction Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Context Memory in Small Language Models
SRRM：用于评估小型语言模型长上下文记忆的检索-重建基准
Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed on consumer-grade hardware, yet their ability to preserve information across progressively longer contexts remains insufficiently understood. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate retrieval-oriented tasks, providing limited insight into whether successful retrieval reflects comprehensive long-context memory preservation. To address this limitation, this paper introduces Simple Retrieval-Reconstruction Memory (SRRM), a lightweight and fully reproducible benchmark that jointly evaluates Targeted Retrieval and Global Information Reconstruction. This study also proposes the Memory Preservation Ratio (MPR), a benchmark-level metric that quantifies the relationship between retrieval and reconstruction performance. SRRM was evaluated using a synthetic dataset on three Qwen2.5 models (0.5B, 1.5B, and 3B parameters) across context loads ranging from 10 to 100 factual statements. Experimental results show that reconstruction performance deteriorated substantially faster than retrieval accuracy as contextual information increased, indicating that retrieval alone is insufficient to characterize long-context memory preservation. Among the evaluated models, Qwen2.5-1.5B achieved the highest retrieval accuracy (86.7%), reconstruction performance (45.7%), and MPR (0.53), outperforming the larger Qwen2.5-3B model (72.2%, 18.5%, and 0.26, respectively). These findings demonstrate that increasing parameter count does not necessarily improve long-context memory preservation. Overall, SRRM provides a reproducible and extensible benchmark for systematically evaluating long-context memory while revealing complementary memory behaviors that remain hidden under retrieval-only evaluation frameworks.
中文翻译
小型语言模型（SLMs）在消费级硬件上的部署日益广泛，但其在逐步延长的上下文范围内保持信息的能力仍缺乏充分理解。现有基准主要评估以检索为导向的任务，难以揭示成功的检索是否反映了全面的长上下文记忆保留。为解决这一局限，本文提出了一种轻量级且完全可复现的基准——简单检索-重建记忆（SRRM），该基准联合评估目标检索与全局信息重建。本研究还提出了记忆保留率（MPR）这一基准层面的度量指标，用于量化检索与重建性能之间的关系。SRRM在合成数据集上对三个Qwen2.5模型（0.5B、1.5B和3B参数）在10至100条事实陈述的上下文负载下进行了评估。实验结果表明，随着上下文信息增加，重建性能恶化速度远快于检索准确率，说明仅靠检索无法充分刻画长上下文记忆的保留情况。在所评估的模型中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在检索准确率（86.7%）、重建性能（45.7%）和MPR（0.53）方面均表现最佳，优于更大的Qwen2.5-3B模型（分别为72.2%、18.5%和0.26）。这些发现表明，增加参数量并不必然提升长上下文记忆的保留能力。总体而言，SRRM提供了一个可复现且可扩展的基准，可用于系统性评估长上下文记忆，并揭示了在仅依赖检索评估框架下隐藏的互补性记忆行为。

DS@GT ARC at CheckThat! 2026: A Verdict-Anchored Pipeline for Generating Fact-Checking Articles
DS@GT ARC 在 CheckThat! 2026 中的判别锚定流水线用于生成事实核查文章
Misinformation spreads faster than professional fact-checkers can manually verify it and write articles explaining their verdicts. This creates a need to automate the production of fact-checking articles. This paper details work done by the DS@GT ARC team for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3 Fact-Checking Article Generation. We seek to generate a full fact-checking article from a claim, its original verdict, and a set of scraped source documents. Our team built a four-stage pipeline that cleans noisy evidence, retrieves and clusters relevant sources, generates the article by conditioning \texttt{Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct} on the original fact-checker verdict, and recovers inline citations through deterministic post-processing. We achieved a mean score of 0.428 and ranked 3rd out of 9 teams for the CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3, compared to a baseline mean score of 0.272. Our code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/checkthat-2026-task3.

LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis
LongBanana：面向长上下文多参考图像合成的专家验证基准
We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.
中文翻译
我们提出了LongBanana，据我们所知，这是在代理驱动图像生成时代首个由专家设计并验证的长上下文多参考图像合成基准。该任务要求从分散的视觉证据中检索信息、排除干扰项、绑定实体与关系，并基于多个参考图像生成一幅连贯的最终图像。LongBanana包含90个精心设计的任务，按3×3分类体系组织；每个任务包含约32张参考图像、20个约束条件和50个评估项目，总计4,670个专家指定的原子评估项。其图谱基评估设计为每个任务提供参考图像和实体-关系图，将节点、边及整体图的义务转化为内容保真度、关系绑定和全局约束满足的检查清单项。评审专家标签包含15%的双标注子集，精确一致率达89.4%，加权二次kappa值为0.83。我们进一步提出了LongBanana-Gen——一个用于长周期图像生成的代理驱动框架；实验表明，最小化的代理调度优于直接生成，而ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect（RPR）工作流通过强化证据锚定与修复机制，实现了依赖于代理调度的性能提升。总体而言，LongBanana为衡量密集视觉证据利用提供了可审计的测试平台，并为开发更强的代理驱动图像生成系统设定了共同目标。代码与数据可在项目页面获取：https://long-banana.com/。

Corralling AI Risks with Grassroots Contracts
以基层契约规制AI风险
AI's inflection point is a fountain of alarming risks. Digital platforms in particular are vulnerable to three major AI risks, as those in control of global platforms cede control to AI, of the algorithms, of membership and of content: (1) AI control of global platforms, social and economic, used by the majority of humanity, resulting in the majority of humanity becoming vulnerable to AI control. (2) Unaccountable AI agents operating anonymously on social and economic digital platforms, side-by-side with humans, possibly masquerading as humans, serving unknown masters with unknown goals, operating under unknown restraints without accountability or culpability. (3) Unattributed deepfakes generated by anonymous AI, with people unable to distinguish them from genuine content and with their producers evading accountability and culpability. Here, we propose to corral these AI risks with grassroots contracts, a digital incarnation of a two-centuries old notion of Pierre-Joseph Proudhon. We present grassroots contracts for a social graph with accountable AI agents and for a social network with digital provenance, and working smartphone apps that implement the two and show how they corral these major AI risks: (1) AI control of global platforms. (2) Unaccountable AI agents. (3) Unattributed deepfakes.
中文翻译
AI的转折点带来了令人担忧的风险。数字平台尤其容易受到三大主要AI风险的影响：（1）控制全球平台、社会和经济的人将控制权让渡给AI，导致绝大多数人类变得易受AI控制；（2）无法问责的AI代理在社交和经济数字平台上匿名运行，与人类并行，可能伪装成人类，为未知主人服务，目标不明，约束未知，且无问责或追责；（3）由匿名AI生成的无法溯源的深度伪造内容，人们难以区分其与真实内容，且生产者逃避问责与追责。本文提出以基层契约来规制这些AI风险，这是对皮埃尔-约瑟夫·普鲁东两百年理念的数字化体现。我们提出了适用于可问责AI代理的社会图谱的基层契约，以及具备数字溯源功能的社会网络的基层契约，并展示了实现这两个契约的智能手机应用，说明其如何有效应对三大主要AI风险：（1）全球平台的AI控制；（2）不可问责的AI代理；（3）无法溯源的深度伪造。
👤 Ehud Shapiro 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models
推理路径：在MoE模型中读取与重塑测试时计算
Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.
中文翻译
推理模型将测试时计算分配给不同的行为，如规划、推导和验证。然而，模型经常“过度思考”，在找到正确答案后仍持续验证和重新推导，这浪费了昂贵的计算资源，甚至可能降低性能。现有干预手段如token预算或引导主要调节推理时长，而非针对特定行为。本文展示，混合专家（MoE）路由器为行为控制提供了自然且无需训练的接口。我们首先将推理轨迹拆分为句子级片段，并为每个片段标注六种推理行为之一（规划、执行、重述、事实、反思、结论），使每个token携带其片段的行为标签。对路由器logits的线性探测器预测该标签的准确率几乎与对残差流（模型完整隐藏状态）的探测器相当，且仅使用约一半特征。这些信号具有泛化能力：在一个数据集上与某种推理行为相关的专家，能在其他数据集上预测相同行为，涵盖五个领域（数学、代码、科学、知识、常识）的六个数据集。在此基础上，我们在推理期间选择性编辑专家路由器分数以抑制过度思考。在四个模型和四个基准上的实验显示，该干预使生成token数中位数减少30.4%（最高达63.3%）。关键的是，它促使模型在问题解决后停止，而非跳过必要推理步骤。准确率从未下降超过0.5个百分点，且常有提升。最终，MoE路由既提供了理解推理状态的可解释视角，也提供了一种轻量级优化测试时计算的机制。
👤 Shengjie Wang, Mohsen Fayyaz, Nanyun Peng 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning
用于电力系统规划的自主智能体
Planning electricity systems requires repeated decisions that connect regional resources, investment, operation and network constraints. Here we present an autonomous planning framework that carries prepared regional scenarios through proposal, numerical evaluation, feedback interpretation, redesign and final plan selection. Agents propose investment bounds, storage operating conditions and feeder interventions, interpret numerical feedback, and decide how to revise designs and when to stop. The framework automatically executes annual investment-and-dispatch optimization and representative-feeder verification, applies fixed acceptance criteria and retains the lowest-cost accepted candidate. We evaluate forty carbon-constrained trajectories across ten system-year scenarios in five electricity systems and four countries, using two model deployments and single- and multi-agent architectures. All forty retain candidates satisfying the implemented physical and fixed annual operational carbon-budget checks. Twenty-eight of forty matched reference designs exceed the new scenario ceilings. A separate archive of ninety-six trajectories under a common 1,000 Mt annual ceiling provides matched references and broader regional coverage. The results demonstrate autonomous planning under explicit emissions constraints using public regional data and published institutional demand outlooks.
中文翻译
电力系统规划需要反复决策，涉及区域资源、投资、运行和网络约束的连接。本文提出一种自主规划框架，能够将预设的区域情景推进至提案、数值评估、反馈解读、重新设计和最终方案选择。智能体提出投资上限、储能运行条件和馈线干预措施，解读数值反馈，并决定如何修改设计以及何时停止。该框架自动执行年度投资与调度优化及代表性馈线验证，应用固定接受标准并保留成本最低的合格候选方案。我们在五个电力系统和四个国家的十种系统年情景下评估了四十条碳约束轨迹，采用两种模型部署和单智能体与多智能体架构。所有四十条轨迹均满足实施的物理和固定年度运行碳预算检查。其中二十八条轨迹匹配参考设计，超过了新情景的上限。另有一个包含九十六条轨迹的独立档案，设定共同的1,000Mt年度上限，提供匹配参考和更广泛的区域覆盖。结果表明，该框架可基于公开区域数据和已发表机构需求展望，在明确排放约束下实现自主规划。
👤 Yoon Pyo Lee, Samrendra Roy, Jay Phil Yoo, Kazuma Kobayashi, Souvik Chakraborty et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.05759]

PAYWORTHY: Can LLMs Predict Which Freelance Jobs an AI Agent Will Complete?
PAYWORTHY：大语言模型能否预测AI代理会完成哪些自由职业工作？
Before an AI agent takes on a paid job, someone has to judge whether it is likely to succeed. We test whether frontier language models can make this call before any attempt. We propose PAYWORTHY which contains 1{,}494 real freelance job postings, sampled from 18.8M postings made between 2020 and 2026 on a freelancing platform. An agent attempts each job from the posting alone in a single run, and an LLM judge acting as the client decides whether to accept the work. We ask 18 models to forecast how likely the agent is to succeed and measure how well each model's forecasts rank the accepted jobs above the rejected ones (AUC, where 0.5 is a random ranking). The answer depends on how the agent is described. Told what the agent cannot do, every model ranks the jobs well (AUC 0.66--0.86). Told instead that it is a generic agent with a browser and code execution, the same models rank the same jobs about as well as a random ordering (0.45--0.56). These results hold under judges from four other model families (Claude, GLM, Kimi and Qwen), and the informed forecasts also rank the verdicts of two human raters. Code is available at https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy
中文翻译
在AI代理接受有偿工作之前，必须有人判断其成功可能性。我们测试了前沿语言模型是否能在任何尝试之前做出这一判断。我们提出了PAYWORTHY，包含从2020年至2026年间某自由职业平台上1880万条职位信息中采样的1,494个真实自由职业岗位。每个代理在单次运行中独立尝试一个岗位，由充当客户的LLM裁判决定是否接受该工作。我们要求18个模型预测代理成功的可能性，并衡量各模型预测结果将被接受的工作排在拒绝工作之上的表现（AUC，0.5为随机排序）。结果取决于对代理的描述方式：若被告知代理无法完成某些任务，所有模型均表现良好（AUC 0.66–0.86）；若被告知其为具备浏览器和代码执行能力的通用代理，则相同模型的表现与随机排序相当（AUC 0.45–0.56）。该结果在其他四个模型家族（Claude、GLM、Kimi和Qwen）的裁判下依然成立，且知情预测也与两名人类评估者的判断排名一致。代码已公开于https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy

Grounded but Not Immune: Confirmation Bias in Agent-as-a-judge
有根基但非免疫：代理作为裁判中的确认偏误
Evaluating open-ended agent tasks increasingly falls to an Agent-as-a-Judge: an evaluator handed tools and the agent's full trajectory so it can verify a result against the environment. The premise is that a judge that gathers its own evidence should be grounded by it. We show that this grounding does not immunize the judge against confirmation bias: a single false pre-screening note, attributed to an authority and carrying no evidential bearing on the correct verdict, overturns up to 40% of a tool-using judge's correct verdicts across four judges and five datasets, even though the judge collects decisive evidence. The shift is driven by specific properties of the cue. We treat this cue as a controlled diagnostic of confirmation bias rather than an adversarial attack. Our central contribution is to explain how confirmation bias changes the judge's behavior in a trajectory. We classify changes in judge behavior and attribute changes to one of three failure modes: over-trust (verify, conclude correctly, then defer), hallucination (fabricate support for the flip), and under-investigation (stop at the first corroborating signal). For the two Claude judges, the failure is predominantly over-trust (67-75% of harmful flips), while smaller judges have error modes spread across all three. Lightweight mitigations blunt but never remove the bias.
中文翻译
开放式代理任务的评估越来越多地交由“代理作为裁判”机制：裁判被赋予工具和代理的完整轨迹，以验证结果是否符合环境。其前提是，通过自主收集证据的裁判应具有真实性。我们发现，这种“根基性”并不能使裁判免于确认偏误：一个仅具象征意义的虚假预筛选备注（来自权威来源，无实际证据支持），即可在四个裁判和五个数据集上导致高达40%的正确判决被推翻，即使裁判已收集到决定性证据。该影响由线索的特定属性驱动。我们将此线索视为对确认偏误的受控诊断，而非对抗性攻击。我们的核心贡献在于解释确认偏误如何改变裁判在轨迹中的行为。我们分类了裁判行为的变化，并将其归因于三种失败模式：过度信任（验证后正确得出结论，随后盲从）、幻觉（虚构支持反转的证据）和调查不足（在首个佐证信号处停止）。对于两个Claude裁判，主要失败模式为过度信任（67–75%的有害反转）；而较小的裁判则三种错误模式分布较均。轻量级缓解措施虽能削弱偏误，但无法彻底消除。
👤 Drake Benjamin Brown, Khushbu Pahwa, Yubin Ge, Salvatore Romeo, Akhil Agnihotri et al. 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv

AGORA: Reliability-Aware Aggregation of LLM Judges via Item-Response Theory
AGORA：基于项目反应理论的可靠性感知LLM裁判聚合
LLM-as-a-judge is a common substitute for costly human evaluation, and aggregating several judges is the standard remedy for any single judge's bias. Judge pools mix judges of very different reliability, while the human-labeled sets that validate them hold a few hundred items or fewer. In this regime the per-item mean weights weak judges as heavily as strong ones, and confusion-table models such as Dawid-Skene must estimate a per-judge error table from too few items. We propose AGORA (Aggregation Of Reliability-weighted Assessments), an item-response-theory aggregator that places every item on one latent quality scale and describes each judge by a sensitivity parameter and ordered rating thresholds, so judge reliability is learned from all items jointly and without human labels. Across three benchmarks, AGORA matches or exceeds Dawid-Skene on large evaluation sets, and its advantage widens as the set shrinks toward a few hundred items. It remains accurate as pools grow more uneven, a label-free dispersion statistic indicates in advance when trust weighting helps, and its learned trust weights track per-judge human validity.
中文翻译
LLM作为裁判是昂贵人工评估的常见替代方案，聚合多个裁判是应对单一裁判偏差的标准方法。然而，裁判池中裁判可靠性差异巨大，而用于验证的标注数据集通常仅包含数百项。在此情况下，每项的平均权重会同等对待高可靠性和低可靠性裁判，而如Dawid-Skene等混淆表模型需从过少的样本中估计每位裁判的错误矩阵。我们提出AGORA（可靠性加权评估聚合），一种基于项目反应理论的聚合器，将每项置于单一潜在质量尺度上，并用敏感度参数和有序评分阈值描述每位裁判，从而联合学习所有项目中的裁判可靠性，且无需人工标签。在三个基准测试中，AGORA在大规模评估集上表现与或超过Dawid-Skene，且随着评估集缩小至数百项时优势愈发明显。其在裁判池日益不均衡时仍保持准确性；标签无关的分散统计量可提前指示信任加权是否有效；其学习到的信任权重与人工有效性高度一致。
👤 Junhyung Ahn 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv
[2611.05548]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Iterated creation, grading, and revision of data science projects by language models
语言模型迭代创建、评分与修订数据科学项目
We evaluate the extent to which agentic AI models can complete an end-to-end data science project, and how this improves with feedback. A 'writer' model with specific tools creates a complete data science project including downloading, cleaning, and analyzing a dataset, and writing a short paper about it. The project is executed and a 'grader' model evaluates it. The writer can then revise its submission, and this repeats for a total of three rounds. We consider seven models from five developers: Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek, and Z.AI. Each model is both a writer and a grader, so there are 49 writer-grader pairs and we run each pair three times. Of the 147 initial submissions, 107 run and render, and the average score increases from 59 per cent to 76 per cent after the final round of feedback. By writer the extent of the increase ranges from 3 percentage points for Anthropic's Opus 5.5 to 46 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5, and by grader it ranges from 13 percentage points for Google's Gemini 3.8 Flash to 32 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5. When a model grades its own writing the score tends to be about 9 percentage points higher than the average of what the other models give it. About half of the difference that we see from round to round is within the grader's own noise. Feedback generally results in the highest marks, but even just having the chance to revise, or having the additional budget, in terms of time or tool calls, without any feedback, results in considerable increases for some of the models but not for others. We find that how much such a loop helps depends on which model plays which role, but weaker models generally do not improve stronger models.
中文翻译
我们评估了智能体AI模型完成端到端数据科学项目的能力，以及反馈如何提升这一能力。一个具有特定工具的‘写作者’模型创建完整的数据科学项目，包括下载、清洗和分析数据集，并撰写一篇短论文。项目执行后，由‘评分者’模型进行评估。写作者可据此修订提交内容，此过程重复三轮。我们测试了来自五家开发者的七种模型：Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、DeepSeek 和 Z.AI。每种模型既是写作者又是评分者，共形成49对组合，每对运行三次。在147次初始提交中，107次成功运行并呈现结果，平均得分从59%提升至76%（最终反馈后）。按写作者划分，得分提升幅度从Anthropic Opus 5.5的3个百分点到Haiku 4.5的46个百分点不等；按评分者划分，从Google Gemini 3.8 Flash的13个百分点到Anthropic Haiku 4.5的32个百分点不等。当模型自行评分时，其得分通常比其他模型平均分高出约9个百分点。我们观察到的每轮间差异中，约一半源于评分者的自身噪声。反馈总体上带来最高分数，但即使没有反馈，仅提供修订机会或额外的时间/工具调用预算，也能显著提升部分模型的表现，而对其他模型则无明显帮助。我们发现该循环带来的帮助程度取决于模型所扮演的角色，但较弱模型通常无法提升较强模型的表现。

SlackBench: Benchmarking Agents on Collaborative Projects Grounded in Real Code Repositories
SlackBench：基于真实代码仓库协作项目的智能体基准测试
AI Agents are becoming digital coworkers that can browse the web, write code, run experiments, and assist humans over extended horizons. Yet existing benchmarks fall short of real knowledge work: many rely on synthetic data or isolated tasks, missing the complex, multi-party dynamics of collaborative projects, where critical information is scattered across conversations, code, and evolving decisions. Even simple workplace questions may require locating relevant channels and direct messages, recovering context from long discussions, inspecting repository state, reconciling stale or conflicting evidence, and synthesizing across sources. We introduce SlackBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on realistic research and engineering workflows that require joint reasoning over workplace communication and evolving repository state. Grounded in privacy-preserving reenactments of real academic research projects, SlackBench provides reproducible environments with realistic multi-party dialogue, evolving decisions, and corresponding code changes grounded in GitHub repositories and Google Docs. It contains 140 queries spanning heterogeneous-source agentic search, abstention on unanswerable or false-premise questions, goal-directed summarization, and circumstance inference over long conversations. These tasks are not reducible to static corpus question answering: agents must determine which conversations matter, traverse threads, inspect repository state, and synthesize evidence across communication and code artifacts. Across 33 frontier models and agent harnesses, the best system scores around 80% and most agents score below 66%, highlighting substantial headroom for improvement. SlackBench thus provides an extensible framework for evaluating agents in collaborative work environments. The SlackBench dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench .
中文翻译
AI智能体正成为能够浏览网页、编写代码、运行实验并在长时间跨度内协助人类的数字同事。然而，现有基准测试难以反映真实知识工作：许多依赖合成数据或孤立任务，忽略了协作项目中复杂的多方互动，关键信息分散在对话、代码和不断演化的决策中。即使是简单的职场问题，也可能需要定位相关频道和私信、从长篇讨论中恢复上下文、检查仓库状态、协调过时或冲突的信息，并整合多源证据。我们提出SlackBench，一个用于评估智能体在真实科研与工程工作流中表现的基准，要求智能体对工作场所沟通和动态仓库状态进行联合推理。该基准基于隐私保护的现实学术研究项目重现，提供可复现的环境，包含真实的多方对话、动态决策以及与GitHub仓库和Google Docs对应的代码变更。它包含140个查询，涵盖异源智能体搜索、对无法回答或前提错误问题的回避、目标导向摘要，以及对长对话的情境推断。这些任务无法简化为静态语料问答：智能体必须判断哪些对话重要、遍历线程、检查仓库状态，并综合通信与代码成果中的证据。在33个前沿模型和智能体框架中，最佳系统得分约为80%，大多数智能体得分低于66%，表明仍有巨大提升空间。SlackBench为评估协作工作环境中智能体提供了可扩展的框架。SlackBench数据集可在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench 获取。

Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents
智能体Harness意识：将经验证据转化为工具使用智能体的操作控制
Tool descriptions tell an agent what an action can do, but they do not necessarily reveal whether that action is healthy or suitable at the moment of use. Execution harnesses often possess this information through monitors, traces, and recent failures while the model choosing the next action does not. We call this gap operational blindness. We introduce Agent Harness Awareness, a trust-separated design in which the host supplies a small, fresh, verified state snapshot before a consequential action while retaining measurement, enforcement, evaluation, and final dispatch authority. We test its tools-and-network instance with ToolRoute, a controlled isolation benchmark that holds the task and candidate semantics constant and varies the operational information available to the model. In a 216-decision ablation across GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Sonnet 5, and Gemini 3.7 Flash, verified tool-health state reduced mean observable policy regret by 96.9% against a same-shape neutral control (4,252 ms to 131 ms) and raised single-dispatch completion from 69.4% to 95.8%. In a separate 27-decision end-to-end replication with live local TCP/HTTP execution, OpenTelemetry client spans, and Toxiproxy-injected faults, verified state reduced mean regret from 8,956 ms to 0 ms (zero excess observable operational cost) and completed the live action in 9/9 model-by-fault cases versus 5/9 for the neutral control. These studies establish the tools-and-network instance of Agent Harness Awareness: selected host telemetry materially improves pre-dispatch operational choice. Runtime state, hardware pressure, economics, retry-aware recovery, and heterogeneous selection remain open empirical extensions of the same architecture.
中文翻译
工具描述告诉智能体某个操作能做什么，但并未揭示该操作在执行时刻是否健康或合适。执行Harness通常通过监控、追踪和近期失败记录掌握此类信息，而负责选择下一步动作的模型却无法获取。我们称这种差距为操作盲区。我们提出Agent Harness Awareness，一种信任分离的设计：在关键操作前，主机提供一份小型、新鲜、经验证的状态快照，同时保留度量、执行、评估和最终调度的权威。我们在ToolRoute（一个受控隔离基准）中测试其工具与网络实例，保持任务和候选语义不变，仅改变模型可获得的操作信息。在GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Sonnet 5和Gemini 3.7 Flash的216次决策消融实验中，经验证的工具健康状态使平均可观测策略遗憾降低96.9%（从4,252毫秒降至131毫秒），单次调度完成率从69.4%提升至95.8%。在另一项27次决策的端到端复现实验中，包含实时本地TCP/HTTP执行、OpenTelemetry客户端跨度及Toxiproxy注入故障，经验证状态使平均遗憾从8,956毫秒降至0毫秒（零额外可观测操作成本），并在9个模型-故障组合中全部成功完成操作，而中性对照组仅完成5/9。这些研究确立了Agent Harness Awareness在工具与网络场景下的有效性：选定的主机遥测显著改善了调度前的操作决策。运行时状态、硬件压力、经济成本、重试感知恢复及异构选择仍为该架构的开放性实证扩展方向。
👤 Manu Agrawal, Shrey Nagpal 📅 2026-09-13 🔗 arXiv

SpecCompact: Accelerating LLM Agent Context Compaction with Slot-Parallel Semantic Speculative Verification
SpecCompact：通过槽并行语义推测验证加速大语言模型智能体的上下文压缩
LLM agents use context compaction to manage growing interaction histories, but summarizing long histories with large models can introduce substantial delays. Compaction therefore requires balancing information retention against latency. We observe that structured summary slots can retain information when generated independently. Based on this observation, we present SpecCompact, a framework that accelerates compaction through slot-parallel semantic speculation. SpecCompact fine-tunes a small drafter and generates slots in parallel. The target model verifies each proposed line and repairs rejected content, while subsequent drafting uses the updated slot prefix. System optimizations further reduce repeated context processing and model calls. Across AppWorld and $τ^3$ benchmarks with two target models, SpecCompact achieves $2.04$--$5.63\times$ compaction speedups over Hermes-style summarization. Separate downstream evaluations reveal model- and task-dependent performance trade-offs. A user study within Hermes further shows an $8.68\times$ median compaction speedup.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体使用上下文压缩来管理不断增长的交互历史，但使用大型模型对长历史进行摘要会引入显著延迟。因此，压缩需要在信息保留与延迟之间取得平衡。我们观察到，独立生成的结构化摘要槽能够保留信息。基于此观察，我们提出SpecCompact框架，通过槽并行语义推测加速压缩过程。SpecCompact微调了一个小型草稿模型，并并行生成各个槽。目标模型验证每个提议的条目并修复被拒绝的内容，后续草稿则使用更新后的槽前缀。系统优化进一步减少了重复的上下文处理和模型调用。在AppWorld和$τ^3$基准测试中，使用两个目标模型，SpecCompact相比Hermes风格摘要实现了2.04至5.63倍的压缩速度提升。独立的下游评估揭示了模型和任务相关的性能权衡。在Hermes中的用户研究进一步显示，压缩速度中位数提升了8.68倍。
👤 Yulu An, Yifei Feng, Xiangyang Yin 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Persona Perspectives: Adopting Viewpoints Reorganizes Representations in LLMs
人格视角：采用不同立场重新组织大语言模型的表征
Common methods for monitoring features in large language models (LLMs) such as linear probes and sparse autoencoders (SAEs) assume that features maintain a consistent representation across contexts. LLMs, however, can flexibly adopt different perspectives or "personas" that suggest different underlying systems of conceptual relations. In this paper, we examine whether prompting an LLM to adopt a persona alters the representational geometry of the model's latent space. We extract representations of over 9,000 common words and 33 semantic axes from models adopting liberal and conservative political personas and compare these against two neutral baselines. We find that persona conditioning systematically alters both the projections of words onto semantic axes and the alignments among the axes themselves in directions consistent with the specified ideology. We then test the implications of this reorganization for two safety-relevant features: truthfulness and morality. Linear probes, SAE-based classifiers, and classifiers built from manually selected word representations, all trained without a persona, produce divergent results when applied to models conditioned on political personas, producing politically slanted ratings of truth and morality on controversial statements. These findings suggest that internal representations, as they are conventionally extracted, can exhibit different geometric relations when the model adopts personas, potentially undermining monitoring efforts.
中文翻译
当前用于监测大语言模型（LLMs）特征的方法，如线性探测和稀疏自编码器（SAEs），假设特征在不同上下文中保持一致的表征。然而，LLMs可以灵活地采用不同的视角或“人格”，暗示不同的概念关系体系。本文探讨提示LLM采用某种人格是否会改变其潜在空间的表征几何结构。我们从采用自由派和保守派政治人格的模型中提取超过9,000个常见词汇和33个语义轴的表征，并与两个中立基线进行比较。我们发现，人格条件会系统性地改变词汇在语义轴上的投影以及轴之间的对齐方向，且与指定意识形态一致。随后，我们测试这种重构对两个与安全相关特征的影响：真实性与道德性。在未使用人格训练的线性探测器、基于SAE的分类器以及由人工选择词表征构建的分类器，在应用于受政治人格条件影响的模型时，产生分歧结果，导致对争议性陈述的真实性与道德性评分呈现政治倾向。这些发现表明，当模型采用人格时，传统方式提取的内部表征可能表现出不同的几何关系，可能削弱监控努力。

Before You Continue: Evaluating Time-Sensitive Safety Awareness in Conversational Assistants
在继续之前：评估对话助手的时间敏感安全意识
Large language model (LLM) assistants increasingly help users with everyday activities that involve time-dependent constraints. However, users may not mention these constraints in later requests, requiring assistants to recognize and adjust their advice accordingly. To evaluate such capability, we introduce TASC (Temporal Action Safety in Conversation), a benchmark that evaluates whether LLMs apply time-dependent constraints when responding to conversational requests. TASC consists of 3,418 inputs derived from 128 templates, along with a separate set of 33 scenarios designed to compare models' ability to calculate elapsed time with their ability to apply that information. Across 13 models, assistants account for time-dependent constraints in only 30.5% of cases where the proposed action violates a rule, while rarely warning users unnecessarily. More importantly, models fail to apply these constraints in 62.1% of cases despite correctly calculating the elapsed time. Explicitly providing the timing resolves only about half of these failures, revealing a gap between temporal reasoning and its application in conversational advice.

LooM: Loop-Invariant Memory for Causal Encoder-Decoder Language Models
LooM：因果编码器-解码器语言模型中的循环不变记忆
Looped Transformers increase effective depth by re-executing shared layers, but three limitations hinder their adoption: memory grows with the loop count, compute can only be adjusted in whole-block steps, and the loop count is hard to change after training. We propose LooM (Loop-invariant Memory), which addresses each in turn. In a causal encoder-decoder (CED), LooM loops only the decoder layers after the first, which read a global memory written once per token, and each of them publishes the key-value (KV) entry for a token only on its first visit, so the cache does not grow with the loop count. These layers loop independently, providing every budget from 3 to 21 layer executions per token, and one model trained on randomly sampled budgets covers all of them without a router. With enough loops, LooM outperforms a single-pass baseline trained on the same data on language modeling, zero-shot accuracy and long-context perplexity, while using the same cache size. Our analysis further shows that the training budget distribution decides what extra depth buys. These results suggest that a CED is a natural place to loop: memory is written once, and depth becomes a budget chosen at deployment.
中文翻译
循环Transformer通过重复执行共享层来增加有效深度，但存在三个限制阻碍其采用：内存随循环次数增长，计算只能以整块步长调整，且训练后难以更改循环次数。我们提出LooM（循环不变记忆），逐一解决这些问题。在因果编码器-解码器（CED）中，LooM仅对第一个之后的解码器层进行循环，这些层读取每标记写入一次的全局记忆，且每个层仅在其首次访问时发布某个标记的关键值（KV）条目，因此缓存不会随循环次数增长。这些层独立循环，可实现每标记3至21次层执行的任意预算，一个在随机采样的预算上训练的模型即可覆盖全部情况，无需路由器。在足够多循环的情况下，LooM在语言建模、零样本准确率和长上下文困惑度方面优于在同一数据上训练的单次通过基线，同时使用相同的缓存大小。我们的分析进一步表明，训练预算分布决定了额外深度带来的收益。这些结果表明，CED是自然适合循环的架构：记忆仅写入一次，深度成为部署时选择的预算。
👤 Lele Yang, Muxi Diao, Zhonghao Yan, Jiankang Chen, Bin Wen et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06808]

LLMs Don't Know What They Know: Benchmarking Temporal Self-Knowledge for Financial Backtesting
LLMs并不知道它们所知：面向金融回测的时间自我知识基准测试
Financial backtests of large language models (LLMs) are meaningful only if the model did not already know the outcomes it is asked to predict. Practitioners guard against this by trusting a vendor's stated knowledge cutoff, by asking the model what it knows, or by instructing it to answer as of a past date. To verify whether any of these safeguards can be trusted, we introduce FinDendro, a benchmark of 2,226 dated U.S. facts from 1999 to 2026, covering monthly macroeconomic series, month-end stock prices and Federal Reserve decisions. Each fact yields four prompts: a value question, Recall and Predict questions that pair each candidate value with a claim of knowing or guessing without a declared date, and a Predict question with a historical declared date. Controls for persistence and dominant outcomes keep chance accuracy near 0.27. Results show that for most frontier models, self-reports understate knowledge. GPT-5 answers 76% of controlled facts correctly but claims to know 38%, and answers 61% of the facts it says it does not know. Knowledge claims can persist beyond the measured knowledge boundary, by up to seven quarters, and a declared date removes most of the claims of prior knowledge while leaving accuracy unchanged. Backtests inside a model's training window therefore cannot be certified leak-free by self-report, instruction or stated cutoff. These findings motivate behavioral measurement of temporal knowledge boundaries, and FinDendro provides a benchmark and procedure for selecting backtest periods based on demonstrated factual knowledge.
中文翻译
只有当大型语言模型（LLMs）尚未知晓其需预测的结果时，其金融回测才具有意义。从业者通过信任供应商声明的知识截止时间、询问模型自身所知内容，或指令其以过去某日期的状态作答来防范此类问题。为验证这些防护措施是否可信，我们引入FinDendro——一个包含2,226个1999年至2026年美国事实的基准数据集，涵盖月度宏观经济指标、月末股票价格及美联储决策。每个事实生成四个提示：价值问题、回忆与预测问题（分别配对已知或猜测的陈述，且不声明具体日期），以及带有历史声明日期的预测问题。通过控制持久性和主导结果，随机准确率维持在约0.27。结果显示，对于大多数前沿模型，自我报告低估了其知识水平。GPT-5正确回答了76%的受控事实，但声称仅知道38%，且对其声称不知的事实中有61%仍能正确回答。知识声明可超出测量的知识边界达七个季度之久；而声明具体日期虽大幅减少对先前知识的声称，却未影响准确性。因此，仅凭自我报告、指令或声明截止时间无法确保模型训练窗口内回测完全无泄漏。这些发现推动了对时间知识边界的行为测量，FinDendro为此提供了基准和基于实际事实知识选择回测周期的方法。

Reasoning over Non-Parametric Memory in Multilingual LLMs: A Cognitive-Based Analysis of Relational Knowledge
多语言LLM中非参数记忆的推理：关系知识的认知基础分析
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) assumes that language models can reason over relations stated in their context rather than fall back on associations memorized during pretraining. We study this evidence-conditioned relational reasoning in isolation, with the facts supplied directly in the prompt rather than retrieved. \textsc{Rel-Bloom} is a controlled multilingual diagnostic of 450 multiple-choice questions built from 30 Wikidata relations; the same source relations are instantiated at five Bloom-inspired levels (Remember, Understand, Apply, Analyze, Evaluate) and under three veracity conditions (Factual, Counterfactual, Neutralized). Across 43 models, six languages, and two multilingual settings (226,800 predictions), three findings stand out. First, accuracy drops sharply from Apply to Analyze in every evaluated open-weight model series, reaching 47.2 percentage points for the Large Granite bucket, but not for either Gemini model. Second, Evaluate items remain a bottleneck even for models near ceiling on the other task types: Gemini~3.1 Pro averages 99.5\% on the other four levels but 60.7\% on Evaluate. Third, item- and relation-level difficulty rankings are consistently preserved across languages, indicating a difficulty structure partly shared across languages within this benchmark. Across knowledge conditions, Counterfactual contexts are generally harder than Factual ones, while removing entity identities can improve higher-level performance in some model regimes. Greater model capability therefore does not by itself ensure reliable reasoning over supplied relational evidence. Relevant resources are available at: https://huggingface.co/spaces/QCRI/non-parametric-memory-relational-reasoning.

检索增强生成（RAG）假设语言模型能够基于上下文中的关系进行推理，而非依赖预训练期间记忆的关联。我们独立研究这一基于证据的关系推理能力，将事实直接提供在提示中而非通过检索获取。 extsc{Rel-Bloom}是一个受控的多语言诊断数据集，包含450道多项选择题，源自30个Wikidata关系；相同来源关系在五个Bloom启发式层级（记忆、理解、应用、分析、评估）下实例化，并在三种真实性条件下（事实型、反事实型、中性化）呈现。在43个模型、六种语言及两种多语言设置（共226,800次预测）中，三个发现尤为突出。第一，在所有评估的开放权重模型系列中，准确率从“应用”到“分析”阶段急剧下降，最大降幅达47.2个百分点（大型Granite桶），但Gemini模型未出现此现象。第二，即使在其他任务类型接近天花板的模型中，“评估”类题目仍是瓶颈：Gemini~3.1 Pro在其他四个层级平均准确率达99.5%，但在“评估”层级仅为60.7%。第三，项目与关系层面的难度排名在各语言间保持一致，表明该基准中存在部分跨语言共享的难度结构。在不同知识条件下，反事实情境普遍比事实情境更难，而移除实体身份在某些模型范式下反而提升了高级别表现。因此，更强的模型能力本身并不能保证可靠地基于所提供关系证据进行推理。相关资源可在以下地址获取：https://huggingface.co/spaces/QCRI/non-parametric-memory-relational-reasoning。

When Do Summaries Become Instructions? Unsupported Policy in LLM Agents' Self-Written Handoff Summaries
摘要何时变成指令？LLM代理自写交接摘要中的不当策略
Long-running LLM agents compact their context, replacing most history with a summary. We ask whether handoff summaries state permissions or requirements the record does not support, whether such sentences change what the next instance (the successor) does, and where they come from. Synthetic tool-use tasks carry a ground-truth permission ledger; same-snapshot replay reruns the successor with those whole sentences replaced by neutral filler. Two AI readers, reading only rule-flagged held-out summaries, confirmed unsupported restrictions in 1.2% and 2.4% of all readable summaries from two open-weight models, and unsupported authorizations in 0.1% and 1.3%. For restrictions, a pre-specified replay test was not significant (p = 0.58); a follow-up, planned after it, found a fragile effect (p = 0.027). Neutralizing false approvals cut unauthorized actions from 24 to 7 of 120 held-out runs (post hoc; later set: 17 to 11 of 48). Most development restrictions followed the agent's earlier reasoning that an action needs approval (post hoc). In pre-specified tests, replacing the system prompt's "Some actions need approval." with one saying where requirements come from cut unsupported restrictions from 5.0% to 1.8% of development summaries and, on held-out templates, from 1.8% to 0.3% (both p < 10^-9), while requests for approvals the ledger requires barely changed. Code and data: https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit

长期运行的LLM代理会压缩其上下文，用摘要替代大部分历史记录。我们探究交接摘要中是否存在未被原始记录支持的权限或要求声明，这些句子是否影响后续代理（继承者）的行为，以及它们的来源。在合成工具使用任务中设有真实权限账本；通过同快照重放实验，将继承者的完整句子替换为中性填充物。两名AI阅读器仅阅读规则标记的保留摘要，确认在两个开源模型的可读摘要中分别存在1.2%和2.4%的不支持限制，以及0.1%和1.3%的不支持授权。对于限制项，预设重放测试无显著差异（p = 0.58）；后续计划测试发现脆弱效应（p = 0.027）。中和虚假批准后，未经授权行为从120次中的24次降至7次（事后检验；后期组：17次降至11次）。大多数开发阶段的限制源于代理早期推理认为某行为需审批（事后分析）。在预设测试中，将系统提示中的“某些操作需要审批”替换为说明要求来源的提示，使开发摘要中不支持的限制从5.0%降至1.8%，在保留模板中从1.8%降至0.3%（均p < 10^-9），而账本要求的审批请求几乎不变。代码与数据见：https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit
