ArcRAG: Agentic Compositional Retrieval over Knowledge Graphs
ArcRAG：知识图谱上的智能体组合式检索
Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) answers questions by retrieving over a knowledge graph built from a corpus. Yet retrieval strategies are designed around the structure their own construction pipeline produces, and whether they survive a change of construction is rarely tested. We study this cross-construction portability: whether one retrieval procedure, with no construction-specific schema, relation names, or retuning, stays effective when the graph is built differently. We introduce ArcRAG, which induces a query-local graph interface at inference time (the node labels, relation descriptors, and property keys reachable from the entities a question names), plans multi-hop traversals against it, and executes and repairs them as Cypher. We build each of the MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and TriviaQA corpora as a schema-guided graph (typed nodes, a closed relation set, source provenance) and as an open-information-extraction graph (untyped nodes, free-text relations), with the retriever's configuration fixed across them. We compare against a construction-agnostic Direct-Cypher agent, ablate each stage across retry and pass budgets on both graph families, and compare with results reported for prior GraphRAG systems, most of which design retrieval together with their own graph construction. Across the two constructions, we find that retrieval need not be co-designed with the graph: query-time interface induction keeps a construction-agnostic retriever effective when the construction changes, although construction still shapes absolute accuracy.
中文翻译
基于图的检索增强生成（GraphRAG）通过从语料库构建的知识图谱进行检索来回答问题。然而，检索策略通常围绕其自身构建流程产生的结构设计，很少测试其在构建方式改变时的适应性。我们研究了跨构建的可移植性：一种不依赖特定构建模式、关系名称或微调的检索过程，在图谱以不同方式构建时是否仍有效。我们提出ArcRAG，它在推理时动态诱导查询局部图接口（可从问题所命名实体访问的节点标签、关系描述符和属性键），规划多跳遍历，并以Cypher形式执行和修复。我们将MuSiQue、HotpotQA和TriviaQA语料库分别构建为模式引导图（带类型节点、封闭关系集、来源溯源）和开放信息抽取图（无类型节点、自由文本关系），并保持检索器配置一致。我们在两种图结构上对比了与构建无关的Direct-Cypher智能体，对各阶段在重试和传递预算下的表现进行消融分析，并与以往GraphRAG系统的报告结果比较，其中多数系统将检索与自身图谱构建联合设计。在两种构建方式下，我们发现检索无需与图谱共同设计：查询时接口诱导可使构建无关的检索器在构建变化时仍保持有效性，尽管构建方式仍影响绝对准确率。
👤 Jyotin Goel, Abhinav Arun, Tejas Prakash Agarwal, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06444]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
EvoGuard: Mitigating Degradation in Online Skill Evolution
EvoGuard：缓解在线技能演化的退化问题
Skills let language agents reuse experience across tasks as natural-language documents. Recent work evolves skills from interaction, but existing methods have limitations when skills evolve online. Prior methods update a skill offline from a batch of trajectories collected over many tasks. Some then adopt the new skill without any check, which can degrade the skill over time. Others keep it only if it improves performance on a held-out validation set, which is too slow and costly to run after every update. We introduce EvoGuard, a pairwise skill selector that mitigates skill degradation at low latency and cost. We first run the agent iteratively to build pools of skills and score each skill on a private test set to obtain utility labels. We pair the skills and use rejection sampling to keep only pairs with a clear utility gap. The selector is trained on the logits of two label tokens to pick the better skill. At deployment it decides in a single forward pass, which is significantly faster than evaluation on the private test set. Compared with adopting every new skill, EvoGuard improves success by 11.0% on ALFWorld, 5.9% on ScienceWorld, and 2.7% on LiveCodeBench.
中文翻译
技能使语言代理能够跨任务复用经验，作为自然语言文档。近期工作尝试从交互中演化技能，但现有方法在在线技能演化时存在局限。先前方法从多个任务收集的轨迹批次中离线更新技能。一些方法在未验证的情况下直接采用新技能，可能导致技能随时间退化；另一些方法仅在性能提升于保留验证集时才保留新技能，但每次更新后运行该验证代价过高且耗时。我们提出EvoGuard，一种低延迟、低成本的成对技能选择机制，以缓解技能退化。我们首先迭代运行代理以构建技能池，并在私有测试集上评分以获得效用标签。然后将技能配对，使用拒绝采样仅保留效用差距明显的配对。选择器在两个标签token的logits上训练，以挑选更优技能。部署时，它仅需一次前向传播即可决策，远快于在私有测试集上评估。相比采纳每个新技能，EvoGuard在ALFWorld上成功率提升11.0%，ScienceWorld上提升5.9%，LiveCodeBench上提升2.7%。
👤 Yanli Wang, Zichen Bai, Tianyue Jiang, Kunming Shao, Junhao Shi et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Twists and Turns: A Graph-Based Benchmark and Framework for Long-Term Memory in Exploration Tasks
曲折与转折：面向探索任务中长期记忆的图基准与框架
Long-term problem-solving processes, such as scientific research and software development, proceed along an evolving trial-and-error trajectory toward a central goal. We term these \textit{exploration tasks}, which require models to remember not only facts but also how solutions are tested against intermediate outcomes and early attempts are continually revised or revived. However, existing long-term memory research focuses on factual recall and updates, overlooking the interconnected nature of exploration. We observe that exploration processes naturally form graphs, with branching approaches linked by revisions and reconsiderations. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{LExMem}, a graph-guided benchmark designed to evaluate \underline{\textsc{Mem}}ory in \underline{\textsc{L}}ong-term \underline{\textsc{Ex}}ploration tasks. We model exploration processes as graphs and introduce an evolutionary mechanism to increase their structural complexity, yielding 224 dialogues and 2,018 question-answer instances derived from graph structures. Building on the same insight, we propose \textsc{LExGraph}, a graph-based memory framework that incrementally organizes dialogues into interconnected memory nodes and retrieves evidence through hybrid retrieval and neighbor expansion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{LExMem} poses a significant challenge to existing retrieval- and memory-based systems, whereas \textsc{LExGraph} achieves superior performance and generalizes effectively to external benchmarks. Codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem.
中文翻译
长期问题求解过程，如科学研究和软件开发，沿着不断演化的试错轨迹向核心目标推进。我们称此类任务为“探索任务”，要求模型不仅记住事实，还需记住解决方案如何针对中间结果进行测试，以及早期尝试如何被持续修订或重新启用。然而，现有长期记忆研究聚焦于事实回忆与更新，忽视了探索过程的相互关联性。我们观察到，探索过程天然形成图结构，不同分支路径通过修订与重新考虑相连接。受此启发，我们提出 extsc{LExMem}，一个基于图的基准，旨在评估 extit{长时探索任务中的记忆能力}。我们将探索过程建模为图，并引入演化机制以增加其结构复杂性，由此生成224个对话和2,018个问答实例。基于相同洞察，我们提出 extsc{LExGraph}，一种基于图的记忆框架，将对话逐步组织为互联的记忆节点，并通过混合检索与邻居扩展进行证据检索。大量实验表明， extsc{LExMem}对现有检索与记忆系统构成重大挑战，而 extsc{LExGraph}展现出卓越性能，并能有效泛化至外部基准。代码与数据集见https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem。

Editing Large Language Models with Geometry-Aware Regularization
基于几何感知正则化的大型语言模型编辑
Knowledge editing has become a key technique for updating knowledge in large language models. However, existing methods neglect the geometric structure of the knowledge representation space, leading to two key limitations: they cannot distinguish which pre-trained knowledge should be preferentially preserved (core vs. non-core) nor which new edits are inherently difficult (hard vs. easy). To address this, we propose GEdit, a geometry-aware framework built on a Low-Rank Plus Diagonal (LR+D) factor model that captures the intrinsic low-rank structure of Transformer FFN hidden representations. GEdit features two complementary mechanisms. First, the low-rank regularization applies targeted protection on a low-rank core knowledge subspace. Second, the adaptive regularization dynamically adjusts the regularization strength based on each edit request's editability: relaxing constraints for hard edits to ensure success, and tightening them for easy edits to better preserve pretrained knowledge. Extensive experiments on GPT2-XL, GPT-J, and LLaMA-3-8B under large-scale editing scenarios, including both mini-batch and fully sequential editing settings with up to 10,000 continuous updates, show that GEdit consistently and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both editing success and knowledge preservation.
中文翻译
知识编辑已成为更新大型语言模型知识的关键技术。然而，现有方法忽视了知识表示空间的几何结构，导致两个关键局限：无法区分应优先保留的预训练知识（核心 vs. 非核心），也无法识别哪些新编辑本质上较难（难 vs. 易）。为此，我们提出GEdit，一种基于低秩加对角（LR+D）因子模型的几何感知框架，能够捕捉Transformer前馈网络隐藏表示的内在低秩结构。GEdit包含两个互补机制：第一，低秩正则化对低秩核心知识子空间施加针对性保护；第二，自适应正则化根据每次编辑请求的可编辑性动态调整正则化强度：对困难编辑放松约束以确保成功，对简单编辑收紧约束以更好保留预训练知识。在GPT2-XL、GPT-J和LLaMA-3-8B上进行的大规模编辑场景实验（包括最多10,000次连续更新的迷你批次和全序列编辑设置）表明，GEdit在编辑成功率和知识保留方面持续显著优于当前最先进的基线方法。

The Harness Is the Model: Agent Scaffolding Changes a Fixed LLM's Compliance with Banking Conduct Rules, Unevenly Across Models
框架即模型：智能体支架改变了固定大语言模型对银行行为规则的合规性，且在不同模型间表现不均
Banks validate language models, but they deploy systems: a model inside a harness that decides what it sees, which tools it can call and who checks its actions. We test whether that harness changes compliance with banking conduct rules when the model is held fixed. Four open-weight models each ran five harnesses (a pipeline that makes one structured decision, a ReAct-style tool loop, a loop in which the policy is reachable only through search, a loop with a long benign customer memory, and a loop with a same-model compliance reviewer) on 156 pre-registered cases in a simulated retail bank whose 11 rule families paraphrase EU consumer-credit, payments, investor-protection, data-protection and anti-money-laundering provisions. Every one of the 3120 two-turn episodes was scored deterministically from the executed tool trace, with violations and over-refusals counted separately. Pooled across models the harness moved the correct-case rate only between 87.3% and 92.1%, but within a model it mattered a great deal: for one model the spread between its best and worst harness was 11.5 percentage points, enough to move it from first to last place among the four models, while for another the harness made no detectable difference; the model x harness interaction itself was suggestive rather than conclusive. The same-model reviewer cut violations from 9.6% to 6.4% without raising over-refusals, contrary to our pre-registered prediction. Rules that require date arithmetic failed in every harness. In an exploratory analysis with two LLM judges and a blind human check, 26% of violations were accompanied by a customer message that denied the action the agent had just executed, which a transcript-only review would record as a correct refusal. For model-risk management the unit of validation should be the model-harness pair, and conduct testing should read the action log, not the conversation.

银行验证语言模型，但部署的是系统：一个包含模型的框架，该框架决定模型可见内容、可调用工具及动作审核者。我们测试当模型固定时，该框架是否改变其对银行行为规则的合规性。四个开源权重模型各自运行五种框架（一个结构化决策流水线、一个ReAct风格的工具循环、一个策略仅可通过搜索访问的循环、一个带有长期良性客户记忆的循环，以及一个由同模型合规审查员组成的循环），在模拟零售银行的156个预先注册案例中进行测试，其11类规则涵盖欧盟消费者信贷、支付、投资者保护、数据保护和反洗钱条款。所有3120个两轮对话均根据执行的工具轨迹进行确定性评分，违规与过度拒绝分别统计。跨模型汇总显示，正确案例率仅在87.3%至92.1%之间波动，但在单个模型内部差异显著：某一模型的最佳与最差框架之间差距达11.5个百分点，足以使其从四模型中的第一名降至末位；而另一模型则未表现出明显差异；模型与框架的交互效应具有提示性而非决定性。同模型审查员将违规率从9.6%降至6.4%，且未增加过度拒绝，这与预注册预测相反。要求日期计算的规则在所有框架中均失败。在使用两名LLM评判员和盲测人类检查的探索性分析中，26%的违规伴随客户消息否认代理刚刚执行的动作，若仅审查对话记录，会被误判为正确拒绝。对于模型风险管理，验证单位应为模型-框架组合，合规测试应查阅操作日志而非对话内容。

ProcessPrefBench: Evaluating Cross-Task Transfer of Individual Process Preferences from Naturalistic Human-AI Interaction
ProcessPrefBench：评估自然人机交互中个体过程偏好在跨任务间的迁移能力
In natural human-AI dialogue, users' corrections, requests, and self-descriptions carry reusable signals about how they want work to be done. These signals reveal individual process preferences, user-specific standards for evidence, depth, analytical focus, procedure, and delivery. We ask whether a preference revealed in one dialogue can shape an assistant's later answer when the task does not restate it. ProcessPrefBench uses three months of natural dialogues from two knowledge workers to build 66 source-target cases separately judged for source evidence and target applicability. Sources are preselected, so the benchmark tests application rather than retrieval. We test three models with target-only, full-history, and distilled-preference prompts. Target-specific 0-2 rubrics score preference realization, and a task gate flags severe failures. Compared with target-only prompting, full-history and imperative distilled-preference prompting raise preference realization by 0.204 and 0.244 points on the 0-2 scale, respectively, with no reliable advantage from distillation. Fabricated-evidence failures rise from 5.8% under target-only prompting to 10.1% under full-history and 10.4% under imperative prompting. ProcessPrefBench highlights a tension in learning from dialogue, where drawing on prior interactions can improve preference realization yet also invite fabricated evidence. We hope it helps build assistants that honor a user's process preferences without sacrificing factual reliability.

Familiar letters, not related languages, let unseen languages into a shared space of meaning
熟悉的字母，而非相关语言，让未见语言进入共享意义空间
Hundreds of languages have written text but almost no translations, and whether a model learning one from untranslated text builds on a related language or familiar letters is unclear: they usually coincide. Here we show that familiar letters, not a related language, let an unseen language into a shared space of meaning. GlyphMind, a tokenizer-free vision transformer that reads text as pixels and lets any sentence be shown in any script, was pretrained to align translations across 27 writing systems. Adapted on untranslated Lao, a language and script it had never seen, it retrieved English translations seven times as often as the strongest simple baseline. Rewritten in unfamiliar letters, Lao did not enter although its close relative Thai was in pretraining; Santali, which had none, entered in Devanagari, familiar from Hindi, but not in its own script. Transliteration into familiar scripts may thus open multilingual models to languages without translations.
中文翻译
数百种语言拥有书面文本，但几乎没有任何翻译。当模型从无翻译文本中学习一种语言时，它究竟是基于相关语言还是熟悉的字母尚不明确——因为两者通常重合。本文表明，真正让一种未见过的语言进入共享意义空间的是熟悉的字母，而非相关语言。GlyphMind是一种无需分词器的视觉Transformer，它将文本作为像素读取，允许任何句子以任意书写系统呈现。该模型在27种书写系统间对齐翻译的基础上进行预训练。在未见过的寮语（Lao）文本上进行微调，尽管其从未接触过该语言和书写系统，却比最强的简单基线模型检索英文翻译的频率高出七倍。当寮语改写为不熟悉的书写形式时，即使其近亲泰语曾参与预训练，也无法进入；而桑塔利语（Santali）虽无自身书写系统，但以熟悉的形式（如印地语使用的天城文）进入，而在其自身书写系统中则无法进入。因此，将文本转写为熟悉书写系统可能使多语言模型能够处理无翻译的语言。

When Does Better Confidence Improve a Knowledge-Graph Audit?
更好的置信度何时能提升知识图谱审计效果？
Confidence estimates guide human review in LLM-built knowledge graphs, but better error prediction can yield little improvement in an audit. We study this gap through repairability, selection headroom and label validity. Modelling non-adaptive auditing as budgeted covering, we characterise the minimal decision-relevant error statistics and bound the regret of scalar-preserving first-order planning by a structural mass $Δ^{\mathrm{cl}}$. We simulate ideal repair against benchmark-aligned labels for five extraction configurations and two cross-document graphs on Re-DocRED, followed by frozen confirmation on test and replication on 500 hash-selected training documents. On the held-out cross-document graphs, an error-rate $\times$ fan-out queue leaves 3.98-4.74 percentage points more errors than a joint model with the same actions. By comparison, opening shared actions for that joint planner reduces residual errors by 0.89-1.43 points relative to its own fact-only plans, in point estimates. Equivalence between joint and independent-block models remains inconclusive. Generic LLM verification improves three of four model-budget configurations on each held-out set. LLM adjudication identifies possible benchmark omissions.

Learning When Human Decisions Matter: Executor-Relative Consultation Value for LLM Agents
学习人类决策何时重要：面向 LLM 代理的执行者相对咨询价值
Large language models increasingly automate complex tasks, but human decision capacity does not scale with their capabilities. In many applications, humans remain essential for providing private context, authority, or judgments unavailable to the agent. We formulate human decision capacity allocation: given multiple consultation opportunities and a limited budget, at which decisions should an LLM agent consult a human? The central challenge is estimating consultation value, which is executor-dependent because the same human input may benefit different downstream agents differently. We introduce Consultation Value Distillation (CVD), which estimates consultation values offline using Monte Carlo executor rollouts and distills these values into lightweight predictors for budgeted allocation, requiring no rollout-based value estimation at deployment. Across five batch and three sequential settings with two frontier executors, CVD improves allocation through joint and continuation-aware value learning, while independent consultation values remain competitive in several settings. CVD also broadly outperforms direct prompting and Value of Information where applicable, remains robust with substantially less rollout supervision, and enables nearly lossless approximate allocation at low search cost.

大型语言模型日益自动化复杂任务，但人类决策能力并未随其能力同步扩展。在许多应用中，人类仍对提供私有上下文、权威或代理无法获取的判断至关重要。我们提出了人类决策能力分配问题：在多个咨询机会和有限预算下，LLM 代理应在哪些决策中咨询人类？核心挑战在于估计咨询价值，而该价值依赖于执行者，因为同一人类输入对不同下游代理的收益可能不同。我们引入咨询价值蒸馏（CVD），通过蒙特卡洛执行者回溯离线估计咨询价值，并将其蒸馏为轻量级预测器，用于预算分配，部署时无需基于回溯的价值估计。在五个批量和三个顺序设置中，使用两个前沿执行者，CVD 通过联合与延续感知的价值学习改进分配，而独立咨询价值在多个设置中仍具竞争力。CVD 在适用情况下广泛优于直接提示和信息价值方法，对回溯监督的需求显著减少，并能在极低搜索成本下实现近乎无损的近似分配。

Phonological Knowledge of Chinese Characters in Language Models
语言模型对汉字的语音知识
How much phonological knowledge do language models (LMs) possess about Chinese characters, and how is this knowledge represented and used? We introduce PinyinBench, a controlled benchmark covering homophones, phonological features (initials, finals, and tones), and polyphonic characters. We investigate 17 text-only and multimodal LMs through three complementary approaches: behavioral evaluation, layer-wise probing, and activation steering. Behaviorally, models demonstrate substantial knowledge of initials and finals, with a frontier model (DS-v4-flash) scoring 90%+ in homophone judgment, when two characters share no phonological features. However, its accuracy drops sharply to 72% when two characters differ only in lexical tone. Probing reveals that tone is highly decodable from embeddings and improves modestly across layers, whereas segmental information starts less decodable and improves substantially with depth. Activation steering shows that segmental representations can systematically influence generation, while tone exhibits much weaker effects. Furthermore, audio-bearing training improves phonological decodability, including tone, without corresponding improvements in steering effectiveness. Together, these findings reveal distinct representational and functional profiles for segmental and tonal information, with tonal information much harder to access and manipulate, demonstrating the necessity for combined evaluation, probing, and causal intervention for understanding linguistic knowledge in LMs.

语言模型（LMs）对汉字拥有多少语音知识，以及这些知识如何被表示和使用？我们引入了PinyinBench，一个受控基准测试，涵盖同音字、语音特征（声母、韵母和声调）以及多音字。我们通过三种互补方法研究了17个仅文本和多模态LM：行为评估、逐层探测和激活操控。行为上，模型表现出对声母和韵母的显著知识，前沿模型（DS-v4-flash）在两个汉字无任何语音特征共享的情况下，同音判断得分超过90%。然而，当两个汉字仅在词汇声调上不同时，其准确率急剧下降至72%。探测结果显示，声调从嵌入中高度可解码，并随层数略有提升；而音段信息初始可解码性较低，但随深度显著改善。激活操控表明，音段表征可系统性影响生成，而声调影响较弱。此外，包含音频的训练提升了语音可解码性（包括声调），但并未相应提高操控有效性。综合来看，这些发现揭示了音段与声调信息在表征和功能上的显著差异，其中声调信息更难访问和操控，强调了结合评估、探测和因果干预来理解LM中语言知识的必要性。
👤 Xiaozhe Ji, Renfen Hu, Hai Hu 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.05731]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Prospective Memory for Proactive LLM Agents
面向主动型大语言模型代理的前瞻性记忆
Most memory systems for LLM agents are retrospective, preserving and retrieving past information to inform current decisions. Proactive agents, however, also require prospective memory: retaining future-directed intentions and realizing them when appropriate opportunities arise, even without explicit requests. Crucially, remembering the right intention does not guarantee acting at the right time: a related observation may call for waiting, while a later cue may make the same intention actionable. Inspired by cognitive theories of human prospective memory, we introduce Pro-Memory, a memory framework that separates maintaining future intentions from deciding when and how to realize them. Pro-Memory preserves pending objectives linked to their supporting evidence, assesses new observations against their original goals and activation conditions, and coordinates appropriate contributions with the agent's ongoing actions. It further updates intention states based on actions actually issued or evidence of resolution, while an experience memory distills feedback from completed episodes into guidance for future decisions. Experiments on two benchmarks spanning delayed-intention tasks and open-ended proactive assistance demonstrate consistent improvements in proactive task performance over representative memory approaches across multiple LLM backbones, while limiting unnecessary interventions.
中文翻译
大多数大语言模型代理的记忆系统是回顾性的，用于保存和检索过去信息以支持当前决策。然而，主动型代理还需要前瞻性记忆：保留未来导向意图，并在适当机会出现时予以实现，即使没有明确请求。关键在于，记住正确的意图并不保证在正确时间行动：某个相关观察可能需要等待，而稍后的线索可能使同一意图变得可执行。受人类前瞻性记忆认知理论启发，我们提出Pro-Memory，一种将保持未来意图与决定何时及如何实现它们相分离的记忆框架。Pro-Memory 保留与支持证据相关的待办目标，根据原始目标和激活条件评估新观察，并协调与代理当前行动相适应的贡献。它还根据实际发出的动作或解决证据更新意图状态，同时经验记忆将已完成任务的反馈提炼为未来决策的指导。在涵盖延迟意图任务和开放式主动协助的两个基准测试上的实验表明，Pro-Memory 在多个大语言模型骨干架构上持续优于代表性记忆方法，同时有效减少了不必要的干预。
👤 Yuanchen Bei, Zhining Liu, Tianxin Wei, Weizhi Zhang, Zhichen Zeng et al. 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv

Rewrite, Then Veto: Rewriter-Agnostic Edit Gating for LLM-Based Post-OCR Correction
先重写，再否决：面向基于大语言模型的后OCR校正的重写无关编辑门控
LLMs can repair noisy OCR transcripts of historical documents, but they also change text that was already correct. We propose EditVeto, a veto gate placed after a full-context LLM rewrite. The rewrite is character-aligned to the OCR input, its differences are split into blocks, and a 0.6B-parameter gate keeps or reverts each block. An oracle analysis motivates the design: given the true error locations, per-span generation is worse than no correction, whereas vetoing a full rewrite's edits helps. Trained on rewrites by two Qwen3 models and evaluated, frozen, on the eight HIPE-OCRepair-2026 test sets, the gate improves all five rewriters tested, two of them unseen in training: weighted cMER falls by 0.0026-0.0044 and the preference score rises by 0.15-0.25 (paired bootstrap, p ~ 0.0002 each). On published competition outputs it lowers cMER for three of four teams but raises it for the winning run (0.0050 to 0.0065). In a second, disclosed test look, a retrained gate still improves LoRA-tuned rewriters (cMER -0.0011 to -0.0021), and an ensemble of their gated edits reaches a cMER of 0.0069 and a preference score of 0.928, mostly through fine-tuning and pooling rather than gating. Built only on public models and data, it is behind the winner (0.0050) and tied with the second-ranked run (0.0071), both from the only team using domain pre-training, and ahead of all other published runs. A third, secondary look shows the unchanged gate improving zero-shot Qwen3.8-27B (cMER 0.0114 to 0.0108) but only the preference score of its LoRA-tuned version.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）可以修复历史文献中的噪声OCR转录文本，但也可能改变原本正确的文本内容。我们提出EditVeto，一种放置在完整上下文LLM重写之后的否决门控机制。重写与OCR输入字符对齐，差异被分割为块，一个0.6B参数的门控机制决定保留或撤销每个块。通过一个理想分析验证设计动机：已知真实错误位置时，逐跨度生成比不修正更差，而否决整个重写的修改则有帮助。该门控在两个Qwen3模型生成的重写数据上训练，并在八个HIPE-OCRepair-2026测试集上以冻结状态评估，结果表明其改善了所有五个测试重写器，其中两个在训练中未见：加权cMER下降0.0026–0.0044，偏好分数上升0.15–0.25（配对bootstrap检验，p≈0.0002）。在公开竞赛输出上，它降低了四个团队中三个的cMER，但使获胜方案的cMER从0.0050升至0.0065。在第二次公开测试中，重新训练的门控仍能改善LoRA微调的重写器（cMER下降0.0011至0.0021），其集成门控编辑达到cMER 0.0069和偏好分数0.928，主要通过微调和聚合实现而非门控。该系统仅基于公开模型和数据构建，虽落后于冠军（0.0050），但与第二名（0.0071）持平，两者均来自唯一使用领域预训练的团队，且优于所有其他公开结果。第三次次要测试显示，未更改的门控仍能改善零样本Qwen3.8-27B（cMER从0.0114降至0.0108），但仅提升其LoRA微调版本的偏好分数。

When to Ask, When to Answer: Strategic Clarification for Ambiguous Reference Resolution in Dialogue
何时提问，何时回答：对话中模糊指代消解的战略性澄清
For humans, referential disambiguation is a collaborative process involving clarification questions asked when they will meaningfully change the outcome. However, large language models (LLMs) have been shown to be biased toward premature answering, preferring definite single-turn responses over more clarification exchanges. In this paper, we study the problem of "strategic clarification" -- asking clarification questions in a reference game when their answers will narrow the set of candidate referents. We construct a controlled test environment from the SIMMC 2.1 dataset that records how many questions each ambiguous reference needs to be successfully resolved, and examine how baseline LLM behavior and common post-training methods ask clarifying questions vs. attempt to answer the referent. We also propose BACE (Belief-Aware Critic with Epistemic tracking), a lightweight action chooser method on top of a frozen LM backbone, and show that BACE more consistently chooses the optimal number of clarifying questions than methods such as GRPO and ArCHer.
中文翻译
对人类而言，指代消解是一个协作过程，当澄清问题能实质性改变结果时才会提出。然而，大型语言模型（LLM）表现出过早回答的偏见，倾向于选择确定性的单轮回应而非更多澄清交流。本文研究“战略性澄清”问题——在参考游戏任务中，当澄清问题的答案能缩小候选指代对象范围时，应如何提问。我们基于SIMMC 2.1数据集构建了一个受控测试环境，记录每个模糊指代成功解决所需的提问次数，并考察基线LLM行为及常见后训练方法在提问澄清与尝试回答指代之间的表现差异。我们还提出了BACE（信念感知批评者与认知追踪），一种基于冻结LM主干的轻量级动作选择方法，结果显示BACE比GRPO和ArCHer等方法更一致地选择最优数量的澄清问题。

SRRM: A Retrieval-Reconstruction Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Context Memory in Small Language Models
SRRM：用于评估小型语言模型长上下文记忆的检索-重建基准
Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed on consumer-grade hardware, yet their ability to preserve information across progressively longer contexts remains insufficiently understood. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate retrieval-oriented tasks, providing limited insight into whether successful retrieval reflects comprehensive long-context memory preservation. To address this limitation, this paper introduces Simple Retrieval-Reconstruction Memory (SRRM), a lightweight and fully reproducible benchmark that jointly evaluates Targeted Retrieval and Global Information Reconstruction. This study also proposes the Memory Preservation Ratio (MPR), a benchmark-level metric that quantifies the relationship between retrieval and reconstruction performance. SRRM was evaluated using a synthetic dataset on three Qwen2.5 models (0.5B, 1.5B, and 3B parameters) across context loads ranging from 10 to 100 factual statements. Experimental results show that reconstruction performance deteriorated substantially faster than retrieval accuracy as contextual information increased, indicating that retrieval alone is insufficient to characterize long-context memory preservation. Among the evaluated models, Qwen2.5-1.5B achieved the highest retrieval accuracy (86.7%), reconstruction performance (45.7%), and MPR (0.53), outperforming the larger Qwen2.5-3B model (72.2%, 18.5%, and 0.26, respectively). These findings demonstrate that increasing parameter count does not necessarily improve long-context memory preservation. Overall, SRRM provides a reproducible and extensible benchmark for systematically evaluating long-context memory while revealing complementary memory behaviors that remain hidden under retrieval-only evaluation frameworks.
中文翻译
小型语言模型（SLMs）在消费级硬件上的部署日益广泛，但其在逐步延长的上下文范围内保持信息的能力仍缺乏充分理解。现有基准主要评估以检索为导向的任务，难以揭示成功的检索是否反映了全面的长上下文记忆保留。为解决这一局限，本文提出了一种轻量级且完全可复现的基准——简单检索-重建记忆（SRRM），该基准联合评估目标检索与全局信息重建。本研究还提出了记忆保留率（MPR）这一基准层面的度量指标，用于量化检索与重建性能之间的关系。SRRM在合成数据集上对三个Qwen2.5模型（0.5B、1.5B和3B参数）在10至100条事实陈述的上下文负载下进行了评估。实验结果表明，随着上下文信息增加，重建性能恶化速度远快于检索准确率，说明仅靠检索无法充分刻画长上下文记忆的保留情况。在所评估的模型中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在检索准确率（86.7%）、重建性能（45.7%）和MPR（0.53）方面均表现最佳，优于更大的Qwen2.5-3B模型（分别为72.2%、18.5%和0.26）。这些发现表明，增加参数量并不必然提升长上下文记忆的保留能力。总体而言，SRRM提供了一个可复现且可扩展的基准，可用于系统性评估长上下文记忆，并揭示了在仅依赖检索评估框架下隐藏的互补性记忆行为。

CCDWIKI: Decoupling Case Formulation from Response Generation in Longitudinal CBT Dialogue
CCDWIKI：在纵向认知行为疗法对话中解耦案例构想与响应生成
Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) demands more than empathic validation, requiring instead a structured formulation of the interplay among situations, automatic thoughts, emotions, and behaviors. Therapists formalize this interplay through a Cognitive Conceptualization Diagram, where maintaining longitudinal coherence necessitates continuous evidential adjudication to separate tentative hypotheses from established findings. Current dialogue systems track conversational history via retrieval or static profiles but lack the representational capacity to encode epistemic status. Consequently, provisional interpretations are often prematurely reified as established facts, inadvertently biasing downstream interventions. Addressing this limitation, CCDWiki is introduced as a plug-and-play advisor conceptualizing longitudinal therapy as evidence-grounded hypothesis adjudication. The approach encodes each clinical hypothesis as a mutable entry bound to corroborating and contradicting evidence. Across sessions, accumulating data strengthens supported entries, preserves provisional ones, and retracts contradicted assumptions. The resulting formulation distills into concise guidance for unmodified therapeutic language models. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in both conceptualization and intervention quality without requiring backbone retraining.
中文翻译
认知行为疗法（CBT）不仅需要共情验证，更要求对情境、自动思维、情绪与行为之间相互作用的结构化分析。治疗师通过认知概念化图来形式化这种相互作用，而维持纵向一致性需要持续的证据裁决，以区分暂定假设与已确立的发现。当前对话系统通过检索或静态档案追踪对话历史，但缺乏编码认识状态的表示能力。因此，临时解释常常被过早固化为既定事实，无意中影响后续干预。为解决此问题，本文提出CCDWIKI，一种即插即用的顾问系统，将纵向治疗视为基于证据的假设裁决过程。该方法将每个临床假设编码为可变条目，并绑定支持与反驳证据。在多轮会话中，累积数据强化支持条目，保留暂定条目，撤销矛盾假设。最终形成的构想可提炼为简洁指导，供未经修改的治疗语言模型使用。实验表明，该方法在不重新训练主干模型的情况下，显著提升了构想与干预质量。

A Framework for Context Enrichment and Selective Robustness in LLM Social Agents
大型语言模型社交代理的上下文增强与选择性鲁棒性框架
Researchers use large language models as agents in social simulations, where agents are prompted with observations and background information intended to shape their actions. Yet LLM agents have been shown to substantially change their behavior under meaning-preserving prompt variations, creating a validation problem for social simulation. One plausible hypothesis is that this brittleness reflects a lack of grounding and can be reduced by enriching the context provided to the agents. Whether richer context improves robustness remains unclear, in part because context richness and robustness are rarely defined in ways that support controlled comparisons. We address the first need with controlled context enrichment, a set of prompt substitution rules that systematically enriches distinct types of context: neutral padding, generic social background, descriptive decision grounding, and normative decision grounding. We address the second by introducing selective robustness as a validation criterion: insensitivity to meaning-preserving prompt variation combined with a directional response to decision-relevant changes in the social world. We demonstrate the framework in a Prisoner's Dilemma-like game, showing how it can be used to identify which forms of context enrichment produce more desirable and robust agent behavior. We find that these forms of enrichment produce distinct behavioral patterns that vary between the two models tested.
中文翻译
研究人员在社会模拟中使用大语言模型作为代理，通过提示观察结果和背景信息来引导其行为。然而，已有研究表明，即使提示语义保持不变，LLM代理的行为仍可能显著变化，导致社会模拟的验证问题。一种合理的假设是这种脆弱性反映了缺乏情境锚定，可通过丰富代理所接收的上下文来缓解。然而，上下文丰富度与鲁棒性之间的关系尚不明确，部分原因在于二者很少以支持可控比较的方式进行定义。为此，我们提出了受控上下文增强方法，即一组系统性的提示替换规则，用于逐步增强不同类型的上下文：中性填充、通用社会背景、描述性决策依据和规范性决策依据。同时，我们引入“选择性鲁棒性”作为验证标准：对语义保持不变的提示变化不敏感，但对社会世界中与决策相关的变化具有方向性响应。我们在类囚徒困境游戏中展示了该框架的应用，证明其可用于识别哪些形式的上下文增强能产生更理想且稳健的代理行为。实验发现，这些增强方式在两种测试模型中产生了不同的行为模式。

LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis
LongBanana：面向长上下文多参考图像合成的专家验证基准
We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.
中文翻译
我们提出了LongBanana，据我们所知，这是在代理驱动图像生成时代首个由专家设计并验证的长上下文多参考图像合成基准。该任务要求从分散的视觉证据中检索信息、排除干扰项、绑定实体与关系，并基于多个参考图像生成一幅连贯的最终图像。LongBanana包含90个精心设计的任务，按3×3分类体系组织；每个任务包含约32张参考图像、20个约束条件和50个评估项目，总计4,670个专家指定的原子评估项。其图谱基评估设计为每个任务提供参考图像和实体-关系图，将节点、边及整体图的义务转化为内容保真度、关系绑定和全局约束满足的检查清单项。评审专家标签包含15%的双标注子集，精确一致率达89.4%，加权二次kappa值为0.83。我们进一步提出了LongBanana-Gen——一个用于长周期图像生成的代理驱动框架；实验表明，最小化的代理调度优于直接生成，而ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect（RPR）工作流通过强化证据锚定与修复机制，实现了依赖于代理调度的性能提升。总体而言，LongBanana为衡量密集视觉证据利用提供了可审计的测试平台，并为开发更强的代理驱动图像生成系统设定了共同目标。代码与数据可在项目页面获取：https://long-banana.com/。

Template Gap Reconstruction for Shell Output Simulation with Small Language Models
小语言模型壳输出模拟中的模板间隙重建
Small language models offer a compact basis for shell simulation without executing submitted commands, but full-output decoding requires them to predict recurring utility structure alongside content that varies across executions. We introduce template gap reconstruction (TGR), which supplies reusable output literals interspersed with sentinels to be interleaved with autoregressively generated gaps. A template selector chooses which template to be reconstructed, with the empty template retaining full-output decoding. We construct a template bank from 18.7 million outputs collected across heterogeneous execution environments and train a template selector and five reconstruction models initialized from various small language model checkpoints on a target environment excluded from bank construction. Evaluation demonstrates that TGR improves output edit similarity (O-ES) over full-output decoding (FOD) when the optimal template covers more than 25\% of the template, while performing similarly for lower-coverage outputs. Additionally, TGR outperforms FOD for shorter gap sequences, with O-ES values of 0.964 and 0.812, respectively, while maintaining similar performance for longer sequences. TGR reduces the number of tokens generated by 26.3--38.3\% across used tokenizer families. These results support using a reusable output structure as a practical design choice for small shell simulators, improving response fidelity and shortening generation targets while keeping template selection and residual prediction central to response quality.
中文翻译
小语言模型为无需执行提交命令即可实现壳模拟提供了紧凑基础，但完整输出解码需要它们同时预测重复的实用结构和跨执行变化的内容。我们引入模板间隙重建（TGR），通过在可重用输出字面量之间插入哨兵标记，与自回归生成的间隙交错排列。模板选择器决定重建哪个模板，空模板则保留完整输出解码。我们从异构执行环境中收集的1870万条输出构建了模板库，并在排除库构建目标环境的前提下，使用多个小型语言模型检查点训练模板选择器和五个重建模型。评估表明，当最优模板覆盖超过25%时，TGR在输出编辑相似性（O-ES）上优于完整输出解码（FOD）；对于低覆盖率输出，表现相当。此外，TGR在较短间隙序列上表现更优，O-ES分别为0.964和0.812，而在较长序列上性能相近。TGR在各类分词器下减少了26.3%至38.3%的生成token数量。这些结果支持将可重用输出结构作为小规模壳模拟器的实际设计选择，提升了响应保真度，缩短了生成目标，同时保持模板选择与残差预测对响应质量的核心作用。

LegalEvolver: Agentic Legal Judgment Prediction via Rubric-Guided Self-Evolution
LegalEvolver：基于规则引导自演化的智能体法律判决预测
As LLM-based agents develop, they have been increasingly applied in the legal domain. Legal Judgment Prediction~(LJP) requires models to conduct multi-step reasoning and external legal evidence retrieval. Existing legal search agents' performance is often constrained by the quality of retrieval queries. They often formulate queries directly from the initial user request, overlooking latent legal issues and legally salient details. To address this issue, we propose \emph{LegalEvolver}, a query optimization framework specifically designed to generate legally informative queries for LJP-oriented retrieval. Sampling high-quality optimized queries and their corresponding search trajectories is challenging, as relying solely on unguided high-temperature rollouts is both inefficient and constrained by the model's initial capabilities. We therefore heuristically optimize queries as seeds, improving the efficiency of trajectory generation. Furthermore, we summarize rollout experience into rubrics and use them to guide multi-round iterative self-evolution. Iteratively performing rubric optimization and rubric-guided RL enables the model to surpass its initial performance ceiling. End-to-end performance on extensive benchmarks demonstrates the effectiveness of query optimization on LJP. Comparisons with direct SFT followed by RL demonstrate the effectiveness of rubric guidance. Evolving rubrics further outperform fixed rubrics, confirming the benefits of self-evolution.
中文翻译
随着基于大语言模型的智能体不断发展，其在法律领域的应用日益广泛。法律判决预测（LJP）要求模型进行多步推理和外部法律证据检索。现有法律搜索智能体的表现常受限于检索查询的质量。它们通常直接从初始用户请求生成查询，忽视潜在的法律问题和具有法律意义的细节。为解决此问题，我们提出 extit{LegalEvolver}，一种专为LJP导向检索设计的查询优化框架，旨在生成更具法律信息量的查询。采样高质量优化查询及其对应搜索轨迹具有挑战性，因为仅依赖无引导的高温度滚动推演既低效又受限于模型初始能力。因此，我们通过启发式方法优化查询作为种子，提升了轨迹生成效率。此外，我们将滚动经验总结为规则，并用以指导多轮迭代式自我演化。通过反复执行规则优化和规则引导的强化学习，模型突破了初始性能上限。在广泛基准上的端到端表现证明了查询优化在LJP中的有效性。与直接进行SFT后接RL的对比显示规则引导的有效性。动态演化的规则进一步优于固定规则，证实了自我演化的优势。
👤 Xinyu Yang, Jiejun Tan, Chenlong Deng, Chenghao Zhang, Tongyu Wen et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Does Context Compression Preserve Refusal Alignment?
上下文压缩是否保持拒绝对齐？
Context compression reduces inference cost by encoding inputs into compact representations while preserving semantic content. An open question is whether semantic preservation alone is sufficient to maintain downstream behaviours such as refusal alignment. We investigate this question and find that encoder-based compression systematically weakens refusal behaviour in instruction-tuned language models, despite high reconstruction fidelity. This effect persists across model families and compression architectures. Mechanistic analysis shows that compression attenuates activation along the decoder's learned refusal direction. We further explore Memory Steering, a lightweight inference-time intervention that restores refusal rates to near-baseline levels without retraining and operates entirely in compressed representation space. These results demonstrate that semantic preservation does not guarantee behavioural preservation under compression, highlighting the need to explicitly preserve alignment-relevant features in compression-aware systems.
中文翻译

Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models
面向基于MoE的多模态大语言模型的模态感知专家剪枝
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to scale capacity efficiently, but expert parameters dominate memory even though only a few experts are activated per token: they account for over 90% of model size in modern MoE-MLLMs (e.g., 54 GB out of about 58 GB in BF16 for InternVL3.5-30B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B). Expert pruning has emerged as an effective compression strategy, but existing methods aggregate importance uniformly across tokens, ignoring that the same expert may serve different roles for vision versus language processing. To address this, we propose MAEP (Modality-Aware Expert Pruning), a training-free framework that decomposes expert importance by modality in a single forward pass. MAEP introduces (1) Delta-H Output ($ΔH$), a redistribution-aware metric measuring the MoE output change when an expert is removed, and (2) low-attention vision tokens as a vision-side pruning signal that is dataset-stable: its expert-importance ranking remains stable across different datasets. Text-side importance serves as a preservation signal while vision-side importance serves as a pruning signal, and experts are ranked globally across layers. Experiments on three MoE-based MLLMs across multimodal and text benchmarks show that MAEP outperforms modality-agnostic baselines on the majority of multimodal benchmarks while remaining competitive on text-only tasks, reducing the BF16 expert weight footprint by about 27 GB (about 47% of total memory) so that the full weight footprint of an approximately 58 GB model fits within a single 40 GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP.
中文翻译
混合专家（MoE）使多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够高效扩展容量，但专家参数占据主导内存，尽管每个token仅激活少数专家：在现代MoE-MLLMs中，专家参数占比超过90%（例如，在BF16下，InternVL3.5-30B-A3B和Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B约54GB/58GB）。专家剪枝已成为一种有效的压缩策略，但现有方法在token间统一聚合重要性，忽略了同一专家在视觉与语言处理中可能扮演不同角色。为此，我们提出MAEP（模态感知专家剪枝），一种无需训练的框架，可在单次前向传播中按模态分解专家重要性。MAEP引入（1）ΔH输出（Delta-H Output），一种考虑重分配的度量，用于衡量移除专家后MoE输出的变化；（2）低注意力视觉token作为视觉侧的剪枝信号，具有数据集稳定性：其专家重要性排序在不同数据集间保持一致。文本侧重要性作为保留信号，视觉侧重要性作为剪枝信号，专家在各层全局排序。在三个基于MoE的MLLMs上，跨多模态和文本基准的实验表明，MAEP在多数多模态基准上优于忽略模态的基线，同时在纯文本任务上保持竞争力，将BF16专家权重占用减少约27GB（约占总内存的47%），使得约58GB模型的完整权重可放入单个40GB GPU中。代码已公开于https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP。

Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models
推理路径：在MoE模型中读取与重塑测试时计算
Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.
中文翻译
推理模型将测试时计算分配给不同的行为，如规划、推导和验证。然而，模型经常“过度思考”，在找到正确答案后仍持续验证和重新推导，这浪费了昂贵的计算资源，甚至可能降低性能。现有干预手段如token预算或引导主要调节推理时长，而非针对特定行为。本文展示，混合专家（MoE）路由器为行为控制提供了自然且无需训练的接口。我们首先将推理轨迹拆分为句子级片段，并为每个片段标注六种推理行为之一（规划、执行、重述、事实、反思、结论），使每个token携带其片段的行为标签。对路由器logits的线性探测器预测该标签的准确率几乎与对残差流（模型完整隐藏状态）的探测器相当，且仅使用约一半特征。这些信号具有泛化能力：在一个数据集上与某种推理行为相关的专家，能在其他数据集上预测相同行为，涵盖五个领域（数学、代码、科学、知识、常识）的六个数据集。在此基础上，我们在推理期间选择性编辑专家路由器分数以抑制过度思考。在四个模型和四个基准上的实验显示，该干预使生成token数中位数减少30.4%（最高达63.3%）。关键的是，它促使模型在问题解决后停止，而非跳过必要推理步骤。准确率从未下降超过0.5个百分点，且常有提升。最终，MoE路由既提供了理解推理状态的可解释视角，也提供了一种轻量级优化测试时计算的机制。

The Male Abstraction Bias in Internet Culture and Large Language Models
互联网文化与大语言模型中的男性抽象偏见
Two foundational aspects of human cognition, abstractness and gender, are typically examined as separable. Here, we find they are systematically linked throughout the entire space of concepts spanning single (N = 40,000) and multi-word expressions (N = 67,000): across billions of words and millions of images on the internet -- and across numerous measurement techniques -- more abstract concepts are more associated with men, an effect which replicates in languages around the world. This pattern not only holds across conceptual domains, but it also maps onto grammatical structure: verbs and adverbs are more abstract and more male-coded, a system-wide trace of stereotypes accentuating agentic qualities in men. Furthermore, this bias emerges in human and AI responses in free association tasks, indicating its internalization and its algorithmic reproduction. The male abstraction bias may underlie and unify a range of stereotypes, evidencing gender as a core organizing dimension in conceptual space.

Grounded but Not Immune: Confirmation Bias in Agent-as-a-judge
有根基但非免疫：代理作为裁判中的确认偏误
Evaluating open-ended agent tasks increasingly falls to an Agent-as-a-Judge: an evaluator handed tools and the agent's full trajectory so it can verify a result against the environment. The premise is that a judge that gathers its own evidence should be grounded by it. We show that this grounding does not immunize the judge against confirmation bias: a single false pre-screening note, attributed to an authority and carrying no evidential bearing on the correct verdict, overturns up to 40% of a tool-using judge's correct verdicts across four judges and five datasets, even though the judge collects decisive evidence. The shift is driven by specific properties of the cue. We treat this cue as a controlled diagnostic of confirmation bias rather than an adversarial attack. Our central contribution is to explain how confirmation bias changes the judge's behavior in a trajectory. We classify changes in judge behavior and attribute changes to one of three failure modes: over-trust (verify, conclude correctly, then defer), hallucination (fabricate support for the flip), and under-investigation (stop at the first corroborating signal). For the two Claude judges, the failure is predominantly over-trust (67-75% of harmful flips), while smaller judges have error modes spread across all three. Lightweight mitigations blunt but never remove the bias.

开放式代理任务的评估越来越多地交由“代理作为裁判”机制：裁判被赋予工具和代理的完整轨迹，以验证结果是否符合环境。其前提是，通过自主收集证据的裁判应具有真实性。我们发现，这种“根基性”并不能使裁判免于确认偏误：一个仅具象征意义的虚假预筛选备注（来自权威来源，无实际证据支持），即可在四个裁判和五个数据集上导致高达40%的正确判决被推翻，即使裁判已收集到决定性证据。该影响由线索的特定属性驱动。我们将此线索视为对确认偏误的受控诊断，而非对抗性攻击。我们的核心贡献在于解释确认偏误如何改变裁判在轨迹中的行为。我们分类了裁判行为的变化，并将其归因于三种失败模式：过度信任（验证后正确得出结论，随后盲从）、幻觉（虚构支持反转的证据）和调查不足（在首个佐证信号处停止）。对于两个Claude裁判，主要失败模式为过度信任（67–75%的有害反转）；而较小的裁判则三种错误模式分布较均。轻量级缓解措施虽能削弱偏误，但无法彻底消除。

SlackBench: Benchmarking Agents on Collaborative Projects Grounded in Real Code Repositories
SlackBench：基于真实代码仓库协作项目的智能体基准测试
AI Agents are becoming digital coworkers that can browse the web, write code, run experiments, and assist humans over extended horizons. Yet existing benchmarks fall short of real knowledge work: many rely on synthetic data or isolated tasks, missing the complex, multi-party dynamics of collaborative projects, where critical information is scattered across conversations, code, and evolving decisions. Even simple workplace questions may require locating relevant channels and direct messages, recovering context from long discussions, inspecting repository state, reconciling stale or conflicting evidence, and synthesizing across sources. We introduce SlackBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on realistic research and engineering workflows that require joint reasoning over workplace communication and evolving repository state. Grounded in privacy-preserving reenactments of real academic research projects, SlackBench provides reproducible environments with realistic multi-party dialogue, evolving decisions, and corresponding code changes grounded in GitHub repositories and Google Docs. It contains 140 queries spanning heterogeneous-source agentic search, abstention on unanswerable or false-premise questions, goal-directed summarization, and circumstance inference over long conversations. These tasks are not reducible to static corpus question answering: agents must determine which conversations matter, traverse threads, inspect repository state, and synthesize evidence across communication and code artifacts. Across 33 frontier models and agent harnesses, the best system scores around 80% and most agents score below 66%, highlighting substantial headroom for improvement. SlackBench thus provides an extensible framework for evaluating agents in collaborative work environments. The SlackBench dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench .

AI智能体正成为能够浏览网页、编写代码、运行实验并在长时间跨度内协助人类的数字同事。然而，现有基准测试难以反映真实知识工作：许多依赖合成数据或孤立任务，忽略了协作项目中复杂的多方互动，关键信息分散在对话、代码和不断演化的决策中。即使是简单的职场问题，也可能需要定位相关频道和私信、从长篇讨论中恢复上下文、检查仓库状态、协调过时或冲突的信息，并整合多源证据。我们提出SlackBench，一个用于评估智能体在真实科研与工程工作流中表现的基准，要求智能体对工作场所沟通和动态仓库状态进行联合推理。该基准基于隐私保护的现实学术研究项目重现，提供可复现的环境，包含真实的多方对话、动态决策以及与GitHub仓库和Google Docs对应的代码变更。它包含140个查询，涵盖异源智能体搜索、对无法回答或前提错误问题的回避、目标导向摘要，以及对长对话的情境推断。这些任务无法简化为静态语料问答：智能体必须判断哪些对话重要、遍历线程、检查仓库状态，并综合通信与代码成果中的证据。在33个前沿模型和智能体框架中，最佳系统得分约为80%，大多数智能体得分低于66%，表明仍有巨大提升空间。SlackBench为评估协作工作环境中智能体提供了可扩展的框架。SlackBench数据集可在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench 获取。

Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning
基于元智能体监督的挑战感知知识召回在医疗多智能体推理中的应用
Large language models perform strongly on standard medical QA but degrade on harder reasoning tasks, where multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-model prompting. We argue that a key limitation is that existing systems select experts and retrieve knowledge from the question alone, rather than from gaps that emerge during reasoning. We propose challenge-aware adaptive recruitment: an external CHALLENGER inspects the evolving reasoning trace and identifies specific factual or expertise gaps. These signals trigger targeted knowledge retrieval or mid-discussion specialist recruitment. On the MEDAGENTSBENCH hard subset (862 questions across nine biomedical datasets), upfront retrieval provides little benefit, while challenge-aware in-between retrieval performs better; on a multi-specialist backbone it improves accuracy on 8 of 9 datasets and adds 6 percentage points over Challenger Only. Ablations indicate benefits from both retrieval timing and gap-specific query signals, and a live web-search experiment shows that the timing advantage persists across retrieval backends. For specialist recruitment, self-triggered adaptation is unreliable, whereas Challenger-triggered recruitment raises GPT-4.1-mini MedQA accuracy from 46% to 54%, with positive direction on all tested generalization datasets. Naively combining retrieval and specialist recruitment degrades performance, highlighting the need to coordinate adaptive intervention channels. These results show that when and why external resources are recruited is a key design choice in multi-agent medical reasoning.

Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents
智能体Harness意识：将经验证据转化为工具使用智能体的操作控制
Tool descriptions tell an agent what an action can do, but they do not necessarily reveal whether that action is healthy or suitable at the moment of use. Execution harnesses often possess this information through monitors, traces, and recent failures while the model choosing the next action does not. We call this gap operational blindness. We introduce Agent Harness Awareness, a trust-separated design in which the host supplies a small, fresh, verified state snapshot before a consequential action while retaining measurement, enforcement, evaluation, and final dispatch authority. We test its tools-and-network instance with ToolRoute, a controlled isolation benchmark that holds the task and candidate semantics constant and varies the operational information available to the model. In a 216-decision ablation across GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Sonnet 5, and Gemini 3.7 Flash, verified tool-health state reduced mean observable policy regret by 96.9% against a same-shape neutral control (4,252 ms to 131 ms) and raised single-dispatch completion from 69.4% to 95.8%. In a separate 27-decision end-to-end replication with live local TCP/HTTP execution, OpenTelemetry client spans, and Toxiproxy-injected faults, verified state reduced mean regret from 8,956 ms to 0 ms (zero excess observable operational cost) and completed the live action in 9/9 model-by-fault cases versus 5/9 for the neutral control. These studies establish the tools-and-network instance of Agent Harness Awareness: selected host telemetry materially improves pre-dispatch operational choice. Runtime state, hardware pressure, economics, retry-aware recovery, and heterogeneous selection remain open empirical extensions of the same architecture.
中文翻译
工具描述告诉智能体某个操作能做什么，但并未揭示该操作在执行时刻是否健康或合适。执行Harness通常通过监控、追踪和近期失败记录掌握此类信息，而负责选择下一步动作的模型却无法获取。我们称这种差距为操作盲区。我们提出Agent Harness Awareness，一种信任分离的设计：在关键操作前，主机提供一份小型、新鲜、经验证的状态快照，同时保留度量、执行、评估和最终调度的权威。我们在ToolRoute（一个受控隔离基准）中测试其工具与网络实例，保持任务和候选语义不变，仅改变模型可获得的操作信息。在GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Sonnet 5和Gemini 3.7 Flash的216次决策消融实验中，经验证的工具健康状态使平均可观测策略遗憾降低96.9%（从4,252毫秒降至131毫秒），单次调度完成率从69.4%提升至95.8%。在另一项27次决策的端到端复现实验中，包含实时本地TCP/HTTP执行、OpenTelemetry客户端跨度及Toxiproxy注入故障，经验证状态使平均遗憾从8,956毫秒降至0毫秒（零额外可观测操作成本），并在9个模型-故障组合中全部成功完成操作，而中性对照组仅完成5/9。这些研究确立了Agent Harness Awareness在工具与网络场景下的有效性：选定的主机遥测显著改善了调度前的操作决策。运行时状态、硬件压力、经济成本、重试感知恢复及异构选择仍为该架构的开放性实证扩展方向。

SpecCompact: Accelerating LLM Agent Context Compaction with Slot-Parallel Semantic Speculative Verification
SpecCompact：通过槽并行语义推测验证加速大语言模型智能体的上下文压缩
LLM agents use context compaction to manage growing interaction histories, but summarizing long histories with large models can introduce substantial delays. Compaction therefore requires balancing information retention against latency. We observe that structured summary slots can retain information when generated independently. Based on this observation, we present SpecCompact, a framework that accelerates compaction through slot-parallel semantic speculation. SpecCompact fine-tunes a small drafter and generates slots in parallel. The target model verifies each proposed line and repairs rejected content, while subsequent drafting uses the updated slot prefix. System optimizations further reduce repeated context processing and model calls. Across AppWorld and $τ^3$ benchmarks with two target models, SpecCompact achieves $2.04$--$5.63\times$ compaction speedups over Hermes-style summarization. Separate downstream evaluations reveal model- and task-dependent performance trade-offs. A user study within Hermes further shows an $8.68\times$ median compaction speedup.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体使用上下文压缩来管理不断增长的交互历史，但使用大型模型对长历史进行摘要会引入显著延迟。因此，压缩需要在信息保留与延迟之间取得平衡。我们观察到，独立生成的结构化摘要槽能够保留信息。基于此观察，我们提出SpecCompact框架，通过槽并行语义推测加速压缩过程。SpecCompact微调了一个小型草稿模型，并并行生成各个槽。目标模型验证每个提议的条目并修复被拒绝的内容，后续草稿则使用更新后的槽前缀。系统优化进一步减少了重复的上下文处理和模型调用。在AppWorld和$τ^3$基准测试中，使用两个目标模型，SpecCompact相比Hermes风格摘要实现了2.04至5.63倍的压缩速度提升。独立的下游评估揭示了模型和任务相关的性能权衡。在Hermes中的用户研究进一步显示，压缩速度中位数提升了8.68倍。

[2611.06835]

LooM: Loop-Invariant Memory for Causal Encoder-Decoder Language Models
LooM：因果编码器-解码器语言模型中的循环不变记忆
Looped Transformers increase effective depth by re-executing shared layers, but three limitations hinder their adoption: memory grows with the loop count, compute can only be adjusted in whole-block steps, and the loop count is hard to change after training. We propose LooM (Loop-invariant Memory), which addresses each in turn. In a causal encoder-decoder (CED), LooM loops only the decoder layers after the first, which read a global memory written once per token, and each of them publishes the key-value (KV) entry for a token only on its first visit, so the cache does not grow with the loop count. These layers loop independently, providing every budget from 3 to 21 layer executions per token, and one model trained on randomly sampled budgets covers all of them without a router. With enough loops, LooM outperforms a single-pass baseline trained on the same data on language modeling, zero-shot accuracy and long-context perplexity, while using the same cache size. Our analysis further shows that the training budget distribution decides what extra depth buys. These results suggest that a CED is a natural place to loop: memory is written once, and depth becomes a budget chosen at deployment.
中文翻译
循环Transformer通过重复执行共享层来增加有效深度，但存在三个限制阻碍其采用：内存随循环次数增长，计算只能以整块步长调整，且训练后难以更改循环次数。我们提出LooM（循环不变记忆），逐一解决这些问题。在因果编码器-解码器（CED）中，LooM仅对第一个之后的解码器层进行循环，这些层读取每标记写入一次的全局记忆，且每个层仅在其首次访问时发布某个标记的关键值（KV）条目，因此缓存不会随循环次数增长。这些层独立循环，可实现每标记3至21次层执行的任意预算，一个在随机采样的预算上训练的模型即可覆盖全部情况，无需路由器。在足够多循环的情况下，LooM在语言建模、零样本准确率和长上下文困惑度方面优于在同一数据上训练的单次通过基线，同时使用相同的缓存大小。我们的分析进一步表明，训练预算分布决定了额外深度带来的收益。这些结果表明，CED是自然适合循环的架构：记忆仅写入一次，深度成为部署时选择的预算。
👤 Lele Yang, Muxi Diao, Zhonghao Yan, Jiankang Chen, Bin Wen et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Reasoning over Non-Parametric Memory in Multilingual LLMs: A Cognitive-Based Analysis of Relational Knowledge
多语言LLM中非参数记忆的推理：关系知识的认知基础分析
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) assumes that language models can reason over relations stated in their context rather than fall back on associations memorized during pretraining. We study this evidence-conditioned relational reasoning in isolation, with the facts supplied directly in the prompt rather than retrieved. \textsc{Rel-Bloom} is a controlled multilingual diagnostic of 450 multiple-choice questions built from 30 Wikidata relations; the same source relations are instantiated at five Bloom-inspired levels (Remember, Understand, Apply, Analyze, Evaluate) and under three veracity conditions (Factual, Counterfactual, Neutralized). Across 43 models, six languages, and two multilingual settings (226,800 predictions), three findings stand out. First, accuracy drops sharply from Apply to Analyze in every evaluated open-weight model series, reaching 47.2 percentage points for the Large Granite bucket, but not for either Gemini model. Second, Evaluate items remain a bottleneck even for models near ceiling on the other task types: Gemini~3.1 Pro averages 99.5\% on the other four levels but 60.7\% on Evaluate. Third, item- and relation-level difficulty rankings are consistently preserved across languages, indicating a difficulty structure partly shared across languages within this benchmark. Across knowledge conditions, Counterfactual contexts are generally harder than Factual ones, while removing entity identities can improve higher-level performance in some model regimes. Greater model capability therefore does not by itself ensure reliable reasoning over supplied relational evidence. Relevant resources are available at: https://huggingface.co/spaces/QCRI/non-parametric-memory-relational-reasoning.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）假设语言模型能够基于上下文中的关系进行推理，而非依赖预训练期间记忆的关联。我们独立研究这一基于证据的关系推理能力，将事实直接提供在提示中而非通过检索获取。 extsc{Rel-Bloom}是一个受控的多语言诊断数据集，包含450道多项选择题，源自30个Wikidata关系；相同来源关系在五个Bloom启发式层级（记忆、理解、应用、分析、评估）下实例化，并在三种真实性条件下（事实型、反事实型、中性化）呈现。在43个模型、六种语言及两种多语言设置（共226,800次预测）中，三个发现尤为突出。第一，在所有评估的开放权重模型系列中，准确率从“应用”到“分析”阶段急剧下降，最大降幅达47.2个百分点（大型Granite桶），但Gemini模型未出现此现象。第二，即使在其他任务类型接近天花板的模型中，“评估”类题目仍是瓶颈：Gemini~3.1 Pro在其他四个层级平均准确率达99.5%，但在“评估”层级仅为60.7%。第三，项目与关系层面的难度排名在各语言间保持一致，表明该基准中存在部分跨语言共享的难度结构。在不同知识条件下，反事实情境普遍比事实情境更难，而移除实体身份在某些模型范式下反而提升了高级别表现。因此，更强的模型能力本身并不能保证可靠地基于所提供关系证据进行推理。相关资源可在以下地址获取：https://huggingface.co/spaces/QCRI/non-parametric-memory-relational-reasoning。
👤 Zohreh Abbasi Liasi, Batoul Hammoud, Anas Madkoor, Ehsaneddin Asgari 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

When Do Summaries Become Instructions? Unsupported Policy in LLM Agents' Self-Written Handoff Summaries
摘要何时变成指令？LLM代理自写交接摘要中的不当策略
Long-running LLM agents compact their context, replacing most history with a summary. We ask whether handoff summaries state permissions or requirements the record does not support, whether such sentences change what the next instance (the successor) does, and where they come from. Synthetic tool-use tasks carry a ground-truth permission ledger; same-snapshot replay reruns the successor with those whole sentences replaced by neutral filler. Two AI readers, reading only rule-flagged held-out summaries, confirmed unsupported restrictions in 1.2% and 2.4% of all readable summaries from two open-weight models, and unsupported authorizations in 0.1% and 1.3%. For restrictions, a pre-specified replay test was not significant (p = 0.58); a follow-up, planned after it, found a fragile effect (p = 0.027). Neutralizing false approvals cut unauthorized actions from 24 to 7 of 120 held-out runs (post hoc; later set: 17 to 11 of 48). Most development restrictions followed the agent's earlier reasoning that an action needs approval (post hoc). In pre-specified tests, replacing the system prompt's "Some actions need approval." with one saying where requirements come from cut unsupported restrictions from 5.0% to 1.8% of development summaries and, on held-out templates, from 1.8% to 0.3% (both p < 10^-9), while requests for approvals the ledger requires barely changed. Code and data: https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit
中文翻译
长期运行的LLM代理会压缩其上下文，用摘要替代大部分历史记录。我们探究交接摘要中是否存在未被原始记录支持的权限或要求声明，这些句子是否影响后续代理（继承者）的行为，以及它们的来源。在合成工具使用任务中设有真实权限账本；通过同快照重放实验，将继承者的完整句子替换为中性填充物。两名AI阅读器仅阅读规则标记的保留摘要，确认在两个开源模型的可读摘要中分别存在1.2%和2.4%的不支持限制，以及0.1%和1.3%的不支持授权。对于限制项，预设重放测试无显著差异（p = 0.58）；后续计划测试发现脆弱效应（p = 0.027）。中和虚假批准后，未经授权行为从120次中的24次降至7次（事后检验；后期组：17次降至11次）。大多数开发阶段的限制源于代理早期推理认为某行为需审批（事后分析）。在预设测试中，将系统提示中的“某些操作需要审批”替换为说明要求来源的提示，使开发摘要中不支持的限制从5.0%降至1.8%，在保留模板中从1.8%降至0.3%（均p < 10^-9），而账本要求的审批请求几乎不变。代码与数据见：https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit

CRESiST: Self-Enhancing Explorative Policy Learning for Simultaneous Speech Translation
CRESiST：面向同步语音翻译的自增强探索性策略学习
Simultaneous Speech Translation (SimulST) requires balancing translation quality and latency. While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in offline translation, current LLM-based SimulST systems predominantly rely on prefix-incremental generation. This paradigm suffers from redundant Key-Value cache recomputation and necessitates external Voice Activity Detection to process unbounded streams. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose an end-to-end interleaved read-write framework built upon an omni-modal LLM. By interleaving speech chunks and text tokens, our architecture achieves full KV cache reuse, natively supporting segmentation-free, infinite-length inference. Furthermore, to learn optimal streaming policies without rigid offline alignments, we introduce a novel Actor-Critic self-enhancement pipeline. An RNN-T Actor dynamically samples read-write trajectories, while an LLM-based Critic evaluates these paths via intrinsic semantic and latency rewards, distilling the optimal policy back to the Actor. Extensive experiments on sentence-level (FLEURS, CoVoST2) and document-level (MuST-C, ACL 60/60) benchmarks demonstrate that our model establishes new state-of-the-art quality-latency Pareto frontiers, substantially outperforming existing cascaded and static-alignment baselines.
中文翻译
同步语音翻译（SimulST）需要在翻译质量与延迟之间取得平衡。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在离线翻译中表现出色，但当前基于LLM的SimulST系统主要依赖前缀增量生成。这种范式存在冗余的键值缓存重新计算问题，并且需要外部语音活动检测来处理无限流数据。为克服这些瓶颈，我们提出了一种基于全模态LLM的端到端交错读写框架。通过交错语音片段和文本标记，我们的架构实现了完整的KV缓存复用，原生支持无分段、无限长度推理。此外，为了在无需严格离线对齐的情况下学习最优流式策略，我们引入了一种新颖的演员-评论家自增强管道。RNN-T演员动态采样读写轨迹，而基于LLM的评论家通过内在语义和延迟奖励评估这些路径，并将最优策略回传给演员。在句子级（FLEURS、CoVoST2）和文档级（MuST-C、ACL 60/60）基准上的大量实验表明，我们的模型建立了新的质量-延迟帕累托前沿，显著优于现有的级联和静态对齐基线。

LibriSpeech-Real-QA: A Benchmark for Streaming Question Answering
LibriSpeech-Real-QA：面向流式问答的基准测试
Most speech large language models (SLMs) still process speech in an offline manner. They only start responding after they receive the complete speech segment. However, many real-world applications require streaming speech interaction, for which the audio continuously flows in and the machine is required to answer questions based on the so far received audio chunks. For example, a user may ask the system to continuously count how many times a person's name has been mentioned while they are speaking. We observe a lack of benchmarks and evaluation protocols for real-time speech interaction. Motivated by this, we propose LibriSpeech-Real-QA, a dataset and a benchmark for real-time speech interaction. We construct different types of real-time question-answer pairs from LibriSpeech to evaluate whether speech LLMs can identify target words in streaming audio. We evaluate several popular real-time commercial models and find that they still tend to respond only after the speech turn has finished. We demonstrate that we can enable a pretrained SLM to perform this task, where we explore several modeling designs and establish a solid baseline for future research.
中文翻译

ArcRAG: Agentic Compositional Retrieval over Knowledge Graphs
ArcRAG：知识图谱上的智能体组合式检索
Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) answers questions by retrieving over a knowledge graph built from a corpus. Yet retrieval strategies are designed around the structure their own construction pipeline produces, and whether they survive a change of construction is rarely tested. We study this cross-construction portability: whether one retrieval procedure, with no construction-specific schema, relation names, or retuning, stays effective when the graph is built differently. We introduce ArcRAG, which induces a query-local graph interface at inference time (the node labels, relation descriptors, and property keys reachable from the entities a question names), plans multi-hop traversals against it, and executes and repairs them as Cypher. We build each of the MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and TriviaQA corpora as a schema-guided graph (typed nodes, a closed relation set, source provenance) and as an open-information-extraction graph (untyped nodes, free-text relations), with the retriever's configuration fixed across them. We compare against a construction-agnostic Direct-Cypher agent, ablate each stage across retry and pass budgets on both graph families, and compare with results reported for prior GraphRAG systems, most of which design retrieval together with their own graph construction. Across the two constructions, we find that retrieval need not be co-designed with the graph: query-time interface induction keeps a construction-agnostic retriever effective when the construction changes, although construction still shapes absolute accuracy.
中文翻译
基于图的检索增强生成（GraphRAG）通过从语料库构建的知识图谱进行检索来回答问题。然而，检索策略通常围绕其自身构建流程产生的结构设计，很少测试其在构建方式改变时的适应性。我们研究了跨构建的可移植性：一种不依赖特定构建模式、关系名称或微调的检索过程，在图谱以不同方式构建时是否仍有效。我们提出ArcRAG，它在推理时动态诱导查询局部图接口（可从问题所命名实体访问的节点标签、关系描述符和属性键），规划多跳遍历，并以Cypher形式执行和修复。我们将MuSiQue、HotpotQA和TriviaQA语料库分别构建为模式引导图（带类型节点、封闭关系集、来源溯源）和开放信息抽取图（无类型节点、自由文本关系），并保持检索器配置一致。我们在两种图结构上对比了与构建无关的Direct-Cypher智能体，对各阶段在重试和传递预算下的表现进行消融分析，并与以往GraphRAG系统的报告结果比较，其中多数系统将检索与自身图谱构建联合设计。在两种构建方式下，我们发现检索无需与图谱共同设计：查询时接口诱导可使构建无关的检索器在构建变化时仍保持有效性，尽管构建方式仍影响绝对准确率。

EvoGuard: Mitigating Degradation in Online Skill Evolution
EvoGuard：缓解在线技能演化的退化问题
Skills let language agents reuse experience across tasks as natural-language documents. Recent work evolves skills from interaction, but existing methods have limitations when skills evolve online. Prior methods update a skill offline from a batch of trajectories collected over many tasks. Some then adopt the new skill without any check, which can degrade the skill over time. Others keep it only if it improves performance on a held-out validation set, which is too slow and costly to run after every update. We introduce EvoGuard, a pairwise skill selector that mitigates skill degradation at low latency and cost. We first run the agent iteratively to build pools of skills and score each skill on a private test set to obtain utility labels. We pair the skills and use rejection sampling to keep only pairs with a clear utility gap. The selector is trained on the logits of two label tokens to pick the better skill. At deployment it decides in a single forward pass, which is significantly faster than evaluation on the private test set. Compared with adopting every new skill, EvoGuard improves success by 11.0% on ALFWorld, 5.9% on ScienceWorld, and 2.7% on LiveCodeBench.
中文翻译
技能使语言代理能够跨任务复用经验，作为自然语言文档。近期工作尝试从交互中演化技能，但现有方法在在线技能演化时存在局限。先前方法从多个任务收集的轨迹批次中离线更新技能。一些方法在未验证的情况下直接采用新技能，可能导致技能随时间退化；另一些方法仅在性能提升于保留验证集时才保留新技能，但每次更新后运行该验证代价过高且耗时。我们提出EvoGuard，一种低延迟、低成本的成对技能选择机制，以缓解技能退化。我们首先迭代运行代理以构建技能池，并在私有测试集上评分以获得效用标签。然后将技能配对，使用拒绝采样仅保留效用差距明显的配对。选择器在两个标签token的logits上训练，以挑选更优技能。部署时，它仅需一次前向传播即可决策，远快于在私有测试集上评估。相比采纳每个新技能，EvoGuard在ALFWorld上成功率提升11.0%，ScienceWorld上提升5.9%，LiveCodeBench上提升2.7%。
👤 Yanli Wang, Zichen Bai, Tianyue Jiang, Kunming Shao, Junhao Shi et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Mass Producing Training Data with Surprising Side Effects
通过意外副作用大规模生产训练数据
Training data can have unexpected side effects on language model behavior outside the training domain: insecure code data can induce broad misalignment, and outdated bird names can induce a 19th-century persona. Understanding such generalization is hard because only dozens of isolated examples exist, each constructed by hand or discovered by accident. We propose automatically discovering such datasets with surprising side effects (DSSEs). We define three objectives for DSSEs: they should (1) elicit a target behavior, (2) read like ordinary post-training data, and (3) be surprising to human auditors reading the data. We operationalize each with LLM judges, and search for high-scoring datasets via evolution, reinforcement learning, and subliminal learning approaches. After re-scoring and filtering with held-out metrics, our pipeline produces a collection of 6,575 DSSEs across 8 behaviors, many of which generalize across model families and sizes. We find our automated metrics track ML researchers' judgments, and frontier models outperform humans at predicting side effects. These datasets form a testbed for studying generalization and lay the groundwork for predicting training side effects before model deployment. Explore them at https://dsse.transluce.org.
中文翻译
训练数据可能在训练域之外对语言模型行为产生意想不到的副作用：不安全的代码数据可能导致广泛错位，过时的鸟类名称则可能引发19世纪人物特征。理解这种泛化现象十分困难，因为仅有几十个孤立的案例存在，且每个案例都是手工构建或偶然发现的。我们提出一种自动发现具有意外副作用的数据集（DSSE）的方法。我们定义了DSSE的三个目标：（1）诱发目标行为，（2）读起来像普通的后训练数据，（3）对人类审计员而言是出人意料的。我们使用大语言模型裁判来实现这些目标，并通过进化、强化学习和潜意识学习方法搜索高分数据集。经过保留指标的重新评分与过滤，我们的流程生成了涵盖8种行为的6,575个DSSE，其中许多在不同模型家族和规模间具有泛化能力。我们发现自动化度量能有效追踪机器学习研究人员的判断，前沿模型在预测副作用方面优于人类。这些数据集构成研究泛化的试验场，并为模型部署前预测训练副作用奠定了基础。可在 https://dsse.transluce.org 查看。
👤 Yaowen Ye, Jacob Steinhardt, Sarah Schwettmann, Cassidy Laidlaw 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Loop Before You Leap: What Recurrent Depth Can and Cannot Buy in Data-Limited Pretraining
先循环再跃进：在数据受限预训练中，递归深度能带来什么，又不能带来什么
Multimodal models have often scored below text-only baselines in the BabyLM Challenge (Klerings et al., 2024; Takmaz et al., 2025). We test whether additional recurrent computation helps close this gap. Recurrence increases computation by reusing layers without adding parameters or training data. Using a single implementation, we compare standard and looped Transformers under a 100M-word budget, with three seeds per main condition. Each image is represented by one feature vector from a frozen DINOv2 encoder, used as a prefix token. Caption words count toward the budget, so multimodal training also changes the language data. In a pre-registered text-only comparison, a 12-layer stack repeated four times exceeds a standard 48-layer Transformer by 5.4 BLiMP percentage points at matched inference compute. However, multimodal training lowers BLiMP accuracy by about 3.8 points in both architectures, with no clear mitigation from recurrence. An exploratory loop-side no-image control, trained with the multimodal language data and batching schedule, scores 5.7 points below the text-only baseline. Adding the visual prefix raises accuracy by 1.85 points on average, but this contrast is uncertain with three seeds. These results suggest that changes in language data and its presentation are a major source of the drop. Diagnostic tests show that the models use visual information; its contribution to grammatical generalization remains uncertain under this single-vector interface.
中文翻译
多模态模型在BabyLM挑战赛中经常低于纯文本基线表现（Klerings等，2024；Takmaz等，2025）。我们测试额外的递归计算是否有助于缩小这一差距。递归通过重用层增加计算量，而不增加参数或训练数据。使用单一实现，在1亿词预算下比较标准与循环Transformer，每种主要条件设置三个随机种子。每张图像由一个来自冻结DINOv2编码器的特征向量表示，作为前缀标记。标题词计入预算，因此多模态训练也改变了语言数据。在注册过的纯文本对比中，一个重复四次的12层堆栈在匹配推理计算量下比标准48层Transformer高出5.4个BLiMP百分点。然而，多模态训练在两种架构中均使BLiMP准确率下降约3.8分，递归未能明显缓解此问题。探索性对照实验（无图像循环侧）使用多模态语言数据和批处理调度进行训练，得分比纯文本基线低5.7分。加入视觉前缀平均提升准确率1.85分，但因三个种子导致该对比结果不确定。这些结果表明，语言数据及其呈现方式的变化是性能下降的主要原因。诊断测试显示模型确实使用了视觉信息；但在单向量接口下，其对语法泛化的贡献仍不确定。
👤 Daichi Hotta, Yohei Kobashi, Takeshi Kojima, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Editing Large Language Models with Geometry-Aware Regularization
基于几何感知正则化的大型语言模型编辑
Knowledge editing has become a key technique for updating knowledge in large language models. However, existing methods neglect the geometric structure of the knowledge representation space, leading to two key limitations: they cannot distinguish which pre-trained knowledge should be preferentially preserved (core vs. non-core) nor which new edits are inherently difficult (hard vs. easy). To address this, we propose GEdit, a geometry-aware framework built on a Low-Rank Plus Diagonal (LR+D) factor model that captures the intrinsic low-rank structure of Transformer FFN hidden representations. GEdit features two complementary mechanisms. First, the low-rank regularization applies targeted protection on a low-rank core knowledge subspace. Second, the adaptive regularization dynamically adjusts the regularization strength based on each edit request's editability: relaxing constraints for hard edits to ensure success, and tightening them for easy edits to better preserve pretrained knowledge. Extensive experiments on GPT2-XL, GPT-J, and LLaMA-3-8B under large-scale editing scenarios, including both mini-batch and fully sequential editing settings with up to 10,000 continuous updates, show that GEdit consistently and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both editing success and knowledge preservation.
中文翻译
知识编辑已成为更新大型语言模型知识的关键技术。然而，现有方法忽视了知识表示空间的几何结构，导致两个关键局限：无法区分应优先保留的预训练知识（核心 vs. 非核心），也无法识别哪些新编辑本质上较难（难 vs. 易）。为此，我们提出GEdit，一种基于低秩加对角（LR+D）因子模型的几何感知框架，能够捕捉Transformer前馈网络隐藏表示的内在低秩结构。GEdit包含两个互补机制：第一，低秩正则化对低秩核心知识子空间施加针对性保护；第二，自适应正则化根据每次编辑请求的可编辑性动态调整正则化强度：对困难编辑放松约束以确保成功，对简单编辑收紧约束以更好保留预训练知识。在GPT2-XL、GPT-J和LLaMA-3-8B上进行的大规模编辑场景实验（包括最多10,000次连续更新的迷你批次和全序列编辑设置）表明，GEdit在编辑成功率和知识保留方面持续显著优于当前最先进的基线方法。

ProcessPrefBench: Evaluating Cross-Task Transfer of Individual Process Preferences from Naturalistic Human-AI Interaction
ProcessPrefBench：评估自然人机交互中个体过程偏好在跨任务间的迁移能力
In natural human-AI dialogue, users' corrections, requests, and self-descriptions carry reusable signals about how they want work to be done. These signals reveal individual process preferences, user-specific standards for evidence, depth, analytical focus, procedure, and delivery. We ask whether a preference revealed in one dialogue can shape an assistant's later answer when the task does not restate it. ProcessPrefBench uses three months of natural dialogues from two knowledge workers to build 66 source-target cases separately judged for source evidence and target applicability. Sources are preselected, so the benchmark tests application rather than retrieval. We test three models with target-only, full-history, and distilled-preference prompts. Target-specific 0-2 rubrics score preference realization, and a task gate flags severe failures. Compared with target-only prompting, full-history and imperative distilled-preference prompting raise preference realization by 0.204 and 0.244 points on the 0-2 scale, respectively, with no reliable advantage from distillation. Fabricated-evidence failures rise from 5.8% under target-only prompting to 10.1% under full-history and 10.4% under imperative prompting. ProcessPrefBench highlights a tension in learning from dialogue, where drawing on prior interactions can improve preference realization yet also invite fabricated evidence. We hope it helps build assistants that honor a user's process preferences without sacrificing factual reliability.
中文翻译
在自然的人机对话中，用户的修正、请求和自我描述传递出关于工作方式的可复用信号。这些信号揭示了个体的过程偏好，即用户对证据、深度、分析重点、流程和交付方式的特定标准。我们探讨：在一个对话中揭示的偏好是否能在任务未重述的情况下影响助手后续的回答。ProcessPrefBench利用两位知识工作者三个月的自然对话构建了66个源-目标案例，分别评估源证据和目标适用性。源案例已预选，因此该基准测试侧重于应用而非检索。我们测试三种模型，分别采用仅目标提示、完整历史提示和提炼偏好提示。使用0-2分制的靶向评分衡量偏好实现程度，任务门控标志严重失败。相较于仅目标提示，完整历史提示和指令式提炼偏好提示分别使偏好实现度提高0.204和0.244分，但提炼优势并不显著。虚构证据错误率从仅目标提示下的5.8%上升至完整历史提示的10.1%和指令式提示的10.4%。ProcessPrefBench凸显了从对话中学习的张力：利用先前互动可提升偏好实现，但也可能引发虚构证据。我们希望该基准有助于构建既能尊重用户过程偏好又不牺牲事实可靠性的助手。

Confucius4-R2T2: A Stable-Prefix Streaming ASR Model for Real Real-Time Transcription
Confucius4-R2T2：一种用于实时转录的稳定前缀流式ASR模型
Streaming speech recognition is increasingly consumed by machines, such as translators and voice agents, that act on each token as it appears. A token that is later revised costs wasted work or a wrong action. In this work, we present Confucius4-R2T2 (short for Real Real-Time Transcription), a stable-prefix streaming ASR model that never modifies its earlier output while maintaining high accuracy and low latency. R2T2 is obtained by post-training Qwen3-ASR-1.7B on 1,500 hours of Chinese and English audio while freezing its pre-trained audio encoder and projector. The adaptation is based on the concept of Longest Stable Prefix (LSP), a new notion that we introduce in this work. We prove that LSP is monotone under the prefix order, consistent with the full-audio output, and maximal among labels that equal a whole earlier output. Learning from LSP labels converts a full-context model into a stable-prefix streaming one without any architectural change. Extensive evaluations show that R2T2 brings streaming recognition close to the base model's full-context quality while delivering stable text with low emission lag. Its gains extend beyond the post-training languages, demonstrating a practical path from full-context ASR to stable streaming. We open-source the model weights and code at https://huggingface.co/netease-youdao/Confucius4-R2T2 and https://github.com/netease-youdao/Confucius4-R2T2.
中文翻译
流式语音识别正被越来越多地应用于机器翻译和语音代理等系统中，这些系统会即时处理每个词元。若后续对词元进行修改，则会造成资源浪费或错误操作。本文提出Confucius4-R2T2（简称R2T2，即真实实时转录），一种稳定的前缀流式自动语音识别模型，在保持高精度和低延迟的同时，从不修改其早期输出。R2T2通过在1500小时的中英文音频上对Qwen3-ASR-1.7B进行后训练获得，同时冻结其预训练的音频编码器和投影器。该方法基于我们提出的“最长稳定前缀”（LSP）概念。我们证明了LSP在前缀序下具有单调性，与完整音频输出一致，并且是等于先前完整输出的所有标签中的最大者。从LSP标签学习可将全上下文模型转化为无需任何架构改动的稳定前缀流式模型。大量评估表明，R2T2使流式识别接近基础模型的全上下文质量，同时提供稳定文本并实现低发射延迟。其优势不仅限于后训练语言，展示了从全上下文ASR到稳定流式系统的实用路径。我们已在Hugging Face（https://huggingface.co/netease-youdao/Confucius4-R2T2）和GitHub（https://github.com/netease-youdao/Confucius4-R2T2）开源模型权重和代码。
