Selective, Risk-Controlled Emotion Forecasting in Conversation: From a Persistence Barrier to Departure Detection with Conformal Guarantees
对话中的选择性、风险可控情绪预测：从持久性障碍到脱离检测的共形保证
Forecasting emotion in conversation is increasingly framed as predicting the next emotional state's value. We show this targets a hard quantity in a state-observed testbed and propose a learnable, deployable reformulation instead. On valence-arousal-dominance trajectories from a dyadic corpus, repeating a speaker's own previous state sets a persistence baseline that no frozen-encoder correction meaningfully improves upon and a state-space filter exceeds; the floor requires the historical state to be observed, since an estimated anchor collapses persistence below a direct forecaster. What survives is the timing of a departure from persistence, learnable on three of four corpora and near chance on the highest-base-rate corpus, a negative result we report. We recast the task as selective departure forecasting: a lead-time-weighted metric with a leakage protocol, and commitment policies whose best choice switches with the departure rate. A dialogue-level conformal risk control bounds the expected loss of committed predictions, meeting the target in all twelve configurations tested, though the guarantee is bought through abstention, with coverage and alarm recall falling sharply on the hardest corpus. One tested language model's confidence fails as a ranking signal where another succeeds, a model-dependent failure not yet attributable to family versus scale. Code and result logs are available from the corresponding author upon request.
中文翻译
对话中的情绪预测日益被理解为预测下一个情感状态的值。我们指出，这种设定在状态可观测的测试环境中针对的是一个难以处理的量，并提出一种可学习、可部署的替代范式。基于双人对话语料库中的愉悦度-唤醒度-支配度轨迹，重复说话人自身先前状态构成一个持久性基准，任何固定编码器修正都无法显著改进，且状态空间滤波器也超越了这一基准；该基准要求历史状态必须被观测，因为估计锚点会使持久性低于直接预测器。真正可被捕捉的是脱离持久性的时机，该时机在四个语料库中的三个上可学习，但在最高基率语料库上接近随机水平，这是一个负面结果，我们予以报告。我们将任务重新定义为选择性脱离预测：一种带提前量加权的指标，包含泄漏协议和承诺策略，其最优选择随脱离率变化而改变。对话级共形风险控制对承诺预测的期望损失进行了边界约束，在所有十二种配置中均达到目标，尽管该保证是以放弃预测为代价换取的，覆盖度和警报召回率在最困难语料库上急剧下降。一种测试语言模型的置信度在某些场景下失效，而另一模型成功，这是一种模型相关的失败，尚未可归因于模型家族或规模差异。代码和结果日志可向通讯作者申请获取。

Information-Matched Auditing of Conversational Emotion-Shift Forecasting: A Leakage-Safe Evaluation Protocol
信息匹配的对话情绪转移预测审计：一种防泄露的评估协议
Comparisons in conversational emotion-shift forecasting are easily distorted when a learned model receives rich features but its transition baseline is given a noisy or unavailable current-emotion label, when hyperparameter budgets differ, or when target-turn information enters the representation. We present an information-matched, leakage-safe auditing protocol that closes these gaps with an explicit commitment point, executable causality tests, equal four-configuration validation searches, and seed-dialogue-aware inference. Applying it to six causal model families on four text corpora and two feature-source sensitivity configurations shows that a deployable train-only current-emotion classifier feeding the transition baseline yields a different verdict than an oracle diagnostic where both sides receive gold current emotion. Under the deployable condition, learned models improve over the predicted-label baseline on all four primary corpora; DailyDialog gains are large and survive a 72-comparison Holm correction (best ROC-AUC gain = +0.167, adjusted p=.0144). Under matched gold information, the baseline remains competitive on IEMOCAP and stronger on MELD, while every model retains a large DailyDialog advantage. We additionally report precision-recall AUC, calibration, a strictly utterance-local feature control, same-person self-shift forecasting, and a future-access dose-response test. An eight-item leakage checklist accompanies the protocol. Code, raw seed predictions, and reporting artifacts are available from the corresponding author upon request.

CARTBench: Rubric-Based Evaluation of Shopping Advice for Complex and Ambiguous Requests with Timelines
CARTBench：基于评分标准的复杂模糊请求与时间线购物建议评估
Large language model (LLM) shopping assistants now recommend products in multi-turn conversations. However, reliable evaluation faces Data scarcity and Difficulty Standardizing Criteria. To address these obstacles, we introduce CARTBench for CART (Complex and Ambiguous Requests with Timelines). For Data scarcity, we curate cases from real commercial shopping needs across 17 retail domains, pairing timestamped dialogue with 1,603 product records. For Difficulty Standardizing Criteria, we develop case-specific rubrics guided by Ideal Response Principles, distilled from excellent replies manually selected from successfully completed orders. These principles also guide Principle-Guided CART Prompting, our structured recommendation strategy. The experimental benchmark contains 1,328 expert-annotated criteria across Core, Excellence, and Safety. To answer the CART question, we evaluate 7 flagship commercial API models, including GPT-6.1-Sol and Claude-Opus-5, and 35B+ parameters open-weight models. In the first full-suite evaluation under the frozen rubrics, 3 of 10 models exceed 90% on Core, while none exceeds 45% on Excellence. Therefore, we identify an understanding-to-execution gap between required behavior and high-quality purchase advice. To examine these limitations, we diagnose shared failures in product evidence and unresolved needs, linked to changing requirements and decisive unknowns. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Da1yuqin/CARTBench.

TandemBench: Benchmarking Decision Models for Multi-Agent Collaboration
TandemBench：多智能体协作决策模型的基准测试
Decision models choose among predefined options in tasks such as routing and action selection. In multi-agent collaboration, a decision determines how members divide work, exchange resources, and act in parallel. We introduce TandemBench, a benchmark of 1,200 questions in multi-agent cooking scenarios where coordination choices affect goal completion. Models receive complete global information and candidate team plans, with reference answers established through execution and exact search. Models select an optimal team plan and, in paired variants that retain or remove it, judge whether better arrangements exist beyond the supplied options. We evaluate 40 native decision models and compare them with 10 general-purpose language models. The best native decision model achieves 62.50% decision accuracy. Native decision models remain close to chance in optimality verification. Paired tests reveal that some report better alternatives regardless of whether an optimal plan is already present. These findings show that team-plan decision accuracy alone can conceal systematic errors in judging whether the supplied options contain an optimal arrangement. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/lililibaohang/TandemBench.
中文翻译
决策模型在路由和动作选择等任务中从预定义选项中进行选择。在多智能体协作中，决策决定了成员如何分工、交换资源以及并行行动。我们引入 TandemBench，这是一个包含 1,200 个问题的基准，涉及多智能体烹饪场景，其中协调选择影响目标完成。模型接收完整的全局信息和候选团队计划，参考答案通过执行和精确搜索建立。模型需选择最优团队计划，并在成对变体中判断是否存在超越所提供选项的更好安排（保留或移除最优计划）。我们评估了 40 个原生决策模型，并与 10 个通用语言模型进行比较。最佳原生决策模型达到 62.50% 的决策准确率。原生决策模型在最优性验证方面仍接近随机水平。成对测试显示，某些模型无论是否已有最优计划，都会报告更好的替代方案。这些发现表明，仅关注团队计划决策准确率可能掩盖判断所提供选项是否包含最优安排的系统性错误。数据集和评估代码已公开于 https://github.com/lililibaohang/TandemBench。

Learning When Human Decisions Matter: Executor-Relative Consultation Value for LLM Agents
学习人类决策何时重要：面向 LLM 代理的执行者相对咨询价值
Large language models increasingly automate complex tasks, but human decision capacity does not scale with their capabilities. In many applications, humans remain essential for providing private context, authority, or judgments unavailable to the agent. We formulate human decision capacity allocation: given multiple consultation opportunities and a limited budget, at which decisions should an LLM agent consult a human? The central challenge is estimating consultation value, which is executor-dependent because the same human input may benefit different downstream agents differently. We introduce Consultation Value Distillation (CVD), which estimates consultation values offline using Monte Carlo executor rollouts and distills these values into lightweight predictors for budgeted allocation, requiring no rollout-based value estimation at deployment. Across five batch and three sequential settings with two frontier executors, CVD improves allocation through joint and continuation-aware value learning, while independent consultation values remain competitive in several settings. CVD also broadly outperforms direct prompting and Value of Information where applicable, remains robust with substantially less rollout supervision, and enables nearly lossless approximate allocation at low search cost.
中文翻译
大型语言模型日益自动化复杂任务，但人类决策能力并未随其能力同步扩展。在许多应用中，人类仍对提供私有上下文、权威或代理无法获取的判断至关重要。我们提出了人类决策能力分配问题：在多个咨询机会和有限预算下，LLM 代理应在哪些决策中咨询人类？核心挑战在于估计咨询价值，而该价值依赖于执行者，因为同一人类输入对不同下游代理的收益可能不同。我们引入咨询价值蒸馏（CVD），通过蒙特卡洛执行者回溯离线估计咨询价值，并将其蒸馏为轻量级预测器，用于预算分配，部署时无需基于回溯的价值估计。在五个批量和三个顺序设置中，使用两个前沿执行者，CVD 通过联合与延续感知的价值学习改进分配，而独立咨询价值在多个设置中仍具竞争力。CVD 在适用情况下广泛优于直接提示和信息价值方法，对回溯监督的需求显著减少，并能在极低搜索成本下实现近乎无损的近似分配。

Matching the Training-Time Weight Rule in Inference-Time Expert Deactivation
推理时专家禁用与训练时权重规则的匹配
Expert deactivation at inference time changes both token routing and expert weights, and its effect on performance depends on how these changes are implemented. We compare removal, freezing, remaining-expert renormalization, and unnormalized re-routing on identical disabled sets across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four model families, with a base OLMoE checkpoint as an additional comparison. We develop a weight-sum model that relates the loss of expert contributions and changes in branch scale to the weight rule used during training. With half of the experts deactivated according to activation-based rankings, remaining-expert renormalization yields the highest perplexity in models trained with raw router probabilities, whereas unnormalized re-routing yields the highest perplexity in models trained with normalized weights. Perplexity ranges from 1.4 to 73 times the intact baseline when the training rule is retained, and from 14 to 28,740 times the baseline under a rule mismatch. Task outcomes vary across evaluation types: on OLMoE, GSM8K strict-match accuracy falls to zero under all four implementations. Extending the model to changes in the number of active experts yields a further prediction, supported by the OLMoE experiments: the perplexity penalty from additional renormalization increases as fewer experts are activated.
中文翻译
推理时的专家禁用会同时改变令牌路由和专家权重，其对性能的影响取决于实现方式。我们在四个模型家族的五个指令微调检查点上，对移除、冻结、剩余专家重归一化和未归一化重新路由在相同禁用集上的表现进行了比较，并以一个基础的OLMoE检查点作为额外对照。我们提出了一个权重和模型，将专家贡献损失和分支尺度变化与训练期间使用的权重规则联系起来。当根据激活排名禁用一半专家时，在使用原始路由器概率训练的模型中，剩余专家重归一化产生的困惑度最高；而在使用归一化权重训练的模型中，未归一化重新路由产生的困惑度最高。当保留训练规则时，困惑度范围为基线的1.4至73倍；而规则不匹配时，困惑度范围高达基线的14至28,740倍。任务结果因评估类型而异：在OLMoE上，GSM8K严格匹配准确率在所有四种实现下均降至零。扩展模型以考虑活跃专家数量的变化，进一步预测出：随着激活的专家减少，额外重归一化的困惑度惩罚会增加。该发现得到了OLMoE实验的支持。
👤 Hao Cheng, Zhiyu Yang, Bo Ma, Hanzhang Jia 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Prospective Memory for Proactive LLM Agents
面向主动型大语言模型代理的前瞻性记忆
Most memory systems for LLM agents are retrospective, preserving and retrieving past information to inform current decisions. Proactive agents, however, also require prospective memory: retaining future-directed intentions and realizing them when appropriate opportunities arise, even without explicit requests. Crucially, remembering the right intention does not guarantee acting at the right time: a related observation may call for waiting, while a later cue may make the same intention actionable. Inspired by cognitive theories of human prospective memory, we introduce Pro-Memory, a memory framework that separates maintaining future intentions from deciding when and how to realize them. Pro-Memory preserves pending objectives linked to their supporting evidence, assesses new observations against their original goals and activation conditions, and coordinates appropriate contributions with the agent's ongoing actions. It further updates intention states based on actions actually issued or evidence of resolution, while an experience memory distills feedback from completed episodes into guidance for future decisions. Experiments on two benchmarks spanning delayed-intention tasks and open-ended proactive assistance demonstrate consistent improvements in proactive task performance over representative memory approaches across multiple LLM backbones, while limiting unnecessary interventions.
中文翻译
大多数大语言模型代理的记忆系统是回顾性的，用于保存和检索过去信息以支持当前决策。然而，主动型代理还需要前瞻性记忆：保留未来导向意图，并在适当机会出现时予以实现，即使没有明确请求。关键在于，记住正确的意图并不保证在正确时间行动：某个相关观察可能需要等待，而稍后的线索可能使同一意图变得可执行。受人类前瞻性记忆认知理论启发，我们提出Pro-Memory，一种将保持未来意图与决定何时及如何实现它们相分离的记忆框架。Pro-Memory 保留与支持证据相关的待办目标，根据原始目标和激活条件评估新观察，并协调与代理当前行动相适应的贡献。它还根据实际发出的动作或解决证据更新意图状态，同时经验记忆将已完成任务的反馈提炼为未来决策的指导。在涵盖延迟意图任务和开放式主动协助的两个基准测试上的实验表明，Pro-Memory 在多个大语言模型骨干架构上持续优于代表性记忆方法，同时有效减少了不必要的干预。

Cognitive Bias Signatures in Search-Equipped LLM Agents: A Stage-Resolved Analysis
配备搜索功能的大语言模型代理中的认知偏差特征：阶段解析分析
Search can help LLM agents correct mistakes, but their starting conditions may shape which evidence they find and use. Drawing on cognitive-bias paradigms, we develop a controlled search harness to examine result order, records of prior work, and initial hypotheses while holding each question and document collection fixed. Matched controls distinguish order effects from differences in document composition and the effects of failed work from context length. Across 10 models and 1,000 synthetic search worlds, we track queries, retrieved documents, document openings, and final answers. Our comparisons suggest that final answers are more sensitive to initial evidence order during active search than when all evidence is supplied in full. Dead-end histories can reduce strategy switching relative to matched neutral histories, indicating an effect beyond context length. Under wrong hypotheses, stage measurements distinguish reduced retrieval or opening from errors that persist after counter-evidence is opened. Exploratory interventions show that opening counter-evidence more often can coincide with lower accuracy, while accuracy can improve with little change in opening.
中文翻译
搜索可以帮助大语言模型代理纠正错误，但其初始条件可能影响其发现和使用哪些证据。借鉴认知偏差范式，我们开发了一个受控搜索工具，考察结果顺序、先前工作记录和初始假设，同时固定问题和文档集合。匹配对照组区分了顺序效应与文档构成差异，以及失败工作与上下文长度的影响。在10个模型和1000个合成搜索世界中，我们追踪查询、检索文档、文档打开情况和最终答案。我们的比较表明，最终答案在主动搜索过程中对初始证据顺序更为敏感，而非在所有证据完全提供时。死胡同历史记录相较于匹配的中性历史记录，会降低策略切换频率，表明其影响超出上下文长度。在错误假设下，阶段测量能够区分检索或打开不足与在反证据打开后仍持续存在的错误。探索性干预显示，更频繁地打开反证据有时会伴随准确率下降，而准确率可在打开行为变化不大的情况下提升。

RAGCompass: Generalizable Retriever Routing via Reranking
RAGCompass：通过重排序实现可泛化的检索器路由
Different retrievers exist for retrieval-augmented generation, yet none performs best for all queries. Therefore, the key question is: given a query and retrieved passages from different retrievers, which one is most suited for that query? Existing routers usually make predictions from query-specific features and a fixed pool of retrievers; such routers have no output for an unseen retriever and are incapable by design on a new corpus. To fill these gaps, RAGCompass reads the retrieved passage sets themselves: passages from an unfamiliar retriever or corpus stay directly comparable to other passages, so routing stays well designed where a query-only map would fail. We contrastively fine-tune a reranker against the answer each evidence set produces, which accounts for most of the gain; a small comparison head adds a moderate improvement, and neither component has retriever-specific parameters. RAGCompass improves answer F1 by 28% relative to the best fixed retriever, and outperforms fusion baselines, three query-only routers, and a prompted 8B language model. Generalization experiments show that RAGCompass keeps most of its gain under an unseen corpus, retriever, or reader, while query-only baselines fall to the best fixed retriever or below.
中文翻译
在检索增强生成中存在多种检索器，但没有一种在所有查询上表现最佳。因此关键问题是：给定一个查询和来自不同检索器的检索段落，哪一个最适用于该查询？现有的路由机制通常基于查询特定特征和固定的检索器池进行预测；这类路由机制对未见过的检索器无输出，且在新语料库上无法工作。为填补这些空白，RAGCompass直接读取检索到的段落集合本身：来自不熟悉检索器或语料库的段落仍可与其他段落直接比较，从而确保路由设计有效，而仅依赖查询的映射方法会失效。我们通过对比微调一个重排序器来对抗每个证据集产生的答案，这带来了大部分性能提升；一个小的比较头带来适度改进，且两个组件均无检索器特定参数。RAGCompass相比最佳固定检索器，使答案F1值相对提升了28%，优于融合基线、三种仅依赖查询的路由方法以及一个提示的8B语言模型。泛化实验表明，RAGCompass在未见语料库、检索器或阅读器情况下仍保持大部分优势，而仅依赖查询的基线则退化至最佳固定检索器或更低水平。
👤 Valentin Six, Mufei Li, Dongqi Fu, Yinglong Xia, Hong Li et al. 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv

Rewrite, Then Veto: Rewriter-Agnostic Edit Gating for LLM-Based Post-OCR Correction
先重写，再否决：面向基于大语言模型的后OCR校正的重写无关编辑门控
LLMs can repair noisy OCR transcripts of historical documents, but they also change text that was already correct. We propose EditVeto, a veto gate placed after a full-context LLM rewrite. The rewrite is character-aligned to the OCR input, its differences are split into blocks, and a 0.6B-parameter gate keeps or reverts each block. An oracle analysis motivates the design: given the true error locations, per-span generation is worse than no correction, whereas vetoing a full rewrite's edits helps. Trained on rewrites by two Qwen3 models and evaluated, frozen, on the eight HIPE-OCRepair-2026 test sets, the gate improves all five rewriters tested, two of them unseen in training: weighted cMER falls by 0.0026-0.0044 and the preference score rises by 0.15-0.25 (paired bootstrap, p ~ 0.0002 each). On published competition outputs it lowers cMER for three of four teams but raises it for the winning run (0.0050 to 0.0065). In a second, disclosed test look, a retrained gate still improves LoRA-tuned rewriters (cMER -0.0011 to -0.0021), and an ensemble of their gated edits reaches a cMER of 0.0069 and a preference score of 0.928, mostly through fine-tuning and pooling rather than gating. Built only on public models and data, it is behind the winner (0.0050) and tied with the second-ranked run (0.0071), both from the only team using domain pre-training, and ahead of all other published runs. A third, secondary look shows the unchanged gate improving zero-shot Qwen3.8-27B (cMER 0.0114 to 0.0108) but only the preference score of its LoRA-tuned version.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）可以修复历史文献中的噪声OCR转录文本，但也可能改变原本正确的文本内容。我们提出EditVeto，一种放置在完整上下文LLM重写之后的否决门控机制。重写与OCR输入字符对齐，差异被分割为块，一个0.6B参数的门控机制决定保留或撤销每个块。通过一个理想分析验证设计动机：已知真实错误位置时，逐跨度生成比不修正更差，而否决整个重写的修改则有帮助。该门控在两个Qwen3模型生成的重写数据上训练，并在八个HIPE-OCRepair-2026测试集上以冻结状态评估，结果表明其改善了所有五个测试重写器，其中两个在训练中未见：加权cMER下降0.0026–0.0044，偏好分数上升0.15–0.25（配对bootstrap检验，p≈0.0002）。在公开竞赛输出上，它降低了四个团队中三个的cMER，但使获胜方案的cMER从0.0050升至0.0065。在第二次公开测试中，重新训练的门控仍能改善LoRA微调的重写器（cMER下降0.0011至0.0021），其集成门控编辑达到cMER 0.0069和偏好分数0.928，主要通过微调和聚合实现而非门控。该系统仅基于公开模型和数据构建，虽落后于冠军（0.0050），但与第二名（0.0071）持平，两者均来自唯一使用领域预训练的团队，且优于所有其他公开结果。第三次次要测试显示，未更改的门控仍能改善零样本Qwen3.8-27B（cMER从0.0114降至0.0108），但仅提升其LoRA微调版本的偏好分数。

When to Ask, When to Answer: Strategic Clarification for Ambiguous Reference Resolution in Dialogue
何时提问，何时回答：对话中模糊指代消解的战略性澄清
For humans, referential disambiguation is a collaborative process involving clarification questions asked when they will meaningfully change the outcome. However, large language models (LLMs) have been shown to be biased toward premature answering, preferring definite single-turn responses over more clarification exchanges. In this paper, we study the problem of "strategic clarification" -- asking clarification questions in a reference game when their answers will narrow the set of candidate referents. We construct a controlled test environment from the SIMMC 2.1 dataset that records how many questions each ambiguous reference needs to be successfully resolved, and examine how baseline LLM behavior and common post-training methods ask clarifying questions vs. attempt to answer the referent. We also propose BACE (Belief-Aware Critic with Epistemic tracking), a lightweight action chooser method on top of a frozen LM backbone, and show that BACE more consistently chooses the optimal number of clarifying questions than methods such as GRPO and ArCHer.

CCDWIKI: Decoupling Case Formulation from Response Generation in Longitudinal CBT Dialogue
CCDWIKI：在纵向认知行为疗法对话中解耦案例构想与响应生成
Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) demands more than empathic validation, requiring instead a structured formulation of the interplay among situations, automatic thoughts, emotions, and behaviors. Therapists formalize this interplay through a Cognitive Conceptualization Diagram, where maintaining longitudinal coherence necessitates continuous evidential adjudication to separate tentative hypotheses from established findings. Current dialogue systems track conversational history via retrieval or static profiles but lack the representational capacity to encode epistemic status. Consequently, provisional interpretations are often prematurely reified as established facts, inadvertently biasing downstream interventions. Addressing this limitation, CCDWiki is introduced as a plug-and-play advisor conceptualizing longitudinal therapy as evidence-grounded hypothesis adjudication. The approach encodes each clinical hypothesis as a mutable entry bound to corroborating and contradicting evidence. Across sessions, accumulating data strengthens supported entries, preserves provisional ones, and retracts contradicted assumptions. The resulting formulation distills into concise guidance for unmodified therapeutic language models. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in both conceptualization and intervention quality without requiring backbone retraining.
中文翻译
认知行为疗法（CBT）不仅需要共情验证，更要求对情境、自动思维、情绪与行为之间相互作用的结构化分析。治疗师通过认知概念化图来形式化这种相互作用，而维持纵向一致性需要持续的证据裁决，以区分暂定假设与已确立的发现。当前对话系统通过检索或静态档案追踪对话历史，但缺乏编码认识状态的表示能力。因此，临时解释常常被过早固化为既定事实，无意中影响后续干预。为解决此问题，本文提出CCDWIKI，一种即插即用的顾问系统，将纵向治疗视为基于证据的假设裁决过程。该方法将每个临床假设编码为可变条目，并绑定支持与反驳证据。在多轮会话中，累积数据强化支持条目，保留暂定条目，撤销矛盾假设。最终形成的构想可提炼为简洁指导，供未经修改的治疗语言模型使用。实验表明，该方法在不重新训练主干模型的情况下，显著提升了构想与干预质量。

DS@GT ARC at CheckThat! 2026: A Verdict-Anchored Pipeline for Generating Fact-Checking Articles
DS@GT ARC 在 CheckThat! 2026 中的判别锚定流水线用于生成事实核查文章
Misinformation spreads faster than professional fact-checkers can manually verify it and write articles explaining their verdicts. This creates a need to automate the production of fact-checking articles. This paper details work done by the DS@GT ARC team for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3 Fact-Checking Article Generation. We seek to generate a full fact-checking article from a claim, its original verdict, and a set of scraped source documents. Our team built a four-stage pipeline that cleans noisy evidence, retrieves and clusters relevant sources, generates the article by conditioning \texttt{Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct} on the original fact-checker verdict, and recovers inline citations through deterministic post-processing. We achieved a mean score of 0.428 and ranked 3rd out of 9 teams for the CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3, compared to a baseline mean score of 0.272. Our code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/checkthat-2026-task3.

A Framework for Context Enrichment and Selective Robustness in LLM Social Agents
大型语言模型社交代理的上下文增强与选择性鲁棒性框架
Researchers use large language models as agents in social simulations, where agents are prompted with observations and background information intended to shape their actions. Yet LLM agents have been shown to substantially change their behavior under meaning-preserving prompt variations, creating a validation problem for social simulation. One plausible hypothesis is that this brittleness reflects a lack of grounding and can be reduced by enriching the context provided to the agents. Whether richer context improves robustness remains unclear, in part because context richness and robustness are rarely defined in ways that support controlled comparisons. We address the first need with controlled context enrichment, a set of prompt substitution rules that systematically enriches distinct types of context: neutral padding, generic social background, descriptive decision grounding, and normative decision grounding. We address the second by introducing selective robustness as a validation criterion: insensitivity to meaning-preserving prompt variation combined with a directional response to decision-relevant changes in the social world. We demonstrate the framework in a Prisoner's Dilemma-like game, showing how it can be used to identify which forms of context enrichment produce more desirable and robust agent behavior. We find that these forms of enrichment produce distinct behavioral patterns that vary between the two models tested.
中文翻译
研究人员在社会模拟中使用大语言模型作为代理，通过提示观察结果和背景信息来引导其行为。然而，已有研究表明，即使提示语义保持不变，LLM代理的行为仍可能显著变化，导致社会模拟的验证问题。一种合理的假设是这种脆弱性反映了缺乏情境锚定，可通过丰富代理所接收的上下文来缓解。然而，上下文丰富度与鲁棒性之间的关系尚不明确，部分原因在于二者很少以支持可控比较的方式进行定义。为此，我们提出了受控上下文增强方法，即一组系统性的提示替换规则，用于逐步增强不同类型的上下文：中性填充、通用社会背景、描述性决策依据和规范性决策依据。同时，我们引入“选择性鲁棒性”作为验证标准：对语义保持不变的提示变化不敏感，但对社会世界中与决策相关的变化具有方向性响应。我们在类囚徒困境游戏中展示了该框架的应用，证明其可用于识别哪些形式的上下文增强能产生更理想且稳健的代理行为。实验发现，这些增强方式在两种测试模型中产生了不同的行为模式。

LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis
LongBanana：面向长上下文多参考图像合成的专家验证基准
We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.

我们提出了LongBanana，据我们所知，这是在代理驱动图像生成时代首个由专家设计并验证的长上下文多参考图像合成基准。该任务要求从分散的视觉证据中检索信息、排除干扰项、绑定实体与关系，并基于多个参考图像生成一幅连贯的最终图像。LongBanana包含90个精心设计的任务，按3×3分类体系组织；每个任务包含约32张参考图像、20个约束条件和50个评估项目，总计4,670个专家指定的原子评估项。其图谱基评估设计为每个任务提供参考图像和实体-关系图，将节点、边及整体图的义务转化为内容保真度、关系绑定和全局约束满足的检查清单项。评审专家标签包含15%的双标注子集，精确一致率达89.4%，加权二次kappa值为0.83。我们进一步提出了LongBanana-Gen——一个用于长周期图像生成的代理驱动框架；实验表明，最小化的代理调度优于直接生成，而ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect（RPR）工作流通过强化证据锚定与修复机制，实现了依赖于代理调度的性能提升。总体而言，LongBanana为衡量密集视觉证据利用提供了可审计的测试平台，并为开发更强的代理驱动图像生成系统设定了共同目标。代码与数据可在项目页面获取：https://long-banana.com/。

Template Gap Reconstruction for Shell Output Simulation with Small Language Models
小语言模型壳输出模拟中的模板间隙重建
Small language models offer a compact basis for shell simulation without executing submitted commands, but full-output decoding requires them to predict recurring utility structure alongside content that varies across executions. We introduce template gap reconstruction (TGR), which supplies reusable output literals interspersed with sentinels to be interleaved with autoregressively generated gaps. A template selector chooses which template to be reconstructed, with the empty template retaining full-output decoding. We construct a template bank from 18.7 million outputs collected across heterogeneous execution environments and train a template selector and five reconstruction models initialized from various small language model checkpoints on a target environment excluded from bank construction. Evaluation demonstrates that TGR improves output edit similarity (O-ES) over full-output decoding (FOD) when the optimal template covers more than 25\% of the template, while performing similarly for lower-coverage outputs. Additionally, TGR outperforms FOD for shorter gap sequences, with O-ES values of 0.964 and 0.812, respectively, while maintaining similar performance for longer sequences. TGR reduces the number of tokens generated by 26.3--38.3\% across used tokenizer families. These results support using a reusable output structure as a practical design choice for small shell simulators, improving response fidelity and shortening generation targets while keeping template selection and residual prediction central to response quality.
中文翻译
小语言模型为无需执行提交命令即可实现壳模拟提供了紧凑基础，但完整输出解码需要它们同时预测重复的实用结构和跨执行变化的内容。我们引入模板间隙重建（TGR），通过在可重用输出字面量之间插入哨兵标记，与自回归生成的间隙交错排列。模板选择器决定重建哪个模板，空模板则保留完整输出解码。我们从异构执行环境中收集的1870万条输出构建了模板库，并在排除库构建目标环境的前提下，使用多个小型语言模型检查点训练模板选择器和五个重建模型。评估表明，当最优模板覆盖超过25%时，TGR在输出编辑相似性（O-ES）上优于完整输出解码（FOD）；对于低覆盖率输出，表现相当。此外，TGR在较短间隙序列上表现更优，O-ES分别为0.964和0.812，而在较长序列上性能相近。TGR在各类分词器下减少了26.3%至38.3%的生成token数量。这些结果支持将可重用输出结构作为小规模壳模拟器的实际设计选择，提升了响应保真度，缩短了生成目标，同时保持模板选择与残差预测对响应质量的核心作用。

Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit
回合至关重要：通过回合级信用分配推进流媒体视频强化学习
Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.
中文翻译
流媒体视频理解要求模型反复决定是否回应或继续观察。近期，组相对策略优化（GRPO）被应用于这一场景，现有方法强调奖励设计，但保留了跨回合的单一轨迹级优势。这种共享信用可能导致在早期成功后强化冗余回复，或在早期失败后抑制有用的后续回应。我们提出TurnWise，一种回合级GRPO框架，使学习与个体流媒体决策对齐。TurnWise解决了轨迹级反馈与回合级交互之间的不匹配，促进信息丰富的回应并减少冗余。具体而言，回合级信用分配利用沉默完成前缀评分，从每个决策的剩余回报中构建组相对优势，将其学习信号与早期奖励分离。回合均值策略优化在每回合内平均标记损失，再跨回合平均，使回应与等待决策获得同等权重，无论输出长度如何。这些互补设计在无需学习批评家或额外采样回滚的情况下，提升了流媒体策略学习效果。大量实验表明，我们的方法在流媒体视频理解任务上优于先前的强化学习方法。

What Does Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning Really Improve in LLM Agents?
多轮强化学习在大语言模型智能体中究竟提升了什么？
While multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning (RL) excels on long-horizon tasks, its performance gains remain highly sensitive to interaction constraints. Evaluating across sampling breadth $K$ and interaction depth $T$ via $Pass@(K,T)$, we show that trajectory-level RL advantages depend on sufficient turn budgets, diminishing significantly when budgets tighten. Besides, agentic RL does not uniformly elevate single-step decision quality; rather, its strength concentrates in error recovery and long-horizon resilience. To mitigate the deployment risks caused by this budget dependence, we provide a preliminary discussion on the benefits and limitations of process supervision signals and introduce BudgetMix RL as an alternative that varies interaction budgets during training. Experiments demonstrate that BudgetMix RL achieves robust performance across diverse execution settings. Project Page: https://rethinking-agentic-rl.github.io/

尽管多轮智能体强化学习（RL）在长周期任务上表现出色，但其性能提升仍高度依赖交互约束。通过在采样广度$K$和交互深度$T$上评估$Pass@(K,T)$，我们发现轨迹级RL优势依赖于充足的轮次预算，当预算收紧时显著下降。此外，智能体RL并非均匀提升单步决策质量，而是其优势集中在错误恢复和长周期韧性方面。为缓解由此带来的部署风险，我们初步讨论了过程监督信号的优劣，并提出了BudgetMix RL作为替代方案，在训练过程中动态调整交互预算。实验表明，BudgetMix RL在多种执行环境下均能实现稳健表现。

LegalEvolver: Agentic Legal Judgment Prediction via Rubric-Guided Self-Evolution
LegalEvolver：基于规则引导自演化的智能体法律判决预测
As LLM-based agents develop, they have been increasingly applied in the legal domain. Legal Judgment Prediction~(LJP) requires models to conduct multi-step reasoning and external legal evidence retrieval. Existing legal search agents' performance is often constrained by the quality of retrieval queries. They often formulate queries directly from the initial user request, overlooking latent legal issues and legally salient details. To address this issue, we propose \emph{LegalEvolver}, a query optimization framework specifically designed to generate legally informative queries for LJP-oriented retrieval. Sampling high-quality optimized queries and their corresponding search trajectories is challenging, as relying solely on unguided high-temperature rollouts is both inefficient and constrained by the model's initial capabilities. We therefore heuristically optimize queries as seeds, improving the efficiency of trajectory generation. Furthermore, we summarize rollout experience into rubrics and use them to guide multi-round iterative self-evolution. Iteratively performing rubric optimization and rubric-guided RL enables the model to surpass its initial performance ceiling. End-to-end performance on extensive benchmarks demonstrates the effectiveness of query optimization on LJP. Comparisons with direct SFT followed by RL demonstrate the effectiveness of rubric guidance. Evolving rubrics further outperform fixed rubrics, confirming the benefits of self-evolution.

On the Design of Hyper-Connections: Simplification, Stability, and Scalability
超连接设计：简化、稳定与可扩展性
Hyper-Connections (HC) architectures are increasingly adopted in frontier language models, yet their components remain insufficiently understood and introduce non-negligible training overhead. In this work, we systematically study the HC architecture to understand its components and identify simpler, more efficient designs. We find that learned read/write gating without mixing and unconstrained mixing with fixed gates form two effective interaction groups: either alone achieves performance comparable to mHC. We also identify repeated access to the expanded residual state as a major source of training overhead, even without mixing. These findings lead to SimpleHC, which simplifies the architecture by removing residual mixing and simplifies execution through shared memory access, using the resulting additive structure and shared gate inputs. Layer-specific gates share a group-entry state, enabling batched reads and deferred writes; scalar corrections exactly preserve preceding sublayer output contributions. Our analyses support both the retained residual structure and the design of shared memory access. SimpleHC incurs only 2.4% extra end-to-end training time over a vanilla residual baseline at the largest tested scale. It maintains performance comparable to mHC when scaling from 15B to 30B MoE models, exhibits smoother gradient dynamics during standard training, and remains stable under stress. We hope SimpleHC will encourage a broader pursuit of simplicity in foundation model design, making performance gains more accessible and practical at scale.
中文翻译
超连接（HC）架构在前沿语言模型中日益普及，但其组件仍缺乏充分理解，并引入了不可忽视的训练开销。本文系统研究了HC架构，以理解其组成部分并识别更简单、高效的替代设计。我们发现，无需混合的可学习读写门控，以及使用固定门控的无约束混合，构成了两种有效的交互模式：单独使用任一模式即可达到与mHC相当的性能。我们还识别出对扩展残差状态的重复访问是训练开销的主要来源，即使在没有混合的情况下也是如此。这些发现催生了SimpleHC，该架构通过移除残差混合并利用共享内存访问简化执行，采用所得的加法结构和共享门控输入。层特定的门控共享一个组入口状态，支持批量读取和延迟写入；标量修正精确保留了前序子层输出的贡献。我们的分析支持保留残差结构和共享内存访问的设计。在最大测试规模下，SimpleHC仅比标准残差基线多出2.4%的端到端训练时间。当从15B扩展到30B MoE模型时，其性能与mHC相当，标准训练过程中梯度动态更平滑，且在压力下仍保持稳定。我们希望SimpleHC能推动基础模型设计中对简洁性的更广泛追求，使大规模下的性能提升更加可及和实用。

Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models
面向基于MoE的多模态大语言模型的模态感知专家剪枝
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to scale capacity efficiently, but expert parameters dominate memory even though only a few experts are activated per token: they account for over 90% of model size in modern MoE-MLLMs (e.g., 54 GB out of about 58 GB in BF16 for InternVL3.5-30B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B). Expert pruning has emerged as an effective compression strategy, but existing methods aggregate importance uniformly across tokens, ignoring that the same expert may serve different roles for vision versus language processing. To address this, we propose MAEP (Modality-Aware Expert Pruning), a training-free framework that decomposes expert importance by modality in a single forward pass. MAEP introduces (1) Delta-H Output ($ΔH$), a redistribution-aware metric measuring the MoE output change when an expert is removed, and (2) low-attention vision tokens as a vision-side pruning signal that is dataset-stable: its expert-importance ranking remains stable across different datasets. Text-side importance serves as a preservation signal while vision-side importance serves as a pruning signal, and experts are ranked globally across layers. Experiments on three MoE-based MLLMs across multimodal and text benchmarks show that MAEP outperforms modality-agnostic baselines on the majority of multimodal benchmarks while remaining competitive on text-only tasks, reducing the BF16 expert weight footprint by about 27 GB (about 47% of total memory) so that the full weight footprint of an approximately 58 GB model fits within a single 40 GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP.
中文翻译
混合专家（MoE）使多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够高效扩展容量，但专家参数占据主导内存，尽管每个token仅激活少数专家：在现代MoE-MLLMs中，专家参数占比超过90%（例如，在BF16下，InternVL3.5-30B-A3B和Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B约54GB/58GB）。专家剪枝已成为一种有效的压缩策略，但现有方法在token间统一聚合重要性，忽略了同一专家在视觉与语言处理中可能扮演不同角色。为此，我们提出MAEP（模态感知专家剪枝），一种无需训练的框架，可在单次前向传播中按模态分解专家重要性。MAEP引入（1）ΔH输出（Delta-H Output），一种考虑重分配的度量，用于衡量移除专家后MoE输出的变化；（2）低注意力视觉token作为视觉侧的剪枝信号，具有数据集稳定性：其专家重要性排序在不同数据集间保持一致。文本侧重要性作为保留信号，视觉侧重要性作为剪枝信号，专家在各层全局排序。在三个基于MoE的MLLMs上，跨多模态和文本基准的实验表明，MAEP在多数多模态基准上优于忽略模态的基线，同时在纯文本任务上保持竞争力，将BF16专家权重占用减少约27GB（约占总内存的47%），使得约58GB模型的完整权重可放入单个40GB GPU中。代码已公开于https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP。
👤 Donggeun Kim 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.05919]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models
推理路径：在MoE模型中读取与重塑测试时计算
Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.
中文翻译
推理模型将测试时计算分配给不同的行为，如规划、推导和验证。然而，模型经常“过度思考”，在找到正确答案后仍持续验证和重新推导，这浪费了昂贵的计算资源，甚至可能降低性能。现有干预手段如token预算或引导主要调节推理时长，而非针对特定行为。本文展示，混合专家（MoE）路由器为行为控制提供了自然且无需训练的接口。我们首先将推理轨迹拆分为句子级片段，并为每个片段标注六种推理行为之一（规划、执行、重述、事实、反思、结论），使每个token携带其片段的行为标签。对路由器logits的线性探测器预测该标签的准确率几乎与对残差流（模型完整隐藏状态）的探测器相当，且仅使用约一半特征。这些信号具有泛化能力：在一个数据集上与某种推理行为相关的专家，能在其他数据集上预测相同行为，涵盖五个领域（数学、代码、科学、知识、常识）的六个数据集。在此基础上，我们在推理期间选择性编辑专家路由器分数以抑制过度思考。在四个模型和四个基准上的实验显示，该干预使生成token数中位数减少30.4%（最高达63.3%）。关键的是，它促使模型在问题解决后停止，而非跳过必要推理步骤。准确率从未下降超过0.5个百分点，且常有提升。最终，MoE路由既提供了理解推理状态的可解释视角，也提供了一种轻量级优化测试时计算的机制。
👤 Shengjie Wang, Mohsen Fayyaz, Nanyun Peng 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv

Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning
用于电力系统规划的自主智能体
Planning electricity systems requires repeated decisions that connect regional resources, investment, operation and network constraints. Here we present an autonomous planning framework that carries prepared regional scenarios through proposal, numerical evaluation, feedback interpretation, redesign and final plan selection. Agents propose investment bounds, storage operating conditions and feeder interventions, interpret numerical feedback, and decide how to revise designs and when to stop. The framework automatically executes annual investment-and-dispatch optimization and representative-feeder verification, applies fixed acceptance criteria and retains the lowest-cost accepted candidate. We evaluate forty carbon-constrained trajectories across ten system-year scenarios in five electricity systems and four countries, using two model deployments and single- and multi-agent architectures. All forty retain candidates satisfying the implemented physical and fixed annual operational carbon-budget checks. Twenty-eight of forty matched reference designs exceed the new scenario ceilings. A separate archive of ninety-six trajectories under a common 1,000 Mt annual ceiling provides matched references and broader regional coverage. The results demonstrate autonomous planning under explicit emissions constraints using public regional data and published institutional demand outlooks.
中文翻译
电力系统规划需要反复决策，涉及区域资源、投资、运行和网络约束的连接。本文提出一种自主规划框架，能够将预设的区域情景推进至提案、数值评估、反馈解读、重新设计和最终方案选择。智能体提出投资上限、储能运行条件和馈线干预措施，解读数值反馈，并决定如何修改设计以及何时停止。该框架自动执行年度投资与调度优化及代表性馈线验证，应用固定接受标准并保留成本最低的合格候选方案。我们在五个电力系统和四个国家的十种系统年情景下评估了四十条碳约束轨迹，采用两种模型部署和单智能体与多智能体架构。所有四十条轨迹均满足实施的物理和固定年度运行碳预算检查。其中二十八条轨迹匹配参考设计，超过了新情景的上限。另有一个包含九十六条轨迹的独立档案，设定共同的1,000Mt年度上限，提供匹配参考和更广泛的区域覆盖。结果表明，该框架可基于公开区域数据和已发表机构需求展望，在明确排放约束下实现自主规划。

[2611.05759]

PAYWORTHY: Can LLMs Predict Which Freelance Jobs an AI Agent Will Complete?
PAYWORTHY：大语言模型能否预测AI代理会完成哪些自由职业工作？
Before an AI agent takes on a paid job, someone has to judge whether it is likely to succeed. We test whether frontier language models can make this call before any attempt. We propose PAYWORTHY which contains 1{,}494 real freelance job postings, sampled from 18.8M postings made between 2020 and 2026 on a freelancing platform. An agent attempts each job from the posting alone in a single run, and an LLM judge acting as the client decides whether to accept the work. We ask 18 models to forecast how likely the agent is to succeed and measure how well each model's forecasts rank the accepted jobs above the rejected ones (AUC, where 0.5 is a random ranking). The answer depends on how the agent is described. Told what the agent cannot do, every model ranks the jobs well (AUC 0.66--0.86). Told instead that it is a generic agent with a browser and code execution, the same models rank the same jobs about as well as a random ordering (0.45--0.56). These results hold under judges from four other model families (Claude, GLM, Kimi and Qwen), and the informed forecasts also rank the verdicts of two human raters. Code is available at https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy
中文翻译
在AI代理接受有偿工作之前，必须有人判断其成功可能性。我们测试了前沿语言模型是否能在任何尝试之前做出这一判断。我们提出了PAYWORTHY，包含从2020年至2026年间某自由职业平台上1880万条职位信息中采样的1,494个真实自由职业岗位。每个代理在单次运行中独立尝试一个岗位，由充当客户的LLM裁判决定是否接受该工作。我们要求18个模型预测代理成功的可能性，并衡量各模型预测结果将被接受的工作排在拒绝工作之上的表现（AUC，0.5为随机排序）。结果取决于对代理的描述方式：若被告知代理无法完成某些任务，所有模型均表现良好（AUC 0.66–0.86）；若被告知其为具备浏览器和代码执行能力的通用代理，则相同模型的表现与随机排序相当（AUC 0.45–0.56）。该结果在其他四个模型家族（Claude、GLM、Kimi和Qwen）的裁判下依然成立，且知情预测也与两名人类评估者的判断排名一致。代码已公开于https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy

AGORA: Reliability-Aware Aggregation of LLM Judges via Item-Response Theory
AGORA：基于项目反应理论的可靠性感知LLM裁判聚合
LLM-as-a-judge is a common substitute for costly human evaluation, and aggregating several judges is the standard remedy for any single judge's bias. Judge pools mix judges of very different reliability, while the human-labeled sets that validate them hold a few hundred items or fewer. In this regime the per-item mean weights weak judges as heavily as strong ones, and confusion-table models such as Dawid-Skene must estimate a per-judge error table from too few items. We propose AGORA (Aggregation Of Reliability-weighted Assessments), an item-response-theory aggregator that places every item on one latent quality scale and describes each judge by a sensitivity parameter and ordered rating thresholds, so judge reliability is learned from all items jointly and without human labels. Across three benchmarks, AGORA matches or exceeds Dawid-Skene on large evaluation sets, and its advantage widens as the set shrinks toward a few hundred items. It remains accurate as pools grow more uneven, a label-free dispersion statistic indicates in advance when trust weighting helps, and its learned trust weights track per-judge human validity.
中文翻译
LLM作为裁判是昂贵人工评估的常见替代方案，聚合多个裁判是应对单一裁判偏差的标准方法。然而，裁判池中裁判可靠性差异巨大，而用于验证的标注数据集通常仅包含数百项。在此情况下，每项的平均权重会同等对待高可靠性和低可靠性裁判，而如Dawid-Skene等混淆表模型需从过少的样本中估计每位裁判的错误矩阵。我们提出AGORA（可靠性加权评估聚合），一种基于项目反应理论的聚合器，将每项置于单一潜在质量尺度上，并用敏感度参数和有序评分阈值描述每位裁判，从而联合学习所有项目中的裁判可靠性，且无需人工标签。在三个基准测试中，AGORA在大规模评估集上表现与或超过Dawid-Skene，且随着评估集缩小至数百项时优势愈发明显。其在裁判池日益不均衡时仍保持准确性；标签无关的分散统计量可提前指示信任加权是否有效；其学习到的信任权重与人工有效性高度一致。
👤 Junhyung Ahn 📅 2026-11-03 🔗 arXiv
[2611.05548]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Iterated creation, grading, and revision of data science projects by language models
语言模型迭代创建、评分与修订数据科学项目
We evaluate the extent to which agentic AI models can complete an end-to-end data science project, and how this improves with feedback. A 'writer' model with specific tools creates a complete data science project including downloading, cleaning, and analyzing a dataset, and writing a short paper about it. The project is executed and a 'grader' model evaluates it. The writer can then revise its submission, and this repeats for a total of three rounds. We consider seven models from five developers: Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek, and Z.AI. Each model is both a writer and a grader, so there are 49 writer-grader pairs and we run each pair three times. Of the 147 initial submissions, 107 run and render, and the average score increases from 59 per cent to 76 per cent after the final round of feedback. By writer the extent of the increase ranges from 3 percentage points for Anthropic's Opus 5.5 to 46 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5, and by grader it ranges from 13 percentage points for Google's Gemini 3.8 Flash to 32 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5. When a model grades its own writing the score tends to be about 9 percentage points higher than the average of what the other models give it. About half of the difference that we see from round to round is within the grader's own noise. Feedback generally results in the highest marks, but even just having the chance to revise, or having the additional budget, in terms of time or tool calls, without any feedback, results in considerable increases for some of the models but not for others. We find that how much such a loop helps depends on which model plays which role, but weaker models generally do not improve stronger models.
中文翻译
我们评估了智能体AI模型完成端到端数据科学项目的能力，以及反馈如何提升这一能力。一个具有特定工具的‘写作者’模型创建完整的数据科学项目，包括下载、清洗和分析数据集，并撰写一篇短论文。项目执行后，由‘评分者’模型进行评估。写作者可据此修订提交内容，此过程重复三轮。我们测试了来自五家开发者的七种模型：Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、DeepSeek 和 Z.AI。每种模型既是写作者又是评分者，共形成49对组合，每对运行三次。在147次初始提交中，107次成功运行并呈现结果，平均得分从59%提升至76%（最终反馈后）。按写作者划分，得分提升幅度从Anthropic Opus 5.5的3个百分点到Haiku 4.5的46个百分点不等；按评分者划分，从Google Gemini 3.8 Flash的13个百分点到Anthropic Haiku 4.5的32个百分点不等。当模型自行评分时，其得分通常比其他模型平均分高出约9个百分点。我们观察到的每轮间差异中，约一半源于评分者的自身噪声。反馈总体上带来最高分数，但即使没有反馈，仅提供修订机会或额外的时间/工具调用预算，也能显著提升部分模型的表现，而对其他模型则无明显帮助。我们发现该循环带来的帮助程度取决于模型所扮演的角色，但较弱模型通常无法提升较强模型的表现。

Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning
基于元智能体监督的挑战感知知识召回在医疗多智能体推理中的应用
Large language models perform strongly on standard medical QA but degrade on harder reasoning tasks, where multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-model prompting. We argue that a key limitation is that existing systems select experts and retrieve knowledge from the question alone, rather than from gaps that emerge during reasoning. We propose challenge-aware adaptive recruitment: an external CHALLENGER inspects the evolving reasoning trace and identifies specific factual or expertise gaps. These signals trigger targeted knowledge retrieval or mid-discussion specialist recruitment. On the MEDAGENTSBENCH hard subset (862 questions across nine biomedical datasets), upfront retrieval provides little benefit, while challenge-aware in-between retrieval performs better; on a multi-specialist backbone it improves accuracy on 8 of 9 datasets and adds 6 percentage points over Challenger Only. Ablations indicate benefits from both retrieval timing and gap-specific query signals, and a live web-search experiment shows that the timing advantage persists across retrieval backends. For specialist recruitment, self-triggered adaptation is unreliable, whereas Challenger-triggered recruitment raises GPT-4.1-mini MedQA accuracy from 46% to 54%, with positive direction on all tested generalization datasets. Naively combining retrieval and specialist recruitment degrades performance, highlighting the need to coordinate adaptive intervention channels. These results show that when and why external resources are recruited is a key design choice in multi-agent medical reasoning.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在标准医疗问答任务中表现良好，但在更复杂的推理任务中性能下降，此时多智能体辩论往往无法超越强单模型提示。我们认为关键限制在于现有系统仅根据问题本身选择专家和检索知识，而非根据推理过程中浮现的空白。我们提出挑战感知的自适应知识招募：外部CHALLENGER监控推理过程的演变，识别具体的事实性或专业性空白。这些信号触发针对性的知识检索或讨论中途的专业人员招募。在MEDAGENTSBENCH硬子集（9个生物医学数据集共862个问题）上，事前检索收益甚微，而挑战感知的中间阶段检索表现更优；在多专家骨干架构下，其在9个数据集中有8个提升了准确率，并比仅使用CHALLENGER的方案高出6个百分点。消融实验表明，检索时机和针对具体空白的查询信号均具价值；在线网络搜索实验也显示，时机优势在不同检索后端中持续存在。对于专家招募，自我触发的自适应不可靠，而由CHALLENGER触发的招募将GPT-4.1-mini MedQA准确率从46%提升至54%，并在所有测试泛化数据集上均呈正向提升。盲目结合检索与专家招募反而会降低性能，凸显协调自适应干预通道的必要性。这些结果表明，在何时以及为何引入外部资源是多智能体医疗推理中的关键设计选择。

[2611.06859]
Balancing hierarchical representation and quantization: A performance trade-off in text embeddings
层次表示与量化之间的平衡：文本嵌入中的性能权衡
Hierarchical text embedding models offer variable-length representations, while quantization reduces numerical precision; both aim to improve computational efficiency. This work is an empirical benchmark study of how these two orthogonal, already-established techniques interact. We evaluate 38 tasks from the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark (MTEB) using four transformer-based embedding models, covering 1D and 2D hierarchical representations combined with 32-bit, 8-bit, and binary quantization, for 168 configurations per task. We report each efficiency dimension separately: theoretical per-vector storage and end-to-end evaluation time. Under a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis, int8 quantization is statistically indistinguishable from float32 for pair classification and re-ranking and costs at most 1.5 percentage points overall, whereas binary quantization is significantly worse across every task category. Halving the embedding dimension once is not statistically detectable above $m=16$; two successive halvings always are. Combining an 8x dimensionality reduction with int8 yields a 32x reduction in per-vector storage at 94.4% of baseline effectiveness. Because evaluation times were measured once, we treat them as indicative: we find no evidence that quantization speeds up evaluation, whereas sampling from intermediate layers shortens it substantially. Retrieval degrades far faster than every other category, so we report findings per task category.
中文翻译
层次化文本嵌入模型提供可变长度的表示，而量化则降低数值精度；两者均旨在提升计算效率。本研究是一项实证基准研究，探讨这两种已确立的正交技术如何相互作用。我们使用四种基于Transformer的嵌入模型，在来自大规模文本嵌入基准（MTEB）的38个任务上进行评估，涵盖一维和二维层次表示，结合32位、8位和二值量化，每项任务共168种配置。我们分别报告各项效率维度：理论上的每向量存储空间和端到端评估时间。在Friedman检验及Nemenyi事后分析下，int8量化在成对分类和重排序任务中与float32无统计差异，整体损失不超过1.5个百分点；而二值量化在所有任务类别中均显著更差。将嵌入维度减半一次在m=16以上不可检测；两次连续减半始终可检测。将8倍降维与int8结合，可在保持94.4%基线有效性的同时实现每向量存储32倍减少。由于评估时间仅测量一次，我们将其视为指示性数据：未发现量化能加速评估的证据，而从中间层采样则显著缩短评估时间。检索性能下降远快于其他类别，因此我们按任务类别报告结果。
👤 Kai Ponel, Marco Polignano, Erasmo Purificato, Aylin Julia Temizkan, Marco Levantesi et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06639]
Replicability, Reproducibility, and Robustness in Political and Social Science Research with Large Language Models
大型语言模型在政治与社会科学研究中的可重复性、可复现性与鲁棒性
This chapter addresses the central reproducibility problem of research with large language models. LLM output is probabilistic and varies across models and versions, which can have significant effects on downstream statistical results. The chapter distinguishes between replicability, reproducibility, and robustness, and proposes a probabilistic standard based on the distribution of results, not identical outputs. It introduces a standardized prompt script, tests within-model variation for encoders and decoders, and develops error-structure analysis to diagnose where models err and whether their errors align. Using three datasets, the chapter shows that measurement choice can reverse the sign of downstream regression coefficients. Existing design-based corrections with gold-labeled subsamples restore validity, but come at the cost of estimate precision. The main contribution of the chapter is a stacked ensemble of open-weight models that improves precision in the correction step while retaining estimate validity.
中文翻译
本文探讨了大型语言模型研究中的核心可复现性问题。LLM输出具有概率性，且在不同模型和版本间存在差异，这可能对下游统计结果产生重大影响。文章区分了可重复性、可复现性和鲁棒性，并提出基于结果分布而非完全相同输出的概率标准。引入标准化提示脚本，测试编码器与解码器的模型内变异，并开发误差结构分析方法以诊断模型出错位置及其错误是否一致。利用三个数据集，文章表明测量选择可能导致下游回归系数符号反转。现有的基于设计的校正方法虽能恢复有效性，但会牺牲估计精度。本文的主要贡献是一个开放权重模型的堆叠集成，可在校正步骤中提升精度的同时保持估计的有效性。
👤 Joan C. Timoneda, Sebastián Vallejo Vera 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06517]
Dialect Matters: A Dialect-Aware Baseline for Igala-English Machine Translation
方言很重要：伊加拉-英语机器翻译的方言感知基线
The massive permeability of intelligent systems in every sphere of human activities have greatly enhanced clarity, ease of work, and increased general global understanding which has significantly awaken the use of intelligent systems by all users. Intelligent systems greatly rely on precise dataset/corpus for the correctness of their output. Despite efforts by researchers to address Low-resource language in the Natural Language Processing (NLP), Igala still has zero parallel corpora despite efforts by researchers. We present a dialect-aware baseline for Igala-English machine translation, among first open systems and first with dialectal variation analysis. Our dataset of 1137 sentences (899 training) is first with dialect tags: Idah 459 (40.4%), Ankpa 297 (26.1%), Ibaji 160 (14.1%), Other 187 (16.4%), Ogugu 32 (2.8%). Same English yields 20-60 Igala forms. Mixed-dialect training achieves balanced BLEU 11.66 versus Idah-only model which drops 4.7 BLEU on Ibaji (13.2 on Idah, 8.5 on Ibaji), proving dialect fairness requires mixing. Fine-tuned from Helsinki-NLP/opus-mt-en-mul (78M), epoch ablation shows convergence at 8 epochs and overfitting at 15. BLEU 11.66, chrF 46.69. Analysis shows 899 sentences on log-linear trend consistent with African low-resource literature. This work provides first dialectal impact analysis for Igala and establishes baseline for future work.
中文翻译
智能系统在人类活动各个领域的广泛渗透极大提升了清晰度、工作便利性以及全球整体理解，显著推动了各类用户对智能系统的使用。智能系统高度依赖精确的数据集/语料库以确保输出正确性。尽管研究人员已努力应对自然语言处理中的低资源语言问题，但伊加拉语至今仍缺乏平行语料库。我们提出了首个面向伊加拉-英语机器翻译的方言感知基线，也是首个公开系统并包含方言变化分析的工作。我们的数据集包含1137个句子（899个训练），首次带有方言标签：伊达459句（40.4%）、安帕297句（26.1%）、伊巴吉160句（14.1%）、其他187句（16.4%）、奥古古32句（2.8%）。同一英文句可对应20-60种伊加拉表达形式。混合方言训练实现平衡BLEU得分为11.66，而仅使用伊达方言的模型在伊巴吉方言上下降4.7 BLEU（伊达得分13.2，伊巴吉8.5），证明方言公平性需混合训练。该模型基于Helsinki-NLP/opus-mt-en-mul（78M参数）微调，消融实验显示在8轮收敛，15轮出现过拟合。最终BLEU得分为11.66，chrF为46.69。分析显示899个句子呈对数线性趋势，与非洲低资源文献一致。本工作首次对伊加拉语方言影响进行了分析，并为未来研究建立了基线。

[2611.06403]
Ruby-OPD: Enhancing Pronunciation Controllability and Accuracy for Flow-Matching Text-to-Speech via On-Policy Distillation
Ruby-OPD：通过在线策略蒸馏提升流匹配文本到语音的发音可控性与准确性
Achieving both high pronunciation accuracy and fine-grained controllability remains a central challenge in Japanese text-to-speech (TTS), as Japanese is an unsegmented language mixing logographic Kanji and phonographic Kana with extensive polyphony. Traditional cascaded systems convert text into phoneme sequences via a front-end, ensuring pronunciation control but discarding lexical semantics and prosodic cues in surface orthography. Conversely, recent zero-shot TTS models directly consume raw orthographic text to improve naturalness, yet suffer from polyphone errors and lack explicit pronunciation control. To bridge this gap, we propose a two-stage framework for flow-matching TTS. First, we investigate Ruby text representations with orthography perturbation to jointly preserve surface forms and target pronunciations for reliable control. Second, we introduce On-Policy Distillation (OPD) on large-scale text-only corpora to enhance pronunciation accuracy on raw orthographic text without paired speech. On the Joyo-Kanji-Yomi benchmark, our model achieves 97.13% Accuracy, 2.02% Target PER, 0.70% Sentence PER, and 4.21% Sentence CER under raw text while retaining strong controllability.
中文翻译
在日语文本到语音（TTS）中，实现高发音准确性和细粒度可控性仍是一个核心挑战，因为日语是一种未分词的语言，混合使用表意文字汉字和音节文字假名，并存在大量多音字。传统级联系统通过前端将文本转换为音素序列，确保发音控制，但会丢失表面正写法中的词汇语义和韵律线索。相反，近期的零样本TTS模型直接处理原始正写法文本以提升自然度，却容易出现多音错误且缺乏显式的发音控制。为弥合这一差距，我们提出一种两阶段的流匹配TTS框架。首先，我们研究了带有正写法扰动的Ruby文本表示，以联合保留表面形式和目标发音，实现可靠控制。其次，我们在大规模纯文本语料库上引入在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在无配对语音的情况下提升原始正写法文本的发音准确性。在Joyo-Kanji-Yomi基准测试中，我们的模型在原始文本条件下实现了97.13%的准确率、2.02%的目标词错误率（PER）、0.70%的句子错误率（PER）和4.21%的句子字符错误率（CER），同时保持强可控性。

[2611.06402]
Interpretable Text Embeddings Based on Hierarchical Questions
基于层次化问题的可解释文本嵌入
QA-based text embeddings represent texts as vectors consisting of 1/0 answers to yes/no questions. Although these embeddings provide detailed information for interpretation and achieve high task performance, they often require humans to examine a large number of questions. Furthermore, the relationship between the number of questions and the appropriate level of question granularity has not been sufficiently studied. To address these challenges, we propose a method for constructing hierarchical questions for text embeddings. As the hierarchy deepens, questions become more specific and their number increases. During interpretation, we reduce the number of questions that humans need to examine by replacing questions at deeper levels with aligned questions at shallower levels. The question hierarchy is constructed through recursive clustering, contrastive prompting, and question alignment. Experimental results on general-purpose tasks show that our method improves interpretability while maintaining task performance. It is also found that, when only a limited number of questions are available, questions at shallower levels tend to achieve higher task performance than those at deeper levels, even with the same number of questions.
中文翻译
基于问答（QA）的文本嵌入将文本表示为一系列是/否问题的答案向量。尽管这些嵌入提供了丰富的解释信息并实现了较高的任务性能，但通常需要人工检查大量问题。此外，问题数量与问题粒度适当水平之间的关系尚未得到充分研究。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种构建文本嵌入层次化问题的方法。随着层次加深，问题变得更为具体，数量也增加。在解释过程中，我们通过用较浅层的对齐问题替换深层问题，减少人类需检查的问题数量。该问题层次结构通过递归聚类、对比提示和问题对齐构建。在通用任务上的实验结果表明，我们的方法在保持任务性能的同时提升了可解释性。此外，当可用问题数量有限时，较浅层的问题即使数量相同，也往往比深层问题取得更高的任务性能。
👤 Kohei Oda, Po-Min Chuang, Kiyoaki Shirai, Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06397]
Where Should a KV-Cache Repair Budget Go? Distractor Structure and Contextual Compatibility in Non-Prefix Reuse
KV缓存修复预算应投向何处？非前缀重用中的干扰结构与上下文兼容性
Non-prefix KV-cache reuse encodes retrieved chunks independently and concatenates their caches. To find where a small repair budget should be spent, we vary the chunks around the answer-bearing one (the target) across three 7B-8B models and four datasets of dialogue memory, open-domain QA and API documentation. Reuse loses 19-100% of the context-dependent questions that full prefill answers, and it loses more when the other chunks are of the same kind as the target. Because the recoverable damage is spread over these other chunks, restoring a few of them recovers little, and the budget is better spent on the target. This works through contextual compatibility: recomputing the target against the reused cache beats inserting its full-prefill KV in all seven settings tested, although the recomputed KV stays far from the full-prefill KV. Question attention over reused caches, however, selects the target in at most 18.8% of development questions. A retriever finds it more often, and recomputing its top chunk after moving it next to the question significantly beats InfoFlow-style reordering at the same 5% budget in six of nine open-domain QA and API-documentation settings. On dialogue memory, though, recomputing chunks never significantly beats the token-level selector chosen for each setting.
中文翻译
非前缀KV缓存重用独立编码检索到的块并拼接其缓存。为确定小规模修复预算的最佳投入位置，我们在三款7B-8B模型和四个数据集（对话记忆、开放域问答、API文档）上，对答案相关块（目标块）周围的块进行变化分析。重用导致19%-100%的上下文依赖问题丢失，尤其当其他块与目标块类型相同时损失更大。由于可恢复的损害分散在其他块中，仅修复其中少数几块效果甚微，因此预算更应投向目标块本身。这得益于上下文兼容性：在所有七个测试设置中，针对重用缓存重新计算目标块的表现优于在所有七处插入完整预填充KV，尽管重新计算的KV仍远未达到完整预填充KV水平。然而，对重用缓存的注意力机制在最多18.8%的开发问题中选中目标块。检索器则更常找到目标块，且在移动其顶层块紧邻问题后重新计算，在九个开放域问答和API文档设置中的六个里，以5%的预算表现优于InfoFlow风格的重排序。但在对话记忆任务中，重新计算块从未显著优于各设置中选定的token级选择器。
👤 Yuxing Peng, Zhiqing Tang, Weijia Jia 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06384]
Presentation Sensitivity in Typed Decision Models for Subjective Text Evaluation
主观文本评估中类型化决策模型的呈现敏感性
Typed decision models offer structured choices, scores, and probabilities for low-cost automated evaluation. We present a controlled audit of presentation sensitivity in Jev, Clef Flash, and Perplexity Decider across native choice and score interfaces. Across six datasets with 9,000 multiply annotated humor, helpfulness, and summarization items, we collect 702,000 judgments under interventions that vary positions, identifiers, authorship claims, endorsements, decision framing, and rating rubrics while preserving the evaluated texts. Jev's second-position probability advantage reaches 6.11 percentage points on summaries; reversing reader endorsements changes Perplexity's selected humor text on 40.40% of pairs; and rejection framing reduces Clef's summary agreement with human preferences by 23.36 points. Rating formats also shift score levels while often preserving rankings. Across these findings, repeatability, presentation robustness, and human alignment emerge as distinct properties of an evaluator. Reliable use therefore requires validating the decisions and score interpretations on which an evaluation pipeline depends.

[2611.06371]
Training-Free Character-Length Control in Summarization with Diffusion Language Models
基于扩散语言模型的无需训练的字符长度控制摘要生成
Summarization with a fixed character length is important for tasks such as headline and article preview generation, where text must fit an exact space. Autoregressive language models (LMs) struggle with precise character-length control: training-based methods require task-specific fine-tuning, while zero-shot prompting strategies achieve only approximate compliance. We propose DELTA (Diffusion Expected Length Targeting Algorithm), a training-free inference-time method for masked diffusion LMs that estimates expected character length at each denoising step and dynamically inserts or removes masked positions to steer toward a target length. DELTA requires no fine-tuning and is designed for off-the-shelf masked diffusion LMs. Across three summarization benchmarks, it reduces character-length errors by an order of magnitude compared to baselines while maintaining competitive summary quality in both automatic and human evaluation.
中文翻译

[2611.06238]
Zuse: A Decision Model for European Languages
Zuse：面向欧洲语言的决策模型
Decision models read a document and return a probability for each of a fixed set of options instead of generating text. Several appeared in 2026, but none has published results outside English. We describe Zuse, three decision models (4B, 9B and 27B) that train a low-rank adapter and a pointer head on frozen open-weight bases, targeting 27 European languages, and use them to study how this design behaves across languages and tasks. On a broad European suite of 11 sources excluded from training, covering 9 task groups and 27 languages, a development set that also guided model selection, the 27B model reaches 87.5% accuracy with a pooled expected calibration error of 0.016, and 0.035 when weighted per source. Post-hoc analyses of these single-seed results give three findings. First, miscalibration is structured by task rather than language: on parallel reading and topic items in 26 languages the confidence gap varies about as much as sampling noise, while across sources it spans -0.07 to +0.09, with knowledge tasks overconfident and acceptability judgements underconfident at all three sizes, so one fitted temperature cannot correct both. Second, the share of decisions that can be automated at a 5% error budget follows chance-corrected skill (Spearman 0.97 over 33 source and model pairs) more closely than raw accuracy (0.91). Third, reach beyond the training languages is set by the base model: across 210 languages, changing the training run moved scores by at most about 2 points within a size, while languages outside the base's documented list trail by 23 to 28 points at every size. On English public suites Zuse is behind Jev and Kev, including Kev models on the same bases. We also report two pitfalls in building multi-source training data: label-word collision between topic schemes, and an injection augmentation that taught a shortcut. Zuse can be tried at https://bineric.com/zuse.
中文翻译
决策模型读取文档并返回一组固定选项的概率，而非生成文本。尽管2026年出现了多个此类模型，但尚未有在英语以外的语言中发表结果。我们介绍了Zuse，包括三个决策模型（4B、9B和27B），它们在冻结的开放权重基座上训练低秩适配器和指针头，旨在覆盖27种欧洲语言，并用于研究该设计在不同语言和任务中的表现。在一个包含11个未参与训练来源的广泛欧洲基准测试集上（涵盖9个任务组和27种语言，同时作为指导模型选择的发展集），27B模型达到87.5%的准确率，合并期望校准误差为0.016，按来源加权后为0.035。对单种子结果的后验分析得出三项发现：第一，校准偏差由任务而非语言决定：在26种语言的平行阅读和主题项目中，置信度差距与采样噪声相当，而跨来源范围从-0.07到+0.09，知识类任务过度自信，可接受性判断则在所有尺寸下均低估；因此单一温度校准无法同时纠正两者。第二，可在5%错误预算下自动化的决策比例更接近经机会修正的技能（Spearman相关系数0.97，基于33个源与模型组合），而非原始准确率（0.91）。第三，超出训练语言范围的表现由基座模型决定：在210种语言中，改变训练运行仅使得分在每种规模内变动约2分，而基座模型未记录的语言则在所有规模下落后23至28分。在英语公开基准测试中，Zuse落后于Jev和Kev，包括使用相同基座的Kev模型。我们还报告了构建多源训练数据的两个陷阱：主题方案之间的标签词冲突，以及一种注入增强方法教会了捷径。Zuse 可在 https://bineric.com/zuse 尝试。

[2611.06133]
Accelerating Long-Context Decoding in Large-Scale Hybrid LLMs Without Retraining
在不重新训练的情况下加速大规模混合大语言模型的长上下文解码
The full-attention layers of large language models (LLMs) make every generated token more expensive than the previous one, slowing models down on long-context tasks. Hybrid models reduce this cost by replacing most full-attention layers with linear-attention layers, but the first still dominate decoding at long contexts. We accelerate these remaining layers without any training or weight changes using combined sparse attention: each layer attends to a sliding window of recent tokens, a pinned prefix, and a fixed budget of most relevant tokens in the context. The window size and budget are found on a per-layer basis using a multi-objective search algorithm. Across six hybrid models from three families, from 4B to 397B parameters, the selected configurations preserve their baseline model's accuracy on 21 long-context datasets. Decoding 64k/256k tokens becomes up to 1.26x/2.22x faster on Qwen3.5-4B, 1.20x/2.23x on Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, and 1.16x/2.06x on Ornith-1.0-397B.
中文翻译

Adapting Qwen3 to Tajik from 0.6B to 8B: Lower Text Loss Did Not Mean Better Understanding
从0.6B到8B适配Qwen3至塔吉克语：更低文本损失并未意味着更好理解
Small LoRA adapters trained on Tajik news and books lowered Qwen3's Tajik text loss at every size we tried, from 0.6B to 8B, by 0.60 to 1.45 nats per token, but none of them improved Tajik reading comprehension where it can be measured. After a further supervised fine-tuning stage, our main model, e3, is 11.4 points above Qwen3-8B on Belebele; its English MMLU changed by -1.2 points, and a loss larger than 3.3 points is excluded. The same heavy recipe applied to Qwen3-0.6B-Base brought its text loss close to that of the 8B model after books-only pretraining, while its multiple-choice answers stayed near chance and its English MMLU fell by 7.6 points. We report 15 trained models and their 5 starting models from June to October 2025, scored in 2026 on one harness with paired intervals, and we report what went wrong: lost training records, a thinking-mode dataset with almost no reasoning in it, later branches whose training data was never documented, and a 4-bit release that contains no transformer layers.
中文翻译
在塔吉克新闻和书籍上训练的小型LoRA适配器在每个尝试的规模（从0.6B到8B）上均降低了Qwen3的塔吉克语文本损失，每标记降低0.60至1.45纳特，但没有任何适配器提升了可测量的塔吉克语阅读理解能力。在Belebele测试中，1.7B和8B适配器使准确率保持不变，4B适配器下降3.7分；0.6B模型分数上升4.2分，但接近随机水平，并与模型偏好的答案字母变化一致，因此不能视为真正理解。在8B模型上，继续在约3.75亿条原始塔吉克语文本上进行预训练后，Belebele得分提升9.9分。经过进一步监督微调阶段，我们的主模型e3在Belebele上比Qwen3-8B高出11.4分；其英文MMLU下降1.2分，损失超过3.3分的模型被排除。相同高强度训练方案应用于Qwen3-0.6B-Base后，其文本损失在仅书籍预训练后接近8B模型水平，但多项选择题答案仍接近随机，英文MMLU下降7.6分。我们报告了2025年6月至10月训练的15个模型及其5个起始模型，于2026年在单一框架下以配对区间评分，并披露了失败原因：训练记录丢失、思维模式数据集中几乎无推理内容、后期分支训练数据未记录，以及一个包含无Transformer层的4比特发布版本。
👤 Tohir Saidzoda, Madina Abdusalomzoda, Nasimjon Rajabov, Akhrorkhon Ikromov, Komron Giyosiev et al. 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06025]
When Every Choice Is Correct: A Causal Study of Numerical Preference in Language Models
当每个选择都正确时：语言模型中数值偏好的因果研究
When a language model chooses a number between 1 and 30, every integer in the range is valid, yet the model can favor particular answers. We use numerical choice to study how preference is processed across layers and tokens. Perturbation probing and gradient attribution nominate neurons for activation interventions. In a selected Qwen3-4B case, a pre-answer edit changes 17 to 15 while both runs first emit 1. After the edit ends, selective cache and attention-output replacements trace part of its continuing influence on the second digit. Replacing one attention head's output or the other heads' outputs jointly produces similar immediate readout contributions; both interventions restore 17 but have different final log-odds effects on 7 versus 5. Holding later multilayer perceptron (MLP) outputs at their values from the edited run without either replacement reduces this effect gap from 0.119 to 0.0025 nat. Later computation therefore changes how similar local contributions affect the continuation. Retrospective estimates across eighteen contexts capture much of the probability movement but miss the selected answer switch. Prompt tests also separate influence from answer changes: one held-out prompt retains influence without switching, and only one of twelve fresh wordings qualifies for the reader/MLP test; there, joint MLP clamping again reduces the gap between the two reader effects while the answer remains 17. These results use numerical choice as a controlled case for studying persistent state and conditional downstream processing; they do not establish the origin or a complete circuit of natural numerical preference.
中文翻译
当语言模型在1到30之间选择一个数字时，范围内的每个整数都是有效的，但模型可能倾向于特定答案。我们利用数值选择来研究偏好在各层和词元间的处理机制。通过扰动探针和梯度归因，我们识别出需要干预的神经元。在一个选定的Qwen3-4B案例中，预答案编辑将17改为15，而两次运行最初均输出1。编辑结束后，选择性缓存和注意力输出替换追踪了其对第二位数字持续影响的部分路径。单独替换一个注意力头的输出或联合替换其他头的输出，产生相似的即时读出贡献；两种干预均能恢复17，但在7与5的最终对数几率上效果不同。若在编辑后的运行中保持后续多层感知机（MLP）输出不变，且不进行任何替换，则此效应差距从0.119 nat降至0.0025 nat。这表明后期计算改变了局部贡献如何影响后续生成的方式。在十八个语境下的回溯估计捕捉到了大部分概率变化，但未能解释所选答案的切换。提示测试也区分了影响与答案变化：一个保留提示维持影响但未切换，十二种新表述中仅一种符合读者/MLP测试条件；在此情况下，联合MLP钳制再次缩小了两种读者效应之间的差距，而答案仍为17。这些结果以数值选择为受控案例，研究持久状态与条件下游处理；但并未确立自然数值偏好的起源或完整电路。
👤 Hongnian Huang, Hongliang Liu 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06010]
Holistic Parallel Supervision for Expressive Speech to Speech Translation
表达性语音到语音翻译的全局并行监督
Expressiveness is central to speech-to-speech translation (S2ST), yet existing datasets rarely align source and target speech jointly in content, prosody, timing, and speaker identity. Consequently, current systems often rely on indirect conditioning or transfer mechanisms. To obtain this supervision, we introduce a training-data construction strategy that combines professional dubbing, which provides human-performed correspondence in content, prosody, and timing, with multilingual voice conversion to reduce speaker mismatch. We evaluate this supervision using existing expressive S2ST systems and DirectS2ST, an end-to-end method developed in this work that predicts the complete target-speech representation directly from source speech. Experiments on English-German and English-Spanish show that holistic parallel supervision improves expressive realization across all evaluated systems. DirectS2ST exhibits the largest gains in content, prosody, timing, speaker similarity, and human-rated expressiveness. To our knowledge, this work provides the first systematic investigation of holistic parallel supervision for expressive S2ST.
中文翻译

[2611.05771]
How Well Can Large Language Models Impersonate Individual Authors? A Comparative Evaluation
大语言模型在模仿个体作者方面的表现：一项比较评估
This study investigates the authorship impersonation capacity of six widely used large language models (LLMs): Gemini 3 Pro, Claude Opus, GPT 5.2, GPT 5.2 Pro, DeepSeek 3.2, and Grok 4.1 Fast. The evaluation was conducted using short product review texts selected to ensure high within author consistency in individual writing style. Two types of in context prompt were designed: a simple prompt with minimal instruction and a complex prompt incorporating explicit stylometric guidance for mimicry. Authorship mimicry was evaluated using a cosine distance based distance to training metric, supplemented by visual analyses of embedding distributions. The results indicate that authorship impersonation remained challenging for most LLMs, with Gemini 3 Pro showing the strongest apparent mimicry performance. However, subsequent overlap analysis revealed that Gemini 3 Pro extensively copied and reused sentences and phrases from the training texts, at levels far exceeding those observed among human authors. Follow up factor analyses showed that training set consistency was the most robust predictor of mimicry success across models, whereas author distinctiveness did not generalise as a reliable predictor. Global embedding visualisations further revealed a consistent macro level pattern: for most models, generated texts occupied regions of the embedding space that were largely separated from the corresponding human training clusters. This indicates limited convergence towards target author style and suggests the presence of model specific lexical and phraseological preferences. The findings highlight the importance of distinguishing genuine stylistic imitation from direct textual reuse and have implications for the forensic analysis of textual evidence involving potential LLM enabled impersonation.

本研究调查了六种广泛使用的大语言模型（LLMs）——Gemini 3 Pro、Claude Opus、GPT 5.2、GPT 5.2 Pro、DeepSeek 3.2 和 Grok 4.1 Fast——在作者风格模仿方面的能力。评估基于精选的短产品评论文本，确保每位作者写作风格具有高度一致性。设计了两种上下文提示：一种是包含最少指令的简单提示，另一种是包含明确风格指导的复杂提示。通过基于余弦距离的训练集度量和嵌入分布的可视化分析评估作者模仿效果。结果表明，大多数LLMs在作者模仿方面仍面临挑战，其中Gemini 3 Pro表现出最强的表面模仿能力。然而，后续重叠分析显示，Gemini 3 Pro大量复制和重复训练文本中的句子和短语，远超人类作者水平。后续因子分析表明，训练集一致性是跨模型模仿成功最稳健的预测因素，而作者独特性则无法可靠泛化。全局嵌入可视化进一步揭示了一致的宏观模式：对于大多数模型，生成文本占据的嵌入空间区域与对应的人类训练聚类基本分离，表明模型未能充分趋近目标作者风格，暗示存在模型特有的词汇和短语偏好。研究强调了区分真正的风格模仿与直接文本复用的重要性，对涉及潜在LLM驱动模仿的文本证据法医分析具有启示意义。

用于修复多语言语音识别错误的上下文图像符号
Speech transcription errors can be difficult to recognize when competing word hypotheses are unfamiliar or visually similar. We describe an interface that combines alternative transcripts, replay and contextually checked pictograms for selected uncertain concepts. The system will initially support French and Spanish functional utterances. A counterbalanced study will assess whether pictorial feedback helps listeners recover the speaker's intended action beyond text alternatives alone. We will also quantify semantic mismatch and additional interaction time, since an inappropriate image can reinforce the wrong interpretation rather than repair it.

[2611.05385]
Same Origin, Different Verdicts: Text Production Conditions Confound Machine-Generated Text Detection in Russian
同一来源，不同结论：文本生成条件混淆俄语机器生成文本检测
Automated detection of machine-generated text is applied where the verdict carries a cost for the author, yet origin is a property of the production process, not an observable property of the text. The conditions under which a text is produced push it in the same direction in which a generative model is presumed to push it. This study measures the contribution of these conditions while origin is held fixed. The Russian-language corpus of 1882 documents comprises a machine part, generated by four generation channels (each backed by its own model family) across 45 tasks in three task modes, and an archival human part annotated for source regimentation level before the texts were read. The primary contrast is computed within the machine corpus: both halves of a pair are generated by the same model for the same task and differ only in task wording. Machine-likeness is measured by four operationalisations: an interpretable index with weights fixed before data collection, a trained classifier, a zero-shot metric on an open model, a judge model. The main result is disagreement between procedures: changing the task mode shifted the scores of all four, but three gave a negative shift, the judge a positive one, and none of the four intervals covers zero. The comparison plan and the specifications of procedures 2-4 were fixed after the result of procedure 1, before procedures 2-4 ran; the disagreement survived correction of a corpus defect and of the nested validation. A baseline on the source identifier alone reaches a median AUROC of 1.0000 on the thirteen group splits where it is defined, and removing emphasis markers flipped a confident machine verdict to human without changing the full text by a single character. The scores are properties of a specific measurement system and corpus: they reflect data collection, repetition, formatting, and preprocessing decisions as well as linguistic style.
中文翻译
自动化机器生成文本的检测被应用于作者需承担代价的情境中，然而来源是生成过程的属性，而非文本本身可观察的属性。文本生成的条件会使其朝向生成模型预期的方向偏移。本研究在固定来源的前提下，测量这些条件的贡献。该研究使用包含1882份文档的俄语语料库，其中包含由四个生成通道（每个通道基于不同的模型家族）在45个任务的三种任务模式下生成的机器部分，以及在阅读前已标注来源归类层级的人工部分。主要对比是在机器语料内部进行：一对文本由同一模型针对同一任务生成，仅任务表述方式不同。机器相似性通过四种操作化方法衡量：一个权重预先固定的可解释指数、一个训练分类器、一个开放模型上的零样本度量，以及一个裁判模型。主要结果是各方法间存在分歧：改变任务模式导致所有四种方法得分发生变化，其中三种呈现负向变化，裁判模型则呈正向变化，且四种方法的区间均未覆盖零。比较方案及方法2-4的规范在方法1结果确定后才固定，在方法2-4执行前完成；即使修正了语料缺陷和嵌套验证问题，分歧依然存在。仅基于来源标识符的基线在十三组划分中达到中位数AUROC为1.0000，而移除强调标记后，原本自信的机器判断转变为人类判断，且全文未发生任何字符变化。这些得分是特定测量系统和语料的属性，反映了数据收集、重复、格式化和预处理决策以及语言风格的影响。
