[2611.05384]
Human-like cross-language generalisation in deep neural speaker embeddings and its acoustic foundations
深度神经网络说话人嵌入的人类式跨语言泛化及其声学基础
Generalisability is a central question for deep neural networks, including the cross-language generalisation of speaker embeddings. This study examined whether deep neural speaker embeddings generalise across languages and how such generalisation relates to human perception and acoustic structure. Using Cantonese--English bilingual speech presented in within- and cross-language conditions, we derived similarity from 18 state-of-the-art models and compared it with human ratings and acoustic features using linear mixed models and representational similarity analysis. Embeddings exhibited cross-language generalisation that broadly paralleled human behaviour, reflecting accurate identity discrimination and sensitivity to language switching. This generalisation relied on largely shared acoustic cues with language-sensitive weighting, mirroring human patterns. Together, these findings provide converging evidence for human--machine alignment in cross-language voice similarity processing.

[2611.05381]
Discourse Analysis of Legislative Bill Discussions
立法法案讨论的语篇分析
Legislative hearings are distinct communication events with their own human recognizable structure and logic, yet they have been analyzed as distinct phenomena beyond their transcripts. Understanding legislative hearings at a deeper level is a major step in analyzing the work and power relationships within democratic bodies. In this work, we present a novel end-to-end pipeline for parsing rhetorical structures from legislative committee hearings with the goal of improving legislative discourse analysis and democratic accountability. We also propose a rhetorical structure grammar and find that 55.21% of California state bill discussions in our dataset can be parsed with this grammar. We evaluate our pipeline with two utterance section classifiers on a random subset of over 2,000 annotated utterances corresponding to 100 hearings. We find that a logistic regression-based classifier with grammar-based output masking and utterance tag smoothing outperforms more complex models, achieving an F1 score of 52.8% against our ground truth discourse section labels.

[2611.06655]
Beyond accuracy: what AI assurance needs to establish in public services
超越准确率：AI保障在公共服务中需要建立什么
Public bodies increasingly rely on AI-produced evidence, including classifications, summaries and assessments. These outputs can inform policy decisions and service delivery, making their reliability a matter of public accountability. Establishing whether this evidence can be relied upon requires testing its outputs and examining how organisations decide whether those tests are sufficient. We combine technical evaluation with analysis of AI governance requirements, informed by measurement theory and management-based regulation. We compare eight GPT-4o configurations classifying 1,200 UK Prime Minister's Questions into 21 policy topics, against human-coded labels and across unchanged repeats. Accuracy ranges from 68.2% to 70.3%, yet 24.3% of questions receive different labels, with changes in topic shares and rankings. Configurations agree more with one another than with human coding: agreement does not guarantee correctness. Under the implemented score-extraction procedure, the proportion flagged for human review ranges from 0.9% to 41.0%. We analyse 37 UK and EU policy documents. Both sets expect testing and oversight, but reporting expectations do not consistently cover changing answers, shared errors, selective evaluation, substantive conclusions or review routing. Some UK guidance explicitly addresses stability. The UK sample emphasises public bodies checking their own use; the EU places substantial testing duties on providers while leaving them important choices about measures and acceptance thresholds. Without appropriate checks, evidence could misrepresent policy issues, redirect attention or resources, and leave errors undetected. Public policy should require organisations to justify reliance through checks beyond aggregate accuracy, clear responsibility, access to expertise and scrutiny of results.
中文翻译

[2611.06132]
Growing One's AI Tools: A Paradigm of Practice-Grown Tools
自我生长的AI工具：一种实践生成工具的新范式
Professionals have long used software built for them by others. This article reports the growth of a professional computer-assisted interpreting (CAI) tool built by its own user, a conference interpreter without programming training. Drawing on an eight-week version-controlled record with telemetry from 133 logged sessions and over 20,000 recognized utterances, the article traces how real conference work drove the tool through 62 commits and six versions. The record shows capabilities deployed on the day their need appeared and one strategy change that cut median end-to-end delay by over a third within one afternoon. The article then proposes the paradigm of practice-grown tools, defined by the shift from using to growing, the co-evolution of tool and practice through breakdown and repair, bounded local computation, and an assessment corollary, and it formulates three propositions toward tool-inclusive assessment of practitioners, from tool-making literacy to a compute-standardized format for certifying what they and their tools achieve together.

[2611.06100]
Reconciling Privacy with Provenance for Language Model Watermarks
语言模型水印中的隐私与溯源协调
Watermarking has great potential for regulating the use of language models (LMs), but its deployment has been marred by controversy, as many users argue that fine-grained tracking of LM usage violates their privacy. This tension reduces the effectiveness of watermarks, as users engage in evasion strategies such as paraphrasing that can remove much of the watermark signal. We show that provenance and privacy can be reconciled if we shift the goal of watermark design from tracking individuals to estimating aggregate usage. We develop locally differentially private watermarks which provably limit the evidence that any individual text can contribute to detection, while still enabling accurate estimation of population frequencies. We introduce the Red-Green Sequence (RGS) watermark, a private and computationally efficient watermark that achieves accurate frequency estimation at high privacy levels across various models, sample sizes and generation entropies in settings that reflect realistic deployment scenarios. On C4 language modeling tasks, we attain $0.0019$ MSE with $0.0038$ mean absolute bias at a low $\varepsilon$ of 1, while in more challenging settings such as an essay writing task with only 100 text samples, we still find errors on the order of $0.0113$ MSE with $0.004$ mean absolute bias at $\varepsilon=2$.
中文翻译

[2611.06048]
SVGFaith: A Two-Layer Meta-Evaluation of Faithfulness Metrics for Text-to-SVG Generation
SVGFaith：面向文本转SVG生成的双层元评估
Text-to-SVG systems are commonly evaluated by applying raster-image metrics to rasterised outputs, yet whether these scores capture fine-grained prompt faithfulness remains unclear. We introduce SVGFaith, a two-layer meta-evaluation framework in which the more faithful candidate of each pair is determined by construction or by human judgment. SVGFaith-Diagnostic contains 1,842 controlled contrastive pairs across eight capability axes, split evenly between two directions: two renderings compared against one prompt, and two descriptions compared against one rendering. SVGFaith-Arena contains 1,327 human-annotated comparisons of real generator outputs. On SVGFaith-Diagnostic, CLIP and three other image--text embedding metrics reach at least 0.789 pairwise accuracy on object identity but at most 0.672 on spatial relations and attribute binding, where chance is 0.5. We further introduce SVGFaith-Score, an interpretable pointwise metric. A domain-tuned decomposer turns each prompt into a dependency graph of atomic visual requirements, a visual question answering model scores whether each requirement is satisfied, and a requirement's score is discounted when its prerequisites score low. In addition to the overall score, the per-requirement scores show which requirements are judged unsatisfied and which inherit discounts from their prerequisites. SVGFaith-Score significantly outperforms all nine baselines, including a Qwen3.5-122B-A10B judge, on Arena and in both Diagnostic directions. Code and data: https://github.com/Yukinonooo/SVGFaith.
中文翻译
文本转SVG系统通常通过将输出栅格化后应用图像指标进行评估，但这些分数是否能捕捉提示的精细忠实度尚不明确。我们提出SVGFaith，一种双层元评估框架，其中每对候选结果的更忠实者由构造或人工判断确定。SVGFaith-Diagnostic包含1,842个受控对比对，覆盖八个能力维度，方向均衡分布：两个渲染结果对比一个提示，以及两个描述对比一个渲染结果。SVGFaith-Arena包含1,327个真实生成结果的人工标注比较。在SVGFaith-Diagnostic上，CLIP及其他三种图文嵌入指标在对象身份上的成对准确率至少达0.789，但在空间关系和属性绑定上最高仅为0.672（随机为0.5）。我们进一步提出SVGFaith-Score，一种可解释的逐点评估指标。通过领域调优的分解器将每个提示转化为原子视觉需求的依赖图，再由视觉问答模型评分每个需求是否满足，当前提条件得分低时，该需求得分将被折扣。除总分外，各需求得分还能揭示哪些需求未被满足及其前驱影响。SVGFaith-Score在Arena及诊断两个方向上显著优于全部九个基线，包括Qwen3.5-122B-A10B判官模型。代码与数据：https://github.com/Yukinonooo/SVGFaith。

[2611.06022]
Who Pays for the Knowledge Models Copy? Model Collapse as Rogers' Paradox
知识模型复制的成本是谁承担？模型坍塌作为罗杰斯悖论
Generative models trained on the output of earlier models lose knowledge across generations, rare knowledge first. The remedies that work all bring in information from outside the loop, and the theory of model collapse treats that supply as given: a fixed archive, drawn from a fixed distribution. We argue that the supply is the problem, and that cultural evolution has the theory of it. We read model collapse as Rogers' paradox: a population that comes to rely on copying ends up no better off than one that never copied, because copies go stale unless someone keeps learning for themselves. We test the identification in three steps. First, Rogers' model, fitted only to fully synthetic training, predicts to within a few percentage points how much rare knowledge language models, from word trigrams to a pretrained transformer, retain under other mixtures of fresh data. Second, when the world itself changes, a lineage that copies its own past needs more fresh data to keep up the faster the world changes, as Rogers' model says it must and as analyses that assume a fixed target cannot; a lineage that restarts every generation from fixed pretrained weights is largely insulated, because those weights are knowledge that is never copied. Third, we close the loop: when the people who write a model's training data can ask the model instead of finding things out, the supply of fresh data becomes endogenous. At equilibrium, the model is exactly as reliable as it is costly for people to find out for themselves, the population is no better off than without the model, and every increase in the share of model-written text must be paid for by more people learning for themselves. Users who check what they are told and relearn when it is wrong break the paradox. Filters that select plausible text are conformist and accelerate collapse; only checks that carry new information act as critical learning.
中文翻译
在早期模型输出上训练的生成模型会随代际传递逐渐丧失知识，稀有知识首当其冲。现有补救措施均需引入环外信息，而模型坍塌理论默认外部供给是固定的——来自固定分布的档案。我们主张，供给本身才是问题所在，而文化演化理论对此已有解释。我们将模型坍塌解读为罗杰斯悖论：一个依赖复制的群体最终不会比从未复制的群体更优越，因为除非有人持续自主学习，否则复制内容终将过时。我们通过三步验证该观点：第一，仅用完全合成训练数据拟合的罗杰斯模型，能以数个百分点的误差预测语言模型（从词三元组到预训练Transformer）在不同新鲜数据混合比例下的稀有知识保留程度；第二，当世界变化加快时，依赖自身历史复制的谱系需要更多新鲜数据才能跟上，这与罗杰斯模型预测一致，而假设固定目标的分析无法解释此现象；依赖每代重启于固定预训练权重的谱系则基本不受影响，因其权重代表永不复制的知识；第三，我们闭合循环：当撰写模型训练数据的人开始依赖模型而非自己探索时，新鲜数据供应变为内生。在均衡状态下，模型可靠性恰好等于人们自主发现信息的成本，群体整体并未因此获益，且模型生成文本占比每增加一点，都必须由更多人自主学习来“支付”代价。那些主动核查信息并在错误时重新学习的用户打破了这一悖论；仅筛选看似合理文本的过滤器属于顺从行为，反而加速坍塌；唯有携带新信息的核查才构成关键学习。
👤 Nam H. Le 📅 2026-11-04 🔗 arXiv
[2611.06001]
How Far Can You Steer? Behavior-Capability Frontiers in Contrastive Activation Addition
你能操控多远？对比激活添加中的行为-能力边界
Activation steering can modify language-model behavior without parameter updates, but behavioral efficacy alone does not determine whether an intervention is usable. Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) steers targeted behaviors using activation directions derived from contrasting examples, potentially degrading unrelated model capabilities in the process. We therefore study CAA through a behavior-capability frontier, selecting interventions that maximize behavioral gain subject to an explicit bound on capability degradation. We evaluate this framework across four instruction-tuned models, seven behaviors, and capability-loss budgets of 1%, 3%, and 5% (absolute percentage points). Validation-selected behavioral effects generalize strongly to held-out data ($ρ= 0.945$), while joint behavior-capability survival among nonzero operating points rises from 37.5% at the 1% budget to 72.7% at 3%, with limited additional improvement at 5%. Control experiments indicate that these effects depend on learned behavior directions rather than generic activation perturbations. Representational geometry is significantly but only modestly predictive of causal cross-behavior transfer. Finally, secondary capability, out-of-distribution, and open-ended evaluations show that interventions satisfying protected benchmarks can still degrade untested capabilities or transfer unevenly to free-form generation. These results characterize activation steering as a constrained intervention whose usable range depends on model, behavior, direction, layer, and evaluation budget, rather than a universal control knob.
中文翻译
激活操控可在不更新参数的情况下改变语言模型行为，但行为有效性并不决定干预的实际可用性。对比激活添加（CAA）利用对比示例提取的激活方向来引导特定行为，但可能在过程中损害无关模型能力。因此，我们通过行为-能力边界框架研究CAA，选择在显式能力退化预算约束下最大化行为增益的干预方案。我们在四个指令微调模型、七个行为类型以及1%、3%和5%的能力损失预算（绝对百分点）下进行评估。经验证选择的行为效应在保留数据上表现出强泛化能力（ρ=0.945），而在非零操作点上，行为与能力共存率从1%预算下的37.5%提升至3%预算下的72.7%，5%预算下改善有限。对照实验表明，这些效果依赖于学习到的行为方向，而非通用激活扰动。表示几何结构虽有一定预测力，但仅呈适度相关性。此外，二次能力、分布外及开放生成评估显示，满足保护基准的干预仍可能损害未测试能力，或在自由生成中产生不均衡迁移。这些结果表明，激活操控是一种受限干预，其可用范围取决于模型、行为、方向、层级和评估预算，而非万能控制旋钮。

Robots in the City: Persona-Based Perceptions of Urban Robots
城市中的机器人：基于人格的都市机器人感知
Pedestrians increasingly encounter robots in urban public spaces without interacting with them, but how robots change the perceived safety of a street remains unclear. We examine how robot presence and type affect perceived safety across urban contexts. We generate 18 images of Seoul streets that vary in time of day, road class, and robot condition (no robot, a delivery robot, or a humanoid). Two multimodal large language models (MLLMs), Qwen3.8-27B and Gemma4-26B, each conditioned on 500 synthetic personas, choose the safer image in each pair, and 25 human respondents complete the same task. Both models prefer streets without robots and favor the delivery robot over the humanoid. Robots are chosen more often at night and less often by older personas, whereas road class has smaller effects. In the model rationales, the delivery robot appears as a physical obstacle, whereas the humanoid appears as either a threat or a source of protection. Like humans, both models chose robots more often at night, but chose the humanoid over the delivery robot far less often, and Gemma showed a night effect for the humanoid about three times that of humans. These results show that the effect of a robot on perceived safety depends on when it appears, and that persona-conditioned MLLMs capture only part of human judgments.

Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Low-Resource Medical ASR: Shallow Fusion and Density Ratio Rescoring for Hausa
仅文本域自适应用于低资源医疗语音识别：浅融合与密度比重评分应用于豪萨语
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often struggle with specialised medical vocabulary, especially in low-resource languages where domain-specific speech data are scarce. We investigate text-only domain adaptation for Hausa medical ASR using the fixed MMS-1B-FL102 acoustic model and two decoding-time language-model fusion methods: Shallow Fusion (SF) and the Density Ratio Approach (DRA). A 4-gram KenLM trained on 19,421 Hausa health sentences serves as the medical target model, while a 75,372-sentence general-domain model provides the source prior for DRA. Evaluation uses 250 recordings of 25 physician-reviewed prompts from 10 speakers across six northern Nigerian states. Under prompt-disjoint five-fold cross-validation, SF and DRA reduce WER from 29.56% to 23.55% and 23.33%, respectively. Both significantly outperform greedy decoding (p < 0.001), while their difference is not significant (p = 0.423). However, general-LM-only SF achieves 23.96% WER, indicating that the medical LM's additional improvement over a general LM is modest. Medical Term Error Rate also improves under fusion, but does not establish a distinct medical-domain advantage. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, text-only language-model fusion can improve Hausa medical ASR without additional domain-specific speech collection.
中文翻译
自动语音识别（ASR）系统在处理专业医学词汇时常遇困难，尤其在低资源语言中，领域特定语音数据稀缺。本文研究使用固定MMS-1B-FL102声学模型，结合两种解码阶段的语言模型融合方法——浅融合（SF）和密度比方法（DRA），实现豪萨语医疗ASR的纯文本域自适应。以19,421条豪萨语健康语句训练的4元语法KenLM作为医疗目标模型，而75,372条句子的通用领域模型则为DRA提供源先验。评估基于来自尼日利亚北部六州10名说话人、经医生审阅的250段录音，涵盖25个提示。在提示不重叠的五折交叉验证下，SF和DRA分别将词错误率（WER）从29.56%降至23.55%和23.33%。两者均显著优于贪婪解码（p < 0.001），但彼此差异不显著（p = 0.423）。然而，仅使用通用语言模型的浅融合也达到23.96% WER，表明医疗语言模型带来的额外提升有限。融合后医学术语错误率也有所改善，但未体现出明显的领域优势。这些发现表明，轻量级、纯文本的语言模型融合可在无需额外领域语音采集的情况下提升豪萨语医疗ASR性能。

[2611.05419]
Unlocking Turing Tensor Cores: A Measured Throughput Account of From-Scratch Pre-training on a Single 22G RTX 2080 Ti
解锁图灵张量核心：基于单块22G RTX 2080 Ti从头预训练的吞吐量测量分析
The RTX 2080 Ti (TU102) ships full second-generation Tensor Cores in hardware, yet the vendor throttles FP16 matrix multiplication at the driver level, leaving usable tensor throughput well below the hardware peak. turing-unlock lifts this restriction with a kernel module that, before the nvidia driver binds the device, writes the FP16 speed selectors (SS0=0x88888888, SS1=0x8) and releases the ACR mutex; published measurements report cuBLAS FP16 matmul throughput rising from 55.7 to 91.4 TFLOPS (about 1.6x). On the same 22G 2080 Ti and under the same from-scratch pre-training protocol as the companion memory ledger (five tiers of Chinese encoder-decoder models, 2,451 steps per tier, 16,384 tokens per step, seed 42), we obtain a before/after throughput account: the small tier (75.7M parameters, 512-wide, 8+8 layers, activation checkpointing on) improves in steady-state throughput from 6,480 to 7,246 tok/s (+11.8%) with wall time cut from 1.77h to 1.61h (9.0% saved); the tiny tier goes from 15,150 to 15,866 tok/s (+4.7%) with wall time savings of 3.8%; the three smaller tiers (pico-micro) gain at most +0.3%; per-tier peak memory stays bit-identical, and final quality metrics are bit-identical or consistent within 10^-3. We conclude that the unlock gain is a function of matrix size, rising monotonically with tier -- imperceptible for pico-micro, visible from tiny upward, and cashed out as a double-digit percentage at small -- and that it is a pure throughput gain that leaves training dynamics untouched.
中文翻译
RTX 2080 Ti（TU102）硬件上配备了完整的第二代张量核心，但厂商在驱动层面限制了FP16矩阵乘法性能，导致可用张量吞吐量远低于硬件峰值。turing-unlock通过一个内核模块解除该限制，在NVIDIA驱动绑定设备前，写入FP16速度选择器（SS0=0x88888888, SS1=0x8）并释放ACR互斥锁；公开测量显示cuBLAS FP16矩阵乘法吞吐量从55.7提升至91.4 TFLOPS（约1.6倍）。在同一块22G的2080 Ti上，采用与配套内存账本相同的从头预训练协议（五层中文编码-解码模型，每层2,451步，每步16,384个token，种子42），我们获得前后吞吐量对比：小模型层（757万参数，512维宽，8+8层，启用激活检查点）稳态吞吐量从6,480提升至7,246 tok/s（+11.8%），实际耗时从1.77小时降至1.61小时（节省9.0%）；极小模型层从15,150提升至15,866 tok/s（+4.7%），耗时节省3.8%；三个更小的层级（pico-micro）提升不超过+0.3%；每层峰值内存保持比特级一致，最终质量指标比特一致或在10^-3范围内一致。我们得出结论：解锁增益与矩阵规模有关，随层级增大单调上升——pico-micro层级几乎不可察觉，tiny及以上层级可见，小型层级达到双位数百分比提升；且该增益仅为纯吞吐量提升，不影响训练动态。
